# AI SDLC Methodology Changelog

This file tracks behavior-changing rules in the AI SDLC pipeline. Each entry carries a **rule reference**, **defect class**, and **validation method**.

## Inclusion heuristic

> If a slice acceptable yesterday would be refused today (or vice versa), it's a changelog entry.

Typo and formatting edits don't qualify; behavior changes do. New skills, new gates, modified pre-finish checks, changed prompt structures for named subagents — these qualify. Doc clarifications that don't change behavior do not.

## How `/pulse` uses this file

This file is also installed at `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` so `/pulse` can surface the most recent dated entry as a Methodology section, regardless of which project the user is in.

When the file is missing (e.g., older install before this feature shipped), `/pulse` gracefully skips the Methodology section and optionally hints that re-running the install would surface methodology metadata.

## Format per entry

```markdown
## v<semver> — <YYYY-MM-DD>

<One-paragraph summary>

### Added | Changed | Retired

- **<RULE-ID> — <Rule name>**
  <Description of what this rule enforces and where>
  - **Rule reference**: <RULE-ID>
  - **Defect class**: <what bad behavior this prevents>
  - **Validation**: <how we verify the rule holds — pytest, structural grep, runtime gate, etc.>
```

Rules are identified by short IDs (e.g., `META-1`, `LINT-MOCK-1`, `WIRE-1`) for cross-reference from SKILL.md prose and from later changelog entries that supersede or refine.

---

## v0.75.0 — 2026-05-29

**BCSG-1 — BC-1 Strict Critical-Gate via acknowledgment** (slice-080; [[ADR-072]] mints a new rule; refines BC-1 in place; supersedes nothing; MEPD-1 posture: **INCLUDE**; 5-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.74.0 → 0.75.0).

BCSG-1 closes the SC-008 enforcement-correctness gap: `tools/build_checks_audit.py` printed "Per BC-1, Critical rules MUST be addressed" but `main()` ended in `return 1 if result.violations else 0`, where `result.violations` held only parse errors — so an applicable non-deferrable Critical rule produced exit 0, and a consumer treating the exit code as the BC-1 gate signal silently passed a slice violating a Critical rule. The complication BCSG-1 had to solve: BC-1 rules have no per-rule status field (applicability is computed and never disappears once a rule applies), so a naive "exit nonzero whenever `critical_applicable > 0`" would make every slice touching an `always:true` Critical rule un-passable.

**Mechanism** (per ADR-072 § Decision — option 2, the acknowledgment-flag design; options 1 blunt-count + 3 status-row-surface rejected):

- **Opt-in `--strict` flag**: under `--strict`, `audit_slice` appends one `BuildCheckViolation(kind="unacknowledged-critical", severity="Critical")` per applicable Critical rule whose `rule_id` is NOT in the `--ack-critical` set, so the UNCHANGED `return 1 if result.violations else 0` yields gate-failure exit 1. Default off → byte-identical legacy behavior (applicable Critical rules stay informational; every existing caller unaffected — AC3).
- **`--ack-critical <RULE-ID...>` sign-off list**: the builder addresses each applicable Critical rule, attests it in build-log.md, and passes its ID. Acknowledgment is an attestation, NOT machine proof the rule's check ran (executable per-rule auto-verification remains the deferred BC-1 v2).
- **Append after BOTH source loops** (project + global), guarded by `if strict:`, run once over the fully-assembled `result.applicable` — so a global-source Critical rule (e.g. BC-GLOBAL-2) is captured, not only project-source ones; unreachable on carry-over-exempt slices (early-returned).
- **Lenient ack** (fail-CLOSED): an `--ack-critical` ID matching no applicable Critical rule is ignored, but `_format_human` surfaces it as a diagnostic ("matched no applicable Critical rule (typo or stale ack?)") alongside the unacknowledged-firing rule IDs — converting the silent-no-op into a visible signal without changing exit semantics.
- **Standing consequence**: BC-PROJ-3 + BC-GLOBAL-2 are `always:true` Critical (git-revert-discipline) rules, so wiring `--strict` into `/build-slice` Step 6 means every slice must `--ack-critical BC-PROJ-3 BC-GLOBAL-2` (plus any slice-specific applicable Critical rules) after attesting in build-log.md. Step 6 prose carries the enumerate-then-ack pattern.

**Code surfaces**: `tools/build_checks_audit.py` — `audit_slice` + `_format_human` gain `strict`/`ack_critical` params; `main()` gains `--strict` + `--ack-critical` argparse flags; `BuildCheckViolation` gains the `unacknowledged-critical` kind; exit logic unchanged. `skills/build-slice/SKILL.md` Step 6 BC-1 block — enumerate-then-ack invocation + mechanical refusal semantics + rewritten v1/v2 caveat (OSDG-1 forward-synced to installed copy).

**5-part PMI-1 atomic bump** 0.74.0 → 0.75.0 covers all 5 canonical version-bearing legs per slice-063/064/066/067/069–078 canonical anchor: `VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + `pyproject.toml [project].version` (PVFS-1) + this `## v0.75.0` header + installed `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` (AVFS-1). Plus separate post-bump forward-sync obligations (NOT PMI-1 atomic legs): installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` (MCFS-1) + installed `~/.claude/skills/build-slice/SKILL.md` (OSDG-1) + venv `ai-sdlc-tools` 0.75.0 (TVFS-1 via `$PY -m pip install --upgrade .`). Shippability row #85 is a SEPARATE BC-PROJ-10 paired-entry-pin consumer-propagation surface.

**Critic stack** (slice-080; dual-Critic): first-Critic NEEDS-FIXES (3B/3M/2m = 8 findings, all VALID per meta-Critic, correct severity); meta-Critic EXTEND (0 suspicious, 1 missed Minor m-add-1 — repro docstrings documenting the rejected option-1 design, same class as M1 in a 2nd surface; 0 severity-wrong); TRI-1 user-ratified accept-all 2026-05-29.

### Added

- **BCSG-1 — BC-1 Strict Critical-Gate via acknowledgment**
  Adds opt-in `--strict` + `--ack-critical <RULE-ID...>` to `tools/build_checks_audit.py`: an applicable Critical rule absent from the ack set becomes an `unacknowledged-critical` violation → gate-failure exit 1 (default-off path byte-unchanged); `_format_human` surfaces unacknowledged-firing + unmatched-ack diagnostics; `/build-slice` Step 6 wires `--strict --ack-critical <ids>` with the enumerate-then-ack pattern.
  - **Rule reference**: BCSG-1 (slice-080; ADR-072 mints a new rule; refines BC-1 in place; supersedes nothing; methodology v0.75.0).
  - **Validation**: `tests/bugs/test_bc1_critical_rule_exit_gate.py` (catalogued failing repro — shippability row #85; FAIL→PASS), `tests/methodology/test_build_checks_audit.py` BCSG-1 cases (unacknowledged-project + global-source-captured + ack-clears + no-false-fire + default-path-unchanged + `_format_human` diagnostic), `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_75_0_bcsg_1_entry_present_in_repo` + `::test_v_0_75_0_bcsg_1_shippability_consumer_propagation`.

## v0.74.0 — 2026-05-29

**PCR-2a — Parallel-Conflict-Resolution v2a (vault-claim sub-mechanism)** (slice-078; [[ADR-071]] mints a new rule; refines PCR-1's class taxonomy in-place; supersedes nothing; second rule on the `PCR-N` family axis (parallel-conflict-resolution) sibling to PSQ-N; MEPD-1 posture: **INCLUDE**; 5-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.73.0 → 0.74.0).

PCR-2a closes the VAULT_CLAIM-class auto-resolution gap PCR-1 left open. Today's PCR-1 behavior at `classify_conflict` + `_regen_slice_queue`'s defense-in-depth gate is to RETURN STOP / RAISE `_SoftResolutionError(VAULT_CLAIM)` for the simplest concurrent-claim collision (two parallel sessions claim the same candidate at different times via `tools.slice_queue_claim --claim <name>`). PCR-2a adds the **strict-newer `Claimed-at` timestamp-winner** rule plus a **read-only loser-replacement suggestion** for the audit log (no claim-on-behalf write — deferred to PCR-2b per ADR-071 § Decision). Hard-conflict resolution + TRI-RESOLVE-1 user triage remain in **slice-079 (PCR-2b)** scope.

**Mechanism** (per ADR-071 § Decision + design.md § Resolution algorithm 7 steps):

- **Strict-newer rule**: winner = entry with the strictly-newer `Claimed-at` ISO-8601 timestamp (lexicographic comparison; PSQ-2 already writes RFC-3339 UTC at `tools/slice_queue_claim.py:629-633`). Equal timestamps fall-closed to STOP with `reason=claimed_at-tie deferred to PCR-2b` — NOT rare given `_now_iso8601_utc()` second-precision strftime (~1-second wall-clock tie window per /critique M1 honest framing).
- **Winner-identity-written-regardless-of-stage** (per /critique M4 ACCEPTED-FIXED): the strict-newer winner may reside in stage 2 (slice branch / ours) OR stage 3 (rebase-target). `_overlay_claims_on_queue_text` writes the winner's identity onto the baseline regardless of stage origin — without this, `_regen_slice_queue:614`'s `baseline_text = text_3 if text_3 else text_2` default would silently demote a stage-2 winner.
- **Defensive post-overlay verification** (per /critique-review M-add-1 ACCEPTED-FIXED): step 3 of the Resolution algorithm carries a `re.search(rf'### {candidate}.*?Claimed-by:\*\* {winner.claimed_by}', overlaid, re.DOTALL)` defensive check; on failure (overlay silently dropped because winner's candidate block lacks `- **Risk-retired:**` pivot per PCR-1's documented behavior at `_overlay_claims_on_queue_text:687`), return `ResolutionResult(action="STOP", conflict_class=VAULT_CLAIM, reason="overlay-silently-dropped — candidate block missing Risk-retired pivot")` rather than ship a silently-stale claim.
- **In-memory loser-replacement read** (per /critique-review M-add-2 ACCEPTED-FIXED): `_pick_loser_replacement(queue_text, exclude_names)` reads from the post-overlay in-memory `overlaid` string produced by step 3, NOT from `architecture/slice-queue.md` on disk — during VAULT_CLAIM rebase-in-progress the disk file contains git's `<<<<<<<` / `=======` / `>>>>>>>` conflict markers that would silently break parsing.
- **Two dispatch sites** (per /critique B3 ACCEPTED-FIXED): both (a) the CLI-facing `resolve_soft_conflict()` L242-253 (new VAULT_CLAIM branch ABOVE the existing `if cls is not ConflictClass.SOFT:` guard) AND (b) the defense-in-depth backstop `_regen_slice_queue()` L627-638 (raises new `_VaultClaimDispatch` sentinel; caught by `resolve_soft_conflict`'s exception loop and re-routed). PCR-1's UNKNOWN-class raise leg at L605-609 is UNTOUCHED.
- **Audit-log section** `## Vault-claim resolution - <ISO-8601 UTC>` (UNIFORM hyphen-space separator with PCR-1's `## Soft-conflict resolution -` per /critique M2 ACCEPTED-FIXED — section-type distinguished at the prefix word `Vault-claim` vs `Soft-conflict`, NOT at trailing dash decoration; avoids Windows smart-dash autocorrect fragility): 8 markdown-bold field-lines (Repo HEAD SHA pre-resolution / Candidate name / Winner Claimed-by / Winner Claimed-at / Loser Claimed-by / Loser Claimed-at / Loser auto-re-pick / Resolution actions). Lazy-create-on-first-of-any-section behavior preserved from PCR-1 (`_append_audit_log` single-open `O_APPEND` pattern reused verbatim).

**Code surfaces**: `tools/parallel_conflict_resolver.py` gains 1 new public function (`resolve_vault_claim_conflict`) + 1 new sentinel exception (`_VaultClaimDispatch`) + 4 new file-local helpers (`_collect_same_candidate_different_identity` — `_has_same_candidate_different_identity` becomes a thin `bool(...)` wrapper preserving the PCR-1 classify-conflict consumer API; `_select_timestamp_winner`; `_parse_queue_candidates_for_replacement`; `_pick_loser_replacement`; `_format_vault_claim_audit_entry`) + 2 modified call sites (`resolve_soft_conflict` L242 + L270 dispatch / exception-catch branches; `_regen_slice_queue` L632-638 raise-leg swap; `_append_audit_log` dispatch on `result.conflict_class`).

**SKILL.md L185-192 prose edits** (`skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md` Step 5b sub-step 2.5, per /critique M3 ACCEPTED-FIXED APED-1 structural-pin discipline): Pin #1 — L185 dispatch paragraph gains a sentence pinning `VAULT_CLAIM` in APPLIED-bound context (co-occurs with `auto-resolves`); Pin #2 — L192 closing summary DROPS `VAULT_CLAIM` from the multi-class `(class1)(...) + HARD (...) + MIXED (...) + UNKNOWN (...) ALL fall-closed` enumeration (the pre-fix shape pinned by `test_substep_2_5_l192_drops_vault_claim_from_fall_closed_enumeration`'s regex `VAULT_CLAIM\s*\([^)]+\)\s*\+\s*HARD\s*\(`).

**Cooperative-not-adversarial threat model** (per ADR-067 / ADR-069 § Threat model carried forward): identity-as-claim, no auth mediation, no claim-backdating defense. R-22 (newly discovered, status: open) — cross-machine clock-skew producing apparent-newer Claimed-at corner case; corrigibility hook = audit log records both Claimed-at timestamps; PCR-2b's Critic-stack adjudication is the documented remediation path.

**Forward references to slice-079 (PCR-2b)**: HARD-conflict resolution via spawned `/critique` + `/critique-review` Critic stack on proposed merge resolution; TRI-RESOLVE-1 user triage gate mirroring TRI-1 for resolution dispositions; auto-claim of loser-replacement on behalf of loser (closes the loser-out-of-sync window); MIXED partial-resolution. PCR-2a deliberately keeps blast-radius bounded by ALL of: not spawning Critic agents (mechanical timestamp-winner), not auto-claiming the replacement (read-only suggestion), not adding new RULE-ID for HARD class (PCR-2b owns that surface).

**5-part PMI-1 atomic bump** 0.73.0 → 0.74.0 covers all 5 canonical version-bearing legs per slice-063/064/066/067/069/070/071/072/073/074/075/076/077 canonical anchor: `VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + `pyproject.toml [project].version` (PVFS-1) + this `## v0.74.0` header + installed `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` (AVFS-1). Plus separate post-bump forward-sync obligations (NOT PMI-1 atomic legs): installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` (MCFS-1) + installed `~/.claude/skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md` (OSDG-1) + venv `ai-sdlc-tools` 0.74.0 (TVFS-1 via `$PY -m pip install --upgrade .`). Shippability row #78 is a SEPARATE BC-PROJ-10 paired-entry-pin consumer-propagation surface (pins `tests/methodology/test_pcr_2a_repro_vault_claim_gate_closed.py::test_vault_claim_gate_closed_returns_resolution_result`).

**Critic stack** (slice-078; 3-Critic stack N=14 cumulative including code-Critic to-fire post-build): first-Critic NEEDS-FIXES (3B/4M/9m = 16 findings — all ACCEPTED-FIXED save m3 OVERRIDDEN, m4 DEFERRED, m9 ACCEPTED-PENDING); meta-Critic EXTEND (M-add-1 Minor + M-add-2 Major — both ACCEPTED-FIXED in same fix block per TPHD-1 sub-mode (b)); TRI-1 user-ratified accept-all 2026-05-29. Self-validating-slice property N=2 (extends slice-077) — slice-078's own /commit-slice --merge may exercise VAULT_CLAIM auto-resolution if a peer claims concurrently; cooperative-acceptable corner case.

### Added

- **PCR-2a — Parallel-Conflict-Resolution v2a (vault-claim sub-mechanism)**
  Adds `resolve_vault_claim_conflict` public API + 4 file-local helpers + audit-row section-type to `tools/parallel_conflict_resolver.py`; wires dispatch into BOTH `resolve_soft_conflict` L242-253 (CLI-facing) AND `_regen_slice_queue` L627-638 (defense-in-depth via `_VaultClaimDispatch` sentinel); edits `skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md` L185-192 dispatch prose to move VAULT_CLAIM from STOP enumeration into APPLIED context (APED-1-pinned).
  - **Rule reference**: PCR-2a (slice-078; ADR-071 mints a new rule on the existing PCR-N family axis; refines PCR-1's class taxonomy in-place; supersedes nothing; methodology v0.74.0).
  - **Validation**: `tests/methodology/test_pcr_2a_vault_claim_resolver.py` (20 tests — predicate helpers + integration), `test_pcr_2a_regen_slice_queue_dispatch.py` (3 tests — both dispatch sites + UNKNOWN-still-fail-closed), `test_commit_slice_skill_vault_claim_dispatch.py` (3 tests — APED-1 Pin #1 + Pin #2 + OSDG-1 forward-sync), `test_parallel_conflict_resolution_log_vault_claim.py` (2 tests — row format + mixed-section append-only), `test_pcr_2a_repro_vault_claim_gate_closed.py` (1 catalogued regression — shippability row #78).

## v0.73.0 — 2026-05-28

**PCR-1 — Parallel-Conflict-Resolution v1 (diagnostic + soft-conflict auto-regen)** (slice-076; [[ADR-069]] mints a new rule; supersedes nothing; first rule on a NEW family axis `PCR-N` (parallel-conflict-resolution), sibling to PSQ-N (parallel-slice-queue) but distinct layer — PSQ-3 = detect conflict at `/commit-slice --merge` Step 5b sub-step 2.5 rebase; PCR-1 = resolve conflict for SOFT class BEFORE PSQ-3's existing SOAD-1 STOP fires; MEPD-1 posture: **INCLUDE**; 5-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.72.0 → 0.73.0; BC-PROJ-9 5-inventory bump 30 → 31 for new tool module).

PCR-1 mints the parallel-conflict-resolution family axis to close a high-frequency low-judgment gap surfaced during a 2026-05-28 5-session parallel-slice workflow conversation: every parallel slice regenerates `architecture/slice-queue.md` + `architecture/slices/_index.md` + appends rows to `architecture/shippability.md` + appends entries to `methodology-changelog.md`. When N sessions concurrently produce slices and `/commit-slice --merge` sequentially, every merge after the first hits rebase conflicts on these auto-regenerated state files even when code blast-radii are perfectly disjoint. PSQ-3's existing behavior STOPs on every such rebase conflict with a SOAD-1 3-option ask — for a 5-session pipeline that's 4+ manual STOPs per merge sequence, defeating the parallel-slice value proposition. PCR-1's graduated 5-class taxonomy (SOFT / VAULT_CLAIM / HARD / MIXED / UNKNOWN) auto-resolves the high-frequency low-judgment SOFT conflicts while preserving manual + Critic review for low-frequency high-judgment HARD conflicts.

**5-class taxonomy** (per ADR-069 § Decision):

- **SOFT** — all U-files in the canonical 2-member SOFT file-set `{architecture/slice-queue.md, architecture/shippability.md}` AND no same-candidate-different-identity claim collision. Auto-regen via dispatch + `git rebase --continue`. NO Critic involvement (deterministic regen from current truth; no judgment to review). Shipped in **slice-076 (this slice)**.
- **VAULT_CLAIM** — sole U-file is `architecture/slice-queue.md` AND same-candidate appears in BOTH branches' parsed claim dicts with DIFFERENT `Claimed-by` values (per /critique B4 ACCEPTED-FIXED). Timestamp-winner resolution + light Critic check. Deferred to **slice-077 (PCR-2)**; in slice-076 fails-closed to existing PSQ-3 SOAD-1 STOP.
- **HARD** — any U-file is a source-code / ADR / SKILL.md / `_index.md` / `methodology-changelog.md` / etc. (any file not in the 2-member SOFT file-set and not pure-claim VAULT_CLAIM diff). Full Critic stack: `/critique` + `/critique-review` + TRI-RESOLVE-1 user triage. Deferred to **slice-077 (PCR-2)**.
- **MIXED** — at least one SOFT U-file AND at least one non-SOFT U-file (HARD or VAULT_CLAIM). Both SOFT+HARD and SOFT+VAULT_CLAIM combos classify as MIXED (per /critique M4 ACCEPTED-FIXED disambiguation). Treated as HARD for resolution purposes (atomicity — never partially auto-resolve the SOFT portion). Deferred to **slice-077 (PCR-2)**.
- **UNKNOWN** — `classify_conflict` cannot determine class (rebase state inconsistent / no U-entries despite rebase-in-progress / file content unreadable / git state unexpected / stage-missing on both sides simultaneously). **Fail-closed STOP** — never auto-resolve (APED-1 silent-disable / default-off-on-malformed criterion: never silent-default to SOFT). In **slice-076 fail-closed behavior** (pinned by `test_classify_conflict_returns_unknown_when_rebase_state_empty` + `test_resolve_soft_conflict_returns_stop_on_unknown_class`).

**SOFT-class file-set** (`_SOFT_FILE_SET` frozenset, forward-slash-keyed per /critique M1 — `git status --porcelain` emits forward-slash on all OSes per git docs):
- `architecture/slice-queue.md` — soft-regenerated via textual claim-overlay on rebase-target stage `:3:` per /critique-review M-add-1 ACCEPTED-FIXED option (2). NO `write_slice_queue` round-trip (avoids the B1 phantom-parser trap — `parse_queue_text` returns only claim metadata, not PSQ-1 5-field enumeration, so a round-trip would lose candidate metadata).
- `architecture/shippability.md` — soft-regenerated via row-union merge by leading `| <NN> |` slice number; same-slice-number with different content escalates to HARD per design.md Edge-cases column (defense-in-depth: each slice owns one row; collision implies a slice number collision elsewhere).

`architecture/slices/_index.md` is **NOT** in SOFT — `/archive`'s regen is Haiku-LLM-dispatched per COST-1, not deterministic; PCR-1 cannot reproduce Haiku's lessons-block synthesis. `methodology-changelog.md` is **NOT** in SOFT — PMI-1 5-leg atomic-bump risk on concurrent bumps means subtly inconsistent merged entries (different RULE-IDs, paired-pin test names, ADR refs).

**Insertion site**: `skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md` Step 5b sub-step 2.5 (the PSQ-3 conflict-outcome path). PCR-1's dispatch is strictly additive — inserts a new paragraph BEFORE the existing SOAD-1 3-option STOP block; the existing 3-option SOAD-1 ask remains verbatim; the existing `git rebase --abort` recovery hint remains verbatim. Order: rebase fires → conflict surfaces → PCR-1 resolver invoked → if SOFT, auto-resolve + `git rebase --continue` + log + skip SOAD-1 + proceed to sub-step 3; else (VAULT_CLAIM / HARD / MIXED / UNKNOWN) → enhanced full-detail diagnostic prints concerned-slice metadata + falls through to existing SOAD-1 STOP block.

**New helper module** `tools/parallel_conflict_resolver.py` (~600 LOC across 11 functions): library API (`diagnose_conflict` / `classify_conflict` / `resolve_soft_conflict`) + frozen dataclasses (`ConflictDiagnostic` / `ConcernedSlice` / `ClaimEntry` / `ResolutionResult`) + `ConflictClass` enum + 7 private helpers (`_extract_u_files`, `_derive_concerned_slices`, `_extract_claim_diff`, `_regen_slice_queue`, `_overlay_claims_on_queue_text`, `_merge_shippability`, `_append_audit_log`) + CLI (`--diagnose | --classify | --resolve-soft` mutually-exclusive + `--json` + `--repo-root`).

**New audit log** `architecture/parallel-conflict-resolution-log.md` (lazy-created on first PCR-1 soft-conflict auto-resolution; append-only; mirrors `architecture/critic-calibration-log.md` pattern). Each entry: ISO-8601 UTC timestamp + repo HEAD SHA pre-resolution + U-files list + concerned-slices map + per-file resolution actions. Best-effort per design.md error model (write failures log to stderr but do NOT block resolution; race-acceptance per ADR-067 cooperative-not-adversarial threat model).

**Forward references to slice-077 (PCR-2)**: VAULT_CLAIM timestamp-winner + light Critic resolution; HARD-conflict full Critic stack + TRI-RESOLVE-1 user triage; MIXED partial-resolution (may revisit if partial-resolution becomes desirable). All four classes fail-closed in PCR-1 v1 — the resolver returns `action: STOP` and the SKILL.md prose falls through to PSQ-3's existing SOAD-1 ask.

**5-part PMI-1 atomic bump** 0.72.0 → 0.73.0 covers all 5 canonical version-bearing legs per slice-063/064/066/067/069/070/071/072/073 canonical anchor: `VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + `pyproject.toml [project].version` (PVFS-1) + this `## v0.73.0` header + installed `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` (AVFS-1). Plus separate post-bump forward-sync obligations (NOT PMI-1 atomic legs; per slice-063 M-add-1 leg-enumeration discipline): installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` (MCFS-1 forward-sync) + installed `~/.claude/skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md` (OSDG-1 / CAD-1 forward-sync of the Step 5b sub-step 2.5 PCR-1 dispatch insertion) + venv `ai-sdlc-tools` 0.73.0 (TVFS-1 via `$PY -m pip install --upgrade .`). **Shippability row #76 is a SEPARATE BC-PROJ-10 paired-entry-pin consumer-propagation surface, NOT a PMI-1 part** (per slice-067 M-add-4 ADR-064 L50 leg-enumeration discipline).

**BC-PROJ-9 5-inventory** for the new `tools/parallel_conflict_resolver.py` module: `plugin.yaml` tools block + `tools/install_audit.py::_CANONICAL_TOOLS` + `INSTALL.md` tool-count literal at L22 AND L166 (both `30 → 31` — per /critique M6 ACCEPTED-FIXED two-site pin) + `tests/methodology/test_utf8_stdout_regression.py::_ROOT_ONLY_TOOLS` (PCR-1 helper uses `--repo-root` with no positional slice arg → root-only bucket per slice-067 / `slice_queue_writer` precedent) + `architecture/shippability.md` row for slice-076. Pinned by `test_parallel_conflict_resolver_in_canonical_tools_plugin_manifest_install_md_at_l22_and_l166` (`tests/methodology/test_parallel_conflict_resolver_tool_inventory.py`).

**Adversarial model**: cooperative-not-adversarial, mirroring PSQ-2 / PSQ-3's threat model (per ADR-067 § "Identity model implications"). PCR-1's purpose is making sequential `--merge`s from cooperating Claude sessions reliable; a malicious actor with local write access to the worktree can always bypass any methodology rule by editing files directly. PCR-1 is NOT a security boundary; it's a coordination convention.

**R-21 (newly discovered, status: open)**: SOFT auto-regen produces semantically-different content from manual-resolve baseline at a corner case (per /critique-review m5 ACCEPTED-PENDING + ADR-069 § Reversibility anticipated-failure-mode discipline). Mitigation: (a) explicit fail-closed on UNKNOWN class; (b) APED-1 empirical-execution battery on 4 minted predicates; (c) `architecture/parallel-conflict-resolution-log.md` audit trail. Candidate fix classes (deferred to slice-077+ on empirical refutation): tighten `classify_conflict` SOFT predicate; extend SOFT-set audit with deterministic equivalence-class verification; fail-closed broader (lower SOFT confidence threshold globally).

**Aggregated lessons**: slice-076 first-Critic surfaced 16 findings (4B/7M/5m); meta-Critic at /critique-review surfaced 5 missed findings (1 severity-adjustment + 5 M-add) — all VALID and ACCEPTED-FIXED at TRI-1; 19 dispositioned findings. Per CRSI-1 v1 walking-skeleton advisory-only + voluntary-restraint precedent, code-Critic findings post-/build-slice will defer to next bundle (slice-077+; voluntary-restraint N=17 cumulative if deferred). Phase-A R-21 latent bug (`### R-21` h3 heading instead of `## R-21` h2 — RR-1 audit's H2-only regex misparsed R-21's `Status: open` as belonging to R-20) was surfaced + fixed in-band at Phase B; class: heading-level convention slip per RR-1 schema. Pre-existing test-fixture format mismatch (`**Claimed-by**:` vs real queue's `- **Claimed-by:**`) corrected at Phase C in-band; class: test-fixture-vs-real-format drift.

### Added

- **PCR-1 — Parallel-Conflict-Resolution v1 (diagnostic + soft-conflict auto-regen)**
  Inserts a NEW PCR-1 dispatch paragraph in `skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md` Step 5b sub-step 2.5 (the PSQ-3 conflict-outcome path) that invokes `python -m tools.parallel_conflict_resolver --resolve-soft --json` BEFORE the existing SOAD-1 STOP block; for SOFT-class conflicts (all U-files in the 2-member SOFT file-set + no same-candidate-different-identity claim collision), auto-resolves via deterministic regen (`_overlay_claims_on_queue_text` for slice-queue.md; row-union for shippability.md) + `git add` + `git rebase --continue`; for non-SOFT (VAULT_CLAIM / HARD / MIXED / UNKNOWN), prints full-detail diagnostic (concerned slices + blast-radius + claim history + commit times + mission-brief links per U-file) and falls through to existing SOAD-1 3-option ask.
  - **Rule reference**: PCR-1 (slice-076; ADR-069 mints a new rule; first rule on a NEW family axis `PCR-N` parallel-conflict-resolution sibling to PSQ-N; supersedes nothing; methodology v0.73.0).
  - **Defect class**: high-frequency low-judgment rebase conflicts on auto-regenerated state files (slice-queue.md / shippability.md) during parallel-slice `/commit-slice --merge` sequences forced manual SOAD-1 STOPs per conflict, defeating the parallel-slice family axis's value proposition (4+ manual interrupts per 5-session merge sequence).

## v0.72.0 — 2026-05-28

**PSQ-3 — Parallel-Slice Queue Rebase-and-Conflict Discipline** (slice-073; [[ADR-068]] mints a new rule; supersedes nothing; sibling on the parallel-slice family axis to PSQ-1 / [[ADR-064]] + PSQ-2 / [[ADR-067]] + BRANCH-2 / [[ADR-063]]; first rule on the *rebase-discipline* axis adjacent to (not extending) the queue-output / claim-machinery / worktree-isolation axes; MEPD-1 posture: **INCLUDE**; 5-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.71.0 → 0.72.0; mints PSQ-3).

PSQ-3 inserts a NEW sub-step 2.5 in `skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md` Step 5b (`--merge` sub-mode) between the existing sub-step 2 (commit on slice branch) and sub-step 3 (default-branch resolution + checkout + no-ff merge). The new sub-step resolves the default branch via the canonical 2-step pattern (primary `git symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` + fallback `git config init.defaultBranch`; STOP exit 2 if neither resolves — NAW-1 ADR-061 §Decision exit-2 contract verbiage), then runs `git rebase <default>` on the slice branch. Three outcome paths: **Fast-forward no-op** (default has not advanced since slice's branch-point; `git rebase` exits 0 without replay; proceed to sub-step 3); **Clean replay** (default has advanced + slice replays cleanly atop the new default tip — common second-to-merge case; proceed to sub-step 3); **Conflict-STOP** (print `git status --porcelain` U-prefixed entries + `git rebase --abort` recovery hint + SOAD-1 3-option ask: (a) abort rebase + investigate / (b) resolve manually + `git rebase --continue` outside skill / (c) cancel slice merge entirely). PSQ-3 NEVER auto-resolves conflicts (no `-X theirs`, no `-X ours`, no merge driver) — preserves the existing /commit-slice "never auto-resolve, never bypass hooks, never force" Critical rules block verbatim.

**Scope is `--merge` only**. `--push` (sub-mode a) is explicitly out of scope — PR-based workflows delegate rebase to GitHub's PR merge-queue / `gh pr merge --rebase` / reviewer-side rebase; local pre-push rebase is opinionated, deferred to a future PSQ-4 if user demand emerges. `--sync-after-pr` (sub-mode d) is explicitly out of scope — post-PR-merge cleanup where rebase is moot (PR already merged on GitHub; local default fast-forwards via `git pull --ff-only`; slice branch about to be safe-deleted).

**NO new tool module**. Per ADR-068 §Options-#3 the `git rebase` invocation IS the runtime gate — no-op fast-forward when at-tip; clean exit 0 when behind without conflict; exit non-zero with U-prefixed conflicting files identifiable via `git status --porcelain` when behind with conflict. A separate audit-tool would duplicate `git rebase`'s own behavior with no additional safety. Structural-pin tests on SKILL.md prose catch drift — analogous to how `--merge`'s `git merge --no-ff` invocation is pinned via prose-pin tests at `tests/methodology/test_commit_slice_skill_merge_flag.py::test_commit_slice_skill_md_specifies_no_ff_merge_and_safe_local_branch_delete` rather than a separate merge-audit module. The cross-family precedent in the parallel-slice rule family is mixed: PSQ-1 + PSQ-2 minted new tool modules because they had non-trivial library APIs to ship (queue write + claim CLI); PSQ-3 has neither — its entire surface is one extra `git rebase` invocation in SKILL.md prose.

**5-part PMI-1 atomic bump** 0.71.0 → 0.72.0 covers all 5 canonical version-bearing legs per slice-063/064/066/067/069/070/071/072 canonical anchor: `VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + `pyproject.toml [project].version` (PVFS-1) + this `## v0.72.0` header + installed `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` (AVFS-1). Plus separate post-bump forward-sync obligations (NOT PMI-1 atomic legs; per slice-063 M-add-1 leg-enumeration discipline): installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` (MCFS-1 forward-sync) + installed `~/.claude/skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md` (OSDG-1 forward-sync of the Step 5b sub-step 2.5 insertion) + venv `ai-sdlc-tools` 0.72.0 (TVFS-1 via `$PY -m pip install --upgrade .`). **Shippability row #73 is a SEPARATE BC-PROJ-10 paired-entry-pin consumer-propagation surface, NOT a PMI-1 part** (per slice-067 M-add-4 ADR-064 L50 leg-enumeration discipline).

**Adversarial model**: cooperative-not-adversarial, mirroring PSQ-2's threat model (per ADR-067 §"Identity model implications"). PSQ-3's purpose is making sequential `--merge`s from cooperating Claude sessions reliable; a malicious actor with local write access to the worktree can always bypass any methodology rule by editing files directly. PSQ-3 is NOT a security boundary; it's a coordination convention.

**SOAD-1 first-instance in /commit-slice**: PSQ-3 introduces the first SOAD-1 structured-options ask in `skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md`. The other 6 confirmation sites (verified via grep — L173/L175/L202/L203/L205/L255) use raw `(yes/no)` prompts and remain unchanged this slice (binary confirmations don't need SOAD-1; retrofitting them is scope-creep tracked as a future `/critic-calibrate` candidate).

**Aggregated lessons**: slice-073 first-Critic surfaced 16 findings (6B/6M/4m); meta-Critic at /critique-review surfaced 6 missed findings (2 Major + 4 minor) — all VALID — extending the **TPHD-1 sub-mode (a) "Builder fix-block introduces N+1 regressions" pattern to N=7 cumulative** (slice-062/064/067/070/071/072/073). The canonical instance was M-add-1: Builder applied M4's slice-064-precedent retirement at design.md L156 but missed two ADR-068 sites carrying the same defect class. `/critic-calibrate` proposal target (post-slice-073): add an explicit "post-fix-block grep -n on the precedent name OR audit anchor across all 3 surfaces (mission-brief + design + ADR)" step to the Builder fix-block discipline.

### Added

- **PSQ-3 — Parallel-Slice Queue Rebase-and-Conflict Discipline**
  Inserts a NEW sub-step 2.5 in `skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md` Step 5b that rebases the slice branch onto the resolved default before the existing no-ff merge; on conflict STOPs with `git status --porcelain` U-prefixed entries + `git rebase --abort` recovery hint + SOAD-1 3-option ask; no auto-resolve. Scope is `--merge` only — `--push` and `--sync-after-pr` are out of scope.
  - **Rule reference**: PSQ-3 (slice-073; ADR-068 mints a new rule; sibling on parallel-slice family axis; supersedes nothing; methodology v0.72.0; first rule on the *rebase-discipline* axis).
  - **Defect class**: two parallel slice branches sequentially merged into the default branch via `/commit-slice --merge` produce silent merge conflicts at sub-step 3 (the no-ff merge) without rebase-onto-default discipline — the second-to-merge slice discovers conflicts AT merge-time (all-at-once aggregate diff) rather than rebase-time (per-commit granular diff).
  - **Validation**: `tests/methodology/test_commit_slice_skill_rebase_flag.py` (5 structural-pin tests pinning the rebase invocation literal + ordering + canonical-2-step default-branch resolution + conflict-STOP language + `git rebase --abort` recovery hint in Step 5b) + `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_72_0_psq_3_entry_present_in_repo` + `test_v_0_72_0_psq_3_shippability_consumer_propagation` (BC-PROJ-10 paired-pin discipline + RPCD-1 / SCPD-1 consumer-reference propagation) + `test_version_files_synchronized_at_v_0_72_0` (PMI-1 5-part atomic bump leg verification); shippability row #73.

## v0.71.0 — 2026-05-27

**PSQ-2 — Parallel-Slice Queue Claim Machinery** (slice-072; [[ADR-067]] mints a new rule; supersedes nothing; sibling on the parallel-slice family axis to PSQ-1 / ADR-064; first rule on the claim-machinery axis adjacent to (not extending) the queue-output axis [[ADR-064]] + worktree-isolation axis [[ADR-063]]; MEPD-1 posture: **INCLUDE**; 5-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.70.0 → 0.71.0; mints PSQ-2). Retires risk-register R-19 (`mitigating` → `retired` — claim machinery makes `architecture/slice-queue.md` freshness a hint not a load-bearing collision-safety signal).

PSQ-2 ships additive `**Claimed-by:** <user.name> <user.email>` + `**Claimed-at:** <ISO-8601 UTC>` optional field lines per entry on PSQ-1's stable on-disk queue file contract (per ADR-064 §Consequences "Slice-068 MAY add new field lines per entry"), plus a new `tools/slice_queue_claim` CLI providing `--claim` / `--release` / `--force-claim` / `--queue` flags. The ownership identity is read via `git config user.name` + `git config user.email` (3-case exit-code-based absence detection: returncode==1 unset / returncode==0+stdout.strip()=="" configured-empty / other non-zero git-tool-error — all map to loud `exit 2`; no anonymous fallback). `tools.slice_queue_writer.write_slice_queue` (PSQ-1) is modified to read existing claims at regen time and merge them onto candidates whose names survive into the new top-10 (silently discards claims on dropped candidates per AC4). `_format_entry` inserts the 2 claim lines AFTER `Risk-retired:` and BEFORE the trailing empty-line entry-separator (index [-2] per Critic m1 ACCEPTED-FIXED); a `_extra_field_lines` pass-through preserves unknown PSQ-3+ forward-compat field lines verbatim on roundtrip (per Critic M3 + meta-Critic m-add-2 ACCEPTED-FIXED). Writes use explicit `Path.write_text(..., newline="")` for LF-only byte-deterministic emission on Windows (per Critic M1 — modelcontextprotocol/python-sdk#2433 — default `newline=None` translates `\n`→`\r\n` in text mode, breaking byte-equal claim-preservation round-trip assertions).

**5-part PMI-1 atomic bump** 0.70.0 → 0.71.0 covers all 5 canonical version-bearing legs per slice-063/064/066/067/069/070/071 canonical anchor: `VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + `pyproject.toml [project].version` (PVFS-1) + this `## v0.71.0` header + installed `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` (AVFS-1). Plus separate post-bump forward-sync obligations (NOT PMI-1 atomic legs; per slice-063 M-add-1 leg-enumeration discipline): installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` (MCFS-1 forward-sync) + installed `~/.claude/skills/slice/SKILL.md` (OSDG-1 forward-sync of the Step 6.5 claim-preservation note) + venv `ai-sdlc-tools` 0.71.0 (TVFS-1 via `$PY -m pip install --upgrade .`). **Shippability row #72 is a SEPARATE BC-PROJ-10 paired-entry-pin consumer-propagation surface, NOT a PMI-1 part** (per slice-067 M-add-4 ADR-064 L50 leg-enumeration discipline).

**Identity model — git-identity-only**: ownership = `git config user.name` + `git config user.email` at claim time. NO session-id, NO PID, NO hostname overlay. ADR-064 L37 + R-19 mid-2026-05 mitigation prose both anticipated `session-id detection` for PSQ-2; ADR-067 §"Options considered" Option 2 explicitly rules this out — git identity is the only ambient ownership signal universal in this codebase. ADR-067 §"Lineage divergence note" documents this as predecessor-spec drift (NOT supersession — ADR-064 is append-only). The trade-off: two Claude sessions running as the same git user cannot distinguish themselves on a single candidate (they are by definition the same claimant). Per ADR-067 §"Adversarial model": cooperative coordination, NOT a security boundary.

**AC count = 6** documented deviation from /slice's ≤5-AC rule (slice-067 N=1 → slice-072 **N=2** promotion of the pattern signal; per Critic M4 ACCEPTED-FIXED). AC5 R-19 retirement + AC6 v-section meta (methodology entry + paired-pin tests + 5-part PMI-1 + shippability row) cannot fold cleanly into AC1-AC5 on new-mechanism mints. Slice-073 reflection nominates `/critic-calibrate` action to formalize "≤5 (or ≤6 when AC6 is exclusively a v-section entry-pin meta-AC)" carve-out.

Dual-Critic + Builder self-catch density: first Critic NEEDS-FIXES with **2 Blockers / 4 Majors / 5 minors** (B1 `_ROOT_ONLY_TOOLS` cp1252-bucket mismatch — slice_queue_claim has no `--root` flag; B2 R-19 + ADR-064 L37 stale session-id forward-references; M1 Windows CRLF round-trip via TextIOWrapper newline=None default; M2 `--release` exit-code ambiguity in-queue-unclaimed vs not-in-queue; M3 `parse_queue_text` forward-compat to PSQ-3+ field lines unspecified; M4 AC5 packs 3 ratchets — N=2 promotion of slice-067 N=1; m1-m5 cosmetics + count fixes); meta-Critic EXTEND with **3 missed findings** (M-add-1 Major design.md L18-19 stale anchor TPHD-1 sub-mode (a) N=6 cumulative; m-add-1 milestone.md L29 prose stale; m-add-2 `parse_queue_text` unclaimed-entry shape underspecified). 14/14 dispositions ratified at TRI-1 user-owned triage — final verdict CLEAN; all ACCEPTED-FIXED in-band on mission-brief.md + design.md + ADR-067.md + milestone.md before /build-slice. **Slice-040 N+1 first-governed-slice doctrine extends to N=19 cumulative on parallel-slice family axis** (slice-067 minted PSQ-1; slice-072 first governed-slice on claim-machinery axis adjacent to PSQ-1's queue-output axis; meta-Critic empirically caught the TPHD-1 sub-mode (a) intra-design.md cross-doc harmonization gap from the AC5→AC6 split fix-block).

**Slice-070 N=3 switch-commit-switch-worktree pattern** confirmed stable: slice-070 + slice-071 + slice-072 all entered /build-slice with dirty pre-build vault artifacts on master + executed the canonical sequence `git switch -c slice/072-... → commit pre-build → git switch master → git worktree add slice/072-...` to satisfy BRANCH-2 prereq rule 4 cleanly. **3-Critic stack N=8 cumulative** (slice-063 → slice-072 inclusive — design-Critic + meta-Critic + code-Critic structural complementarity validated; code-Critic runs at next slice if advisory findings emerge).

### Added

- **PSQ-2 — Parallel-Slice Queue Claim Machinery**
  Adds optional `Claimed-by:` + `Claimed-at:` field lines per entry on `architecture/slice-queue.md`; new `tools/slice_queue_claim` CLI provides claim / release / force-claim; `write_slice_queue` preserves existing claims across /slice Step 6.5 regeneration; `parse_queue_text` is permissive to PSQ-3+ forward-compat field lines via `_extra_field_lines` pass-through.
  - **Rule reference**: PSQ-2 (slice-072; ADR-067 mints a new rule; sibling on parallel-slice family axis; supersedes nothing; methodology v0.71.0; first rule on the claim-machinery axis adjacent to PSQ-1 queue-output axis + BRANCH-2 worktree-isolation axis).
  - **Defect class**: two parallel Claude sessions reading the queue simultaneously could rationally pick the same top-ranked candidate and start working on it in parallel worktrees (R-19); queue freshness is currently load-bearing for collision safety.
  - **Validation**: `tests/methodology/test_psq_2_claim_machinery.py` (17 unit tests covering AC1-AC4) + `tests/methodology/test_utf8_stdout_regression.py::test_slice_queue_claim_survives_cp1252_with_u2192` (bespoke cp1252 test mirroring `test_install_audit_survives_cp1252_with_u2192` at L121-129) + `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_71_0_psq_2_entry_present_in_repo` + `test_v_0_71_0_psq_2_shippability_consumer_propagation` (BC-PROJ-10 paired-pin discipline + RPCD-1 / SCPD-1 consumer-reference propagation); shippability row #72 + risk-register R-19 status flip `mitigating` → `retired`.

### Retired

- **R-19 — Stale `architecture/slice-queue.md` may mislead a second Claude session**
  Status flipped `mitigating` → `retired`; PSQ-2's `Claimed-by:` field makes queue freshness a hint not a load-bearing collision-safety signal. Predecessor-spec drift preserved verbatim: R-19 Mitigation prose + ADR-064 L37 both anticipated `session-id detection`; ADR-067 §Decision is authoritative on the chosen git-identity-only model (per Critic B2 ACCEPTED-FIXED).

## v0.69.0 — 2026-05-25

**PSQ-1 — Parallel-slice queue output: `/slice` Step 6.5 (NEW) writes `architecture/slice-queue.md` containing the top-10 parallel-safe candidates from Step 1's source-fan-out (sources #1-8), each tagged with graphify-derived blast-radius + a 4-value `Parallel-safety` enum (`NON-OVERLAPPING` | `OVERLAPS-WITH-slice-NNN[, slice-MMM]` | `UNKNOWN-NO-HINT-FILES` | `UNKNOWN-NO-GRAPH`) computed against the union of expected blast-radius file sets for currently-active slices. New helper module `tools/slice_queue_writer.py` (~430 LOC; library API + CLI; injection seam `blast_resolver` per slice-059 TVFS-1 / slice-063 NAW-1 precedent). New OSDG-1-guarded prose Step 6.5 in `skills/slice/SKILL.md` wraps the helper invocation in try/except ImportError for bootstrap-defense (slice-067 itself + future PSQ-1-revert scenario covered by ONE idempotent mechanism per slice-066 idempotent-guard lesson). (slice-067; [[ADR-064]]; mints a new rule PSQ-1; supersedes nothing; first rule on the parallel-slice family axis adjacent to BRANCH family — BRANCH-2 makes parallel work physically possible, PSQ-1 makes it discoverable; PSQ-2 / PSQ-3 will make it coordinated at slice-068 / slice-069.)**

PSQ-1 ships the **discoverability layer** of the 4-slice BRANCH-2 parallel-slice family (slice-066 worktree mechanics / slice-067 queue output / slice-068 claim machinery / slice-069 rebase + conflict discipline). Without the queue file, BRANCH-2's worktree-per-slice is physically usable but discoverability-blind — a second session has no machine-readable list of safe candidates. The queue's 5-field-per-entry contract (`Source`, `Blast-radius`, `Parallel-safety`, `Effort`, `Risk-retired`) is a stable on-disk format slice-068 (PSQ-2 claim machinery) will extend additively per ADR-064 §Consequences.

**Classification precedence** (highest priority first): `UNKNOWN-NO-GRAPH` (graphify graph missing — caller-side override) > `UNKNOWN-NO-HINT-FILES` (candidate has empty `hint_files` set) > `OVERLAPS-WITH-slice-NNN[, slice-MMM]` (non-empty intersection with one or more active slices) > `NON-OVERLAPPING` (non-empty `hint_files`, empty intersection). The `compute_parallel_safety()` early-return guards implement this precedence; the empty-hint-files-vs-zero-active-slices collision (AC4-(d)) is explicit per design.md L80. **Atomicity** (must-not-defer #1) via `.tmp` sibling + `os.replace()`; crash-durability via `os.fsync()` explicitly OUT OF SCOPE (queue is regenerable on every `/slice` invocation).

**5-part PMI-1 atomic bump** 0.68.0 → 0.69.0 covers all 5 canonical version-bearing legs: `VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + `pyproject.toml [project].version` (PVFS-1) + this `## v0.69.0` header + installed `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` (AVFS-1). **Bootstrap (slice-067 only)**: slice-067's own `/slice` invocation did NOT write `architecture/slice-queue.md` — the helper does not exist at /slice time (the slice that authors the helper cannot self-apply its own deliverable). Bootstrap-reference instance #1 per slice-066 `WORKTREE=skip-bootstrap` precedent. The queue file is first written at slice-067's `/build-slice` Phase E mid-slice smoke (dogfood). Every slice after 067's `/slice` routinely writes the queue via Step 6.5 + the ImportError guard. **Slice-040 N+1 first-governed-slice catch**: slice-067 surfaced a structural conflict between BRANCH-2 worktree-per-slice + gitignored `architecture/` (line 13 of .gitignore) — `git worktree add` creates a worktree without slice vault files. ## v0.68.0 — 2026-05-24

**BRANCH-2 — Worktree-per-slice + branch: `/build-slice` `## Prerequisite check ### Branch state` sub-section rewritten to create `git worktree add <main-parent>/<main-name>-wt/slice-NNN-<slice-name> -b slice/NNN-<slice-name> <default>` + `cd` into the worktree, replacing BRANCH-1's `git checkout -b` on the main tree. `/commit-slice --merge` (Step 5b) + `/commit-slice --sync-after-pr` (Step 5d) extended with **idempotent worktree-remove guard** before the existing safe branch-delete — order is load-bearing (a branch checked out in a worktree cannot be safely deleted per git-scm.com/docs/git-worktree). `tools/branch_workflow_audit.py` gains 4 new violation kinds (`worktree-not-registered`, `worktree-cwd-mismatch`, `worktree-path-shape-violation`, `worktree-skip-malformed`) + 4 new helpers (`_is_repo_root_a_worktree`, `_resolve_expected_worktree_path`, `_worktree_registered`, `_check_worktree_skip_line`) + new `_WORKTREE_SKIP_LINE_RE` regex mirroring `_BRANCH_SKIP_LINE_RE`'s shape. (slice-066; [[ADR-063]]; mints a new rule BRANCH-2; partial-supersedes [[ADR-019]] sub-mode (a) build-time branch-create only — sub-mode (b) was already partial-superseded by ADR-020 at slice-022; sub-mode (c) audit-time pre-finish refusal is EXTENDED in place via the 4 new violation kinds, NOT superseded.)** Closes R-17 (mitigating → retired) — the candidate-fix-(b) named verbatim at `risk-register.md:295` ships: uncommitted slice-A WIP on the main tree cannot contaminate slice-B because slice-B's filesystem is a physically distinct worktree. ADR-063 is the **N=2 application of the slice-022 [[ADR-020]] partial-supersession encoding pattern** (single `supersedes:` frontmatter slot + body-level scope enumeration; ADR-019 stays unmodified per append-only — held across N=2 partial supersessions).

The fix is structural: the worktree-create command at `## Prerequisite check ### Branch state` physically isolates the slice's filesystem from the main tree. The new audit's `worktree-cwd-mismatch` violation catches the canonical "Claude forgot to `cd` into the worktree" failure mode (cwd in main tree + slice branch checked out in worktree elsewhere) — closing the audit-vs-action mismatch class that R-17 named as a structural gap in BRANCH-1's prerequisite check. Path-comparison semantics use `Path.resolve(strict=False)` + `samefile()` + `os.path.normcase(os.path.realpath(...))` fallback to tolerate Windows symlinks/junctions/case-insensitivity (per WebSearch evidence on [microsoft/vscode#101244](https://github.com/microsoft/vscode/issues/101244) drive-letter / path-mismatch class). The `WORKTREE=skip` escape-hatch line mirrors `BRANCH=skip`'s canonical grammar shape (`<YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM> DEVIATION: WORKTREE=skip — rationale: <text>`) and is cross-spec-parity-pinned across N=3 surfaces — (1) `skills/build-slice/SKILL.md` Step 7c, (2) `skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md` `--merge`/`--sync-after-pr` reminder, (3) `tools/branch_workflow_audit.py` `_WORKTREE_SKIP_LINE_RE`. `BRANCH=skip` is **preserved post-BRANCH-2 as a legacy parallel escape-hatch** (per ADR-063 §Scope of supersession "Carried forward unchanged" 4th-surface inheritance) — both grammars coexist.

**5-part PMI-1 atomic bump** 0.67.0 → 0.68.0 covers all 5 canonical version-bearing legs per slice-063/064 canonical anchor: `VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + `pyproject.toml [project].version` (PVFS-1) + this `## v0.68.0` header + installed `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` (AVFS-1 forward-sync). Plus two separate post-bump forward-sync obligations (NOT PMI-1 atomic-bump legs; per slice-063 M-add-1 leg-enumeration discipline): installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` (MCFS-1 cp-sync) + installed venv `ai-sdlc-tools` 0.68.0 (TVFS-1 via `$PY -m pip install --upgrade .`). **CLAUDE.md L32 edit + shippability row #66 are SEPARATE BC-PROJ-9 5-inventory + BC-PROJ-10 paired-entry-pin consumer-propagation surfaces, NOT PMI-1 parts** (per slice-066 /build-slice Phase A Builder-self-catch — rev-1 design.md + ADR-063 §Consequences mistakenly enumerated these inside the 5-part bump; corrected pre-build).

**Bootstrap (slice-066 only)**: slice-066 itself runs WITHOUT worktree mode at its own `/build-slice` (the SKILL.md prose authoring the worktree-create step doesn't yet exist at slice-066 start; cannot self-apply). Discharge: canonical `WORKTREE=skip-bootstrap — rationale: slice-066 authors the worktree-create prose; bootstrap-reference instance #1` line in `build-log.md` Events. The **idempotent worktree-remove guard** added per /critique B5 ACCEPTED-FIXED additionally makes slice-066's own `/commit-slice --merge` safe — when the worktree doesn't exist (the bootstrap case), the guard logs "worktree absent — skip" and proceeds to branch-delete. Every slice from slice-067 onward inherits a self-gating BRANCH-2 audit.

## v0.67.0 — 2026-05-23

**Extend NAW-1 union-of-three-sources to /code-review: `skills/code-review/SKILL.md` Step 1 diff resolution rewritten to mirror slice-063 NAW-1 / ADR-061 §Decision L60-67 union-of-three-sources read mechanism (working-tree-vs-base + `git ls-files --others --exclude-standard` untracked + commits-vs-base, unioned + deduplicated by path) — closes the B1 falsifier class on the /code-review SKILL.md surface (N=2 cumulative across NAW-1's audit-tool surface + /code-review's SKILL.md surface; slice-063 retired the class on NAW-1; this slice retires it on /code-review) (slice-064; ADR-062; mints no new rule; supersedes nothing — the slice-064 entry is a sibling-surface application of NAW-1's read mechanism, NOT a retirement-discharge / pure-conformance shape — extends the canonical pattern from one methodology surface to another without minting a new RULE-ID).** Pre-slice-064 /code-review SKILL.md Step 1 used a single `git diff "$base"...HEAD --name-only --diff-filter=ACMR` command — commit-vs-commit only per [git-scm.com/docs/git-diff](https://git-scm.com/docs/git-diff) "`<commit>...<commit>` … starting at a common ancestor of both" — which returns EMPTY at `/build-slice` Step 6 where slice work is uncommitted in the working tree (commits land at `/commit-slice` per PCA-1 HARD-STOP terminal contract). Every governed slice's in-loop /code-review hop was a silent false-positive on the clean-tree-from-Step-6 invocation (writing `Result: NO-CODE-CHANGES — nothing to review` and auto-advancing to /validate-slice with the slice's actual code diff entirely unreviewed). Slice-063 reflection L28 + L94 + L40 explicitly named this as the active slice-064 deferral; slice-061 + slice-062 + slice-063 governed slices all hit it pre-fix; SOAD-1-augmented WT-aware workarounds held for slice-063 but every future slice's /code-review otherwise re-applies the same manual SOAD-1 augmentation. **Behavior change** (per this file's Inclusion heuristic — extending an existing read mechanism's coverage to a sibling methodology surface without minting a new RULE-ID; precedent slice-049/050/051/057/058/059/062 N=7 cumulative + slice-064 = **N=8 cumulative inclusive**): a /code-review SKILL.md invocation at clean-tree-from-Step-6 was acceptable-yesterday (the silent false-positive auto-advanced without any audit refusing it) and is refused-today (the union-of-three-sources resolution observes all states slice code can occupy at Step 6, and the `no-code-changes` branch fires only on a genuinely empty union — vault-only slice or reverted-WT-before-Step-6).

The fix is surgical: `skills/code-review/SKILL.md` Step 1's bash block is rewritten to invoke three commands in sequence — (i) `git diff "$base" --name-only --diff-filter=ACMR -- ':(exclude)architecture/**' ':(exclude)docs/**'` (working-tree-vs-base; modified + staged-but-uncommitted adds), (ii) `git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- ':(exclude)architecture/**' ':(exclude)docs/**'` (untracked-new), (iii) `git diff "$base"...HEAD --name-only --diff-filter=ACMR -- ':(exclude)architecture/**' ':(exclude)docs/**'` (commits-vs-base; the pre-slice-064 leg preserved as one of three sources). Inline literal pathspecs on each leg (POSIX-portable; NOT bash-array `exclude=(...)` + `"${exclude[@]}"` which would silently fail on POSIX `sh`; per /critique M1 fix). After the three commands, **post-bash-block prose explicitly instructs Claude to "Union the three outputs by path; deduplicate"** — Claude's runtime aggregation obligation is SKILL.md-prose-binding, NOT ADR-only (per /critique M2 fix + CLAUDE.md "skill prose IS executable contract" axiom; without the prose pin a future Claude could plausibly run only Source (iii) for token budget, concatenate without deduplicating, intersect instead of union, or ignore Source (ii) — all silent failures the AC#1 filter-shape pin alone cannot reach). Step 2's prompt-template `# Diff content` block is rewritten in lock-step to use per-file `git diff "$base" -- <file>` (WT-vs-base; observes uncommitted edits) instead of the stale aggregate `git diff <base>...HEAD -- <files>` (commit-vs-commit) that would silently re-introduce the B1 falsifier downstream on the per-file diff content even after Step 1's union resolves the file list correctly. The Step 1 `default-branch-unresolvable` and `no-code-changes` error cases are preserved verbatim; the latter's semantics tighten from "fires on every governed slice (false-positive)" to "fires only on a genuinely-empty union (e.g., vault-only edit or reverted WT)".

Plus two separate post-bump forward-sync obligations (NOT PMI-1 atomic-bump legs; per slice-063 M-add-1 leg-enumeration discipline): installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` (MCFS-1 cp-sync) + installed venv `ai-sdlc-tools` 0.67.0 (TVFS-1 via `$PY -m pip install --upgrade .`). **BC-PROJ-9 5-inventory fan-out does NOT fire** — slice-064 adds zero `tools/*.py` modules, zero new `skills/*/SKILL.md` files (it modifies one existing), zero `agents/*.md` files; the 5 inventories `install_audit._CANONICAL_TOOLS` + `test_utf8_stdout._ROOT_ONLY_TOOLS` + `plugin.yaml` tools-block + `INSTALL.md` tool-count literals (L22 + L166) + the new shippability row are: first 4 UNCHANGED, 5th (row #64) IS added by this slice but for BCR-1 traceability + entry-pin propagation, NOT for new-`tools/*.py` registration.

## v0.66.0 — 2026-05-23

**NAW-1 — New-Agent Warning: `/build-slice` Step 6 emits a non-blocking WARN when the slice diff adds any `agents/*.md` file, naming the new agent path + the session-restart-before-next-slice recommendation + the R-18 cross-reference (slice-063; ADR-061; mints a new rule; supersedes nothing — the first audit-enforced gate on the **discovery-gate** axis, adjacent to but NOT extending the PMI-1 / PVFS-1 / AVFS-1 / MCFS-1 / TVFS-1 forward-sync family).** R-18 (slice-061-discovered; N=2 cumulative recurrence at slice-061 + slice-062): the Claude Code agent registry is loaded at session start; mid-session writes to `~/.claude/agents/*.md` are invisible to `Agent(subagent_type=…)` calls until the user restarts Claude Code. Both witnessed slices (slice-061 + slice-062) hit `Agent type 'code-review' not found` at their `/code-review` auto-advance hop because slice-060 shipped the `code-review` subagent in the SAME Claude Code session. slice-062 reflection L40 + L78 promoted R-18 from watch-list to active slice-063 mitigation nomination. **Behavior change** (per this file's Inclusion heuristic — a new RULE-ID + new ADR + new in-loop methodology gate + new `tools/*.py` + new agent registry-cache discovery surface): a slice that ships a new subagent without methodology-discoverable surface was acceptable-yesterday (the failure-mode showed up only AT the next slice's `/code-review` auto-advance, too late) and is refused-today (NAW-1 surfaces the failure-mode at `/build-slice` Step 6 BEFORE the next slice's chain runs, retiring R-18 `mitigating` → `retired`).

The new standalone tool `tools/new_agent_warning_audit.py` clones the AVFS-1/TVFS-1 structural shape (CLI `--root` / `--check` / `--json`; `CheckResult` dataclass; `tools._stdout` UTF-8 first-statement; `main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int` signature) with a specialized **union-of-three-sources read mechanism** (B1 critique fix). The naive `git diff <base>...HEAD` is commit-vs-commit only (per [git-scm.com/docs/git-diff](https://git-scm.com/docs/git-diff) "`<commit>...<commit>` … starting at a common ancestor of both") and would return EMPTY for an uncommitted new agent — slice work lives in the uncommitted working tree at `/build-slice` Step 6 (commits land only at `/commit-slice`, the LAST step, user-invoked post-`/reflect`). The audit therefore reads a union of three sources covering all states a new agent file can occupy at Step 6: (i) `git diff --name-only --diff-filter=A {base} -- 'agents/*.md'` (working-tree-vs-base; modified + staged-but-uncommitted adds), (ii) `git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- 'agents/*.md'` (untracked-new), (iii) `git diff --name-only --diff-filter=A {base}...HEAD -- 'agents/*.md'` (commits-vs-base). Results unioned + deduplicated. Default-branch resolution mirrors BRANCH-1's logic at `tools/branch_workflow_audit.py:127-146` — `git symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` → `git config init.defaultBranch` → `None` (M3 critique fix: there is NO `main` literal fallback in the implementation; `None` ⇒ NAW-1 exits 2). Two injection seams (`default_branch_resolver`, `added_files_resolver`) make the audit deterministically testable per the m3-redesigned seam-driven self-application pattern (slice-059 TVFS-1 `installed_version_resolver` precedent).

**Binary exit contract by construction** (intentionally narrower than AVFS-1/TVFS-1's tri-state — there is NO "drift" branch for a discovery gate): `0` clean (no added agents, quiet stdout) or `0` warn (≥1 added, WARN line(s) on stdout each citing agent path + session-restart + R-18); `2` usage error (repo root unresolvable, `git` unavailable, default-branch resolution failed, or any git subprocess call non-zero). **NEVER exit 1** — a new-agent slice is NOT a slice regression; the WARN is informational, never a HALT. The pytest regression suite explicitly asserts `exit_code == 0` on BOTH clean AND warn branches (the load-bearing contract).

The **5-part PMI-1 atomic bump** 0.65.0 → 0.66.0 covers all 5 canonical version-bearing legs per slice-060 + slice-062 precedent (NOT the slice-059 4-part shape, which had no new tool): `VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + `pyproject.toml [project].version` (PVFS-1) + this `## v0.66.0` header + installed `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` (AVFS-1 forward-sync). Plus two separate post-bump forward-sync obligations bucketed under BC-PROJ-9 fan-out (NOT PMI-1 atomic-bump legs per M-add-1 meta-Critic fix): installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` (MCFS-1 cp-sync) + installed venv `ai-sdlc-tools` 0.66.0 (TVFS-1 via `$PY -m pip install --upgrade .`). The BC-PROJ-9 5-inventory fan-out for the new `tools/*.py` module touches `plugin.yaml` + `tools/install_audit.py` `_CANONICAL_TOOLS` + `tests/methodology/test_utf8_stdout_regression.py` `_ROOT_ONLY_TOOLS` + `INSTALL.md` two hardcoded `27` → `28` literals at L22 + L166 + `architecture/shippability.md` row #63. Wiring is 1-point (Step 6 only) — there is intentionally NO `/reflect` Step 5b-naw counterpart because NAW-1 has no installed-side mutation to verify (distinct from AVFS-1/MCFS-1/TVFS-1's `/reflect` Step 5b-X parity; adding one would be cargo-cult parity, not a real gate).

Dual-Critic stack: first Critic BLOCKED with 1 Blocker (B1 read-mechanism falsification — `git diff <base>...HEAD` is commit-vs-commit only per git-scm.com; would return EMPTY for uncommitted Step-6 work; web-confirmed + execution-confirmed) + 4 Majors (M1 phantom test-file `test_risk_register.py` PTFCD-1, M2 `--strict-pre-finish` CLI flag drift FBCD-1 sub-mode (a), M3 BRANCH-1 "fallback `main`" misattribution against `tools/branch_workflow_audit.py:127-146` implementation, M4 AC#2 test housed in wrong file Mini-CAD-1 vs structural-anchor) + 5 Minors (m1 BC-PROJ-9 attribution to slice-050 instead of slice-059, m2 overbroad `agents/*.md` pathspec, m3 bootstrap-brittle self-application test, m4 CAD-1/OSDG-1 family-confusion phrasing, m5 missing EPGD-1 entry-pin anchor list). ## v0.64.0 — 2026-05-23

**CRSI-1 — Code-Review Skill Insertion: `/code-review` is added to the canonical per-slice pipeline chain between `/build-slice` and `/validate-slice` as a walking-skeleton in-loop adversarial code-Critic step (slice-060; ADR-059; mints a new rule; supersedes nothing; extends the dual-Critic `/critique` + `/critique-review` design-review family onto code-as-artifact).** The PCA-1 canonical chain grows from 8 → 9 covered skills (the v0.41.0 entry's "8 covered skills" phrasing is preserved verbatim as historical record per SUP-1 append-only). v1 is **advisory only** — findings are written to `architecture/slices/slice-NNN-<name>/code-review.md` but DO NOT block `/validate-slice` (no TRI-1 triage gate, no verdict-driven HALT). Slice-061 follows with AI-bloat passes (multi-impls / half-wired modules / stale scaffolding / session-break inconsistency ported from `/diagnose`); slice-062 follows with the TRI-1 triage gate + verdict-driven block on `/validate-slice` predecessor entry. **Behavior change** (per this file's Inclusion heuristic — a new RULE-ID + a new ADR + new in-loop pipeline step + new skill + new agent + canonical chain extension): a slice that ships without per-slice adversarial code-review was acceptable-yesterday (no in-loop code-review surface existed) and is refused-today (the PCA-1 chain now routes `/build-slice` → `/code-review` → `/validate-slice`; auto-advance forces the new step on every slice).

The new `/code-review` skill (`skills/code-review/SKILL.md`) orchestrates the Agent-tool spawn of a new code-Critic agent (`agents/code-review.md`) which inherits structural fields from `agents/critique.md` verbatim modulo design→code substitution: same 9 dimensions with code-as-artifact failure-mode examples, same framework citations (Wiegers, Cockburn, Hendrickson, Fowler, Beck, Patton, Newman, Fielding, OWASP, McGraw, Sommerville, ISO/IEC/IEEE 42010, Kiczales), same `path/to/file.py:line` specificity rule, same tool set (`Read, Glob, Grep, Bash, WebSearch`), same model (`opus`). The skill resolves the slice's filtered code diff (`git diff <base>...HEAD --name-only --diff-filter=ACMR -- ':(exclude)architecture/**' ':(exclude)docs/**'`), hands inputs to the agent, writes findings to `code-review.md` (always written even on `NO-CODE-CHANGES`), and auto-advances to `/validate-slice`. Read-only safety: the agent's `tools:` frontmatter omits `Write`, `Edit`, `NotebookEdit` — this is the load-bearing access-control surface per ADR-059 / per Claude Code subagent-frontmatter being a hard runtime constraint ([Create custom subagents](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/sub-agents); skill-frontmatter `allowed-tools` enforcement bug GitHub anthropics/claude-code#18837 does NOT apply because the slice uses subagent frontmatter, not skill frontmatter).

The **5-part PMI-1 atomic bump** 0.63.0 → 0.64.0 covers all version-bearing legs: `VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + `pyproject.toml [project].version` + this `## v0.64.0` header + installed `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` (AVFS-1 forward-sync). Slice-060's drift-guard family-add extends OSDG-1 (`tests/methodology/test_code_review_skill_drift.py` — slice-049/051 lineage) AND CAD-1 (`tests/methodology/test_code_review_agent_drift.py` — slice-007 lineage), both reusing `tests/skill_drift_equality.py::assert_md_forward_synced` (EOL-DRIFT-1 / ADR-033 EOL-agnostic comparator). The PCA-1 audit (`tools/pipeline_chain_audit.py`) extends `_CANONICAL_CHAIN` from 8 to 9 tuple entries; six hardcoded "8" references across `tools/pipeline_chain_audit.py` (lines 56, 225, 309) + `tools/install_audit.py:84` + `tests/methodology/test_pipeline_chain_audit.py` (docstring + `assert len(result.skills_checked) == 8`) + `tests/methodology/test_pipeline_position_block_drift.py` (docstring/messages) all updated to "9" in lock-step (SCPD-1 sub-mode (b) consumer-reference propagation onto `architecture/shippability.md` row #27 — the row prose stays historical per SUP-1 but `test_clean_chain_exits_zero`'s assertion is propagated). PMI-1 atomic-bump inventory fan-out (BC-PROJ-9 sub-class for this slice) is 5-part because no new `tools/*.py` is introduced — slice-060 is a skill+agent member-addition slice, not a new-tool slice.

Dual-Critic stack: first Critic NEEDS-FIXES with 5 Blockers + 5 Majors + 4 Minors (all VALID at correct severity); meta-Critic EXTEND with M-add-1 (Phase B forward-sync omitted edited `skills/build-slice/SKILL.md` + `skills/validate-slice/SKILL.md` — the same fan-out class as B2/B5; Fowler refactoring discipline) + M-add-2 (TF-1 plan omitted the BC-PROJ-10:173 paired `_shippability_consumer_propagation` test — N≥17 instances stable across the changelog test module) + m-add-1 (Dim 9 reframe clarification on phantom-import-in-`.py` as code-as-artifact under Dim 1 / Dim 5); all 17 ACCEPTED-FIXED by Builder; TRI-1 user-ratified verdict CLEAN. Calibration note for /reflect: the meta-Critic caught 3 build-time-REACHABLE classes the first Critic missed in Builder's own /critique fix-prose — slice-049 / slice-042 "design correction is itself an unguarded adversarial surface" class, N=4+ cumulative.

## v0.63.0 — 2026-05-23

**TVFS-1 — ai-sdlc-tools Version Forward-Sync: the `ai-sdlc-tools` pip distribution installed in the running interpreter's venv site-packages MUST equal the trimmed contents of the `VERSION` file (slice-059; ADR-058; mints a new rule; supersedes nothing; extends the PMI-1 / PVFS-1 / AVFS-1 / MCFS-1 forward-sync family onto the installed-pip-artifact leg).** After slice-054/PVFS-1 the version-bearing surface had one ungated leg left: the *installed* `ai-sdlc-tools` pip package. PVFS-1 gates the *source* `pyproject.toml [project].version`, so every *new* `pip install <repo>` builds a correctly-versioned wheel — but nothing forces the *re-install*. The installed package drifted silently to `0.20.0` while the source advanced to `0.62.0` (15 of 26 canonical tool modules present; CRP-1, PCA-1, BRANCH-1 absent); the gap is invisible from the source repo — where `$PY -m tools.X` resolves the in-repo `tools/` package — yet breaks every other adopted project, where it resolves to the stale venv copy. **Behavior change** (per this file's Inclusion heuristic — a new RULE-ID on the forward-sync family + new ADR + 4-part PMI-1 atomic bump): an installed-pip-package version drift was acceptable-yesterday (no gate caught it) and is refused-today (TVFS-1 HALTs `/build-slice` Step 6 / `/reflect` Step 5b-tvfs). The gate is the standalone tool `tools/ai_sdlc_tools_version_forward_sync.py` — a structural clone of AVFS-1 (`tools/ai_sdlc_version_forward_sync.py`): tri-state exit contract (0 synced / 0 not-installed-WARN / 1 drift-HALT / 2 usage), `_stdout` UTF-8 convention. A standalone tool — NOT a PVFS-1-style pytest assertion — because the read is out-of-repo, environment-mutable, runtime metadata (ADR-058 sits on the satisfied side of the ADR-056 tool-vs-pytest boundary; AVFS-1 / MCFS-1 precedent).

**Read-mechanism hazard (B1, first-Critic finding).** The naive `importlib.metadata.version("ai-sdlc-tools")` is CWD-shadowed by the in-repo `ai_sdlc_tools.egg-info/` build artifact — `importlib.metadata` discovers metadata from every `sys.path` entry, and PVFS-1 keeps the egg-info `Version` equal to `VERSION`, so the naive read is tautologically green even when the venv is stale. `_resolve_installed_version()` therefore scopes the enumeration to `sysconfig.get_path("purelib")`, excluding the egg-info by construction. `check()` exposes an `installed_version_resolver` injection seam (B2) so the regression suite drives a genuine non-tautological drift case; the `>1` duplicate-`.dist-info` case carries its own distinct exit-2 remediation message (M-add-2).

The 4-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.62.0 → 0.63.0 covers the canonical-VERSION legs: `VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + `pyproject.toml [project].version` + this `## v0.63.0` header + installed `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` (AVFS-1 forward-sync). slice-059's own Step 6 is the TVFS-1 bootstrap: the build sequence runs `$PY -m pip install --upgrade .` after the bump to refresh the installed `ai-sdlc-tools` → `0.63.0`. Dual-Critic stack: first Critic BLOCKED (3 blockers / 3 majors / 2 minors — B1 the empirically-proven CWD-shadowing defect), meta-Critic EXTEND (all 8 first-Critic findings confirmed VALID at correct severity + M-add-1 / M-add-2 missed); all 10 ACCEPTED-FIXED, TRI-1 verdict CLEAN.

- **Rule reference**: TVFS-1 (slice-059; ADR-058; mints a new rule; supersedes nothing — naming-class peer of PMI-1 / PVFS-1 / AVFS-1 / MCFS-1 forward-sync-family rules).
- **Defect class**: the installed `ai-sdlc-tools` pip package drifts from `VERSION` (a version-bumping slice that forgets `pip install --upgrade`); or the v0.63.0 entry / ADR-058 lineage / `ai-sdlc-tools Version Forward-Sync` rule-name expansion / `mints a new rule` / `supersedes nothing` / `Rule reference` / `4-part PMI-1 atomic bump` anchor silently lost.
- **Validation method**: `tests/methodology/test_ai_sdlc_tools_version_forward_sync.py` (8 tests: 4 states + duplicate-dist + egg-info-isolation + wiring + non-catalog); the v0.63.0 entry-pins `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_63_0_tvfs_1_entry_present_in_repo` + `::test_v_0_63_0_tvfs_1_shippability_consumer_propagation`; `architecture/shippability.md` row 59; and the PMI-1 4-part atomic version bump 0.62.0 → 0.63.0.

Gate is the standalone tool `tools/ai_sdlc_tools_version_forward_sync.py` (purelib-scoped read defeating the in-repo `egg-info` CWD-shadowing class; `installed_version_resolver` test seam); wired non-opt-out at `/build-slice` Step 6 + a dedicated `/reflect` Step 5b-tvfs. Closes the last ungated version-bearing leg — the installed pip artifact.
  - **Rule reference**: TVFS-1 (slice-059; ADR-058 mints a new rule; supersedes nothing; extends the PMI-1 / PVFS-1 / AVFS-1 / MCFS-1 forward-sync family onto the installed-pip-artifact leg)

## v0.62.0 — 2026-05-21

**PVFS-1 — Pyproject Version Forward Sync: `pyproject.toml [project].version` MUST equal the trimmed contents of the `VERSION` file (slice-054; ADR-056; mints a new rule; supersedes nothing; extends the PMI-1 / AVFS-1 / MCFS-1 forward-sync family on the pyproject.toml leg).** Before slice-054 the canonical-VERSION lock-step gate family had a structural carve-out: PMI-1 (`tools/plugin_manifest_audit.py:207-217`, slice-021 / v0.19.0) gates `plugin.yaml.version` against `VERSION` only — it does NOT read `pyproject.toml`. Pyproject was an ungated PMI-1 sibling: not a deliberate exemption, just an uncovered surface. The drift went unnoticed for ~41 methodology-changelog versions: `pyproject.toml [project].version = "0.20.0"` while `VERSION = "0.61.0"`, so `pip install <repo>` produced `ai-sdlc-tools 0.20.0` for every external consumer — a stale pip artifact label 41 minor versions behind the methodology semver. SC-001 in `diagnose-out/backlog.md` (CRITICAL severity, topmost in `## Recommended order`) surfaced the gap via the slice-053 `/diagnose` round-trip; slice-054 closes the carve-out by minting PVFS-1 as the pyproject↔VERSION gate sibling of PMI-1's plugin.yaml↔VERSION gate. **Behavior change** (per this file's Inclusion heuristic — a new RULE-ID on the methodology-surface forward-sync family + new ADR + 4-part PMI-1 atomic bump): a pyproject↔VERSION drift was acceptable-yesterday (no gate caught it) and is refused-today (the new test FAILs the methodology suite + HALTs `/build-slice` Step 6 / PCA-1 auto-advance). The gate is the pytest assertion at `tests/methodology/test_pyproject_version_matches_version_file.py::test_repro_sc001_pyproject_project_version_matches_version_file` (BFRD-1-established at `/repro` BEFORE the fix; WRITTEN-FAILING with `'0.20.0' == '0.61.0'` pre-fix; PASSING with `'0.62.0' == '0.62.0'` post-fix). The 4-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.61.0 → 0.62.0 covers all 4 canonical-VERSION legs: `VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + this `## v0.62.0` header + installed `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` (AVFS-1 forward-sync). **No standalone audit tool minted** (SOAD-1/BCR-1 rule-without-tool precedent — the pytest assertion IS the gate; slice-050 AVFS-1 minted a standalone tool because `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` lives OUT of repo, requiring runtime read, while pyproject.toml is in-repo so pytest is structurally sufficient — Route C was correctly rejected at /design-slice as over-engineering per /reduce). `tools/plugin_manifest_audit.py` (PMI-1) is **NOT edited** — PVFS-1 is a sibling gate, not a PMI-1 sub-rule.

**First end-to-end BCR-1 round-trip dogfood**: slice-054 is the first slice to exercise slice-053's `**Closes:** SC-NNN` → `/reflect`-injects-`**Addressed:**` wire. The mission-brief.md carries the `**Closes:** SC-001` sentinel header at the top; `/reflect` Step 2 will append `- **Addressed:** slice-054-fix-pyproject-toml-version-drift on 2026-05-21` to `diagnose-out/backlog.md` SC-001 block AFTER `**Evidence:**` (M4 sentinel-anchored trigger). AC4 verification at /validate-slice walks the position-pin (awk + line-number) to confirm BCR-1's positional insertion contract holds on its first real exercise. The shippability row #54 PVFS-1 enrichment cites BOTH the new `PVFS-1` rule-ID AND the originating `SC-001` finding-ID (per /critique-review M-add-1 — the SC-NNN trace axis MUST be regression-pinned on the slice that mints the BCR-1 dogfood).

The dual-Critic stack (first Critic NEEDS-FIXES with 0B/4M/2m all VALIDATED-ACCEPTED-FIXED; meta-Critic EXTEND with 1 Major missed M-add-1 + 1 Minor missed m-add-1 both VALIDATED-ACCEPTED-FIXED + 1 self-withdrawn m-add-2 transparently recorded) ran with zero false alarms / zero severity miscalibrations / zero over-eager accepts. The N=6 zero-false-alarm streak on codification slices (046/048/050/051/052/053) extends to N=7. Notable findings: M1 (AC3 pin test left "TBD at /design-slice" → minted `test_pyproject_has_no_stale_0_20_0_or_count_literals` co-located in existing test_pyproject_version_matches_version_file.py), M2 (stale-literal enumeration 2-of-3 → expanded to 3-of-3 sites: lines 3+6+66), M3 (FBCD-1 sub-mode (a) smoke-gate literal cross-file inconsistency mission-brief `0.61.0` vs design `0.62.0` → mission-brief realigned to `0.62.0`), M4 (AC4 BCR-1 grep position-semantic gap → tightened to position-pinned awk + line-number check), M-add-1 (PVFS-1 entry-pin shippability-consumer-propagation pinned only PVFS-1, missing SC-001 — BCR-1 traceability-axis pin on first dogfood; design expanded to assert BOTH), m-add-1 (function-name `v_0_20_0` vs assertion bare `0.20.0` cosmetic divergence → renamed to drop `v_` prefix; TPHD-1 sub-mode (b) harmonized design.md + mission-brief TF-1 row 3). m1 (stale `VERSION = 0.59.0` heading in SC-001 backlog block) DEFERRED to `/critic-calibrate` per BCR-1 append-only discipline. PRESERVED unchanged: PMI-1 (`tools/plugin_manifest_audit.py`) is NOT edited (PVFS-1 is sibling not sub-rule); the AVFS-1 / MCFS-1 / OSDG-1 / BCR-1 / SOAD-1 / STP-1 / PCA-1 / BCI-1 / CRP-1 / BRANCH-1 / UTF8-STDOUT-1 gates; the slice-049/050 entry-pin schema; INST-1 canonical inventories (no new tool/skill/agent — only `tests/methodology/test_pyproject_version_matches_version_file.py` extension + 2 entry-pin tests in test_methodology_changelog.py + 1 BCR-1 input-contract test + an ADR + the version bump).
