## v0.61.0 — 2026-05-21

**BCR-1 — Backlog Consume-and-Round-trip discipline: when `diagnose-out/backlog.md` exists in the repo, `/slice` MUST consult it as a primary candidate source AND `/reflect` MUST round-trip closed `SC-NNN` findings back into the file (slice-053; ADR-055 extends the BC-PROJ-10 / Inclusion-heuristic lineage; mints a new rule; supersedes nothing).** Before slice-053 the `/diagnose → /slice-candidates → /slice → /reflect` pipeline was half-open: `/slice` enumerated 6 candidate sources (risk register / recent deferrals / recent discoveries / concept scope / aggregated lessons / user-stated intent) but did NOT name `diagnose-out/backlog.md` — the `/slice-candidates` round-trip output containing owner-confirmed slice candidates was structurally invisible to Claude at `/slice` invocation. Symmetric gap on the return path: `/reflect` Step 2 (vault updates) named risk-register / ADRs / concept / design.md but NOT `diagnose-out/backlog.md`, so closed backlog rows accumulated as silent "to-do" entries. The deviation surfaced empirically on slice-053's own `/slice` invocation (2026-05-20): a `backlog.md` containing 26 owner-confirmed candidates (incl. the critical-severity SC-001 `pyproject.toml [project].version = 0.20.0` vs `VERSION = 0.60.0` live `pip install` defect) existed in the repo and Claude ranked candidates from internal pipeline signals alone — missed the entire backlog. The user interrupted and flagged the missing-wire as the major deviation; slice-053 wires both halves of the loop. **Behavior change** (per this file's Inclusion heuristic — a new behavior contract on user-invocable skill surfaces + new ADR + new RULE-ID): a `/slice` invocation that skipped `backlog.md` was acceptable-yesterday (no contract told Claude to read it) and is refused-today (the new BCR-1 anchor-presence audit FAILs the methodology suite if the consume directive is silently dropped). The contract has two halves, both pinned by anchor-presence + position-pin tests in `tests/methodology/test_bcr_1_backlog_round_trip.py` (8 tests + 2 entry-pins in `test_methodology_changelog.py`; SOAD-1 multi-surface + BFRD-1 section-scoped precedents):

- **Consume side (`/slice`)**: when `diagnose-out/backlog.md` exists, it is a mandatory candidate source in the "Gather candidates from ALL these sources" block. Absent file → no-op clean. The `/slice` SKILL.md prose names the file literally + carries the M1-locked canonical phrase `MUST consult diagnose-out/backlog.md as a mandatory candidate source when it exists` verbatim (slice-053 first-Critic M1 ACCEPTED-FIXED: locked full-sentence at design time, NOT a two-token shaving, per BFRD-1 / SOAD-1 precedent).
- **Round-trip side (`/reflect`)**: when a just-completed slice's `mission-brief.md` OR `reflection.md` carries an explicit `**Closes:** SC-\d{3}[, SC-\d{3}, …]` sentinel header (mirrors GitHub closes-issue convention; mentioned-vs-closes disambiguation per slice-053 first-Critic M4 ACCEPTED-FIXED — bare `SC-\d{3}` regex over the whole prose would false-trigger on documentation-class mentions), `/reflect` Step 2 MUST update `diagnose-out/backlog.md` with an in-place additive `- **Addressed:** slice-NNN-<name> on YYYY-MM-DD` line at the END of each closed candidate block — AFTER the `**Evidence:**` sub-bullet list (or after the last top-level metadata bullet if no `**Evidence:**` list per slice-053 /critique-review M-add-1 graceful-degradation clause), BEFORE the next `### SC-NNN` header or end-of-file. Multiple `**Addressed:**` lines under one candidate are valid (prior-slice double-shipment per slice-053 first-Critic m5) — APPEND, never replace. The `/reflect` SKILL.md prose names the file literally + carries the M1-locked canonical phrase `append **Addressed:** slice-NNN-<name> on YYYY-MM-DD under each closed candidate block` verbatim, the M2-extended trigger-regex grammar pin `SC-\d{3}` (R-13 BCR-1↔producer-side cross-skill brittleness mitigation — Newman contract-test framework; the audit FAILs until both sides agree on the grammar), and the M4 closes-sentinel literal `**Closes:** SC-`.

**Slice-053 self-bootstrap**: slice-053 itself does NOT carry a `**Closes:** SC-NNN` sentinel header in its mission-brief.md (the literal `**Closes:**` is absent — only `**SC-001**` bold-emphasis mentions appear, which are documentation-class context, not closure-trigger), so the BCR-1 round-trip correctly no-ops on this slice. The first slice that triggers the round-trip will be slice-054 = SC-001 fix-pyproject-toml-version-drift (a critical-severity live defect — `pip install` produces a 40-minor-version-stale artifact — queued as the immediate next-slice candidate via the now-wired channel).

The dual-Critic stack (first Critic NEEDS-FIXES with 1B/4M/5m all VALIDATED-ACCEPTED-FIXED; meta-Critic EXTEND with 3 additional Minor missed findings M-add-1/2/3, all VALIDATED-ACCEPTED-FIXED) ran cleanly with zero false alarms / zero severity miscalibrations / zero over-eager accepts. Notable findings: B1 (insert anchor under `**Risk profile:**` was structurally fragile — mid-metadata-block insert displacing fixed-position metadata bullets; relocated to after `**Evidence:**` block), M2 (R-13 producer-side OSDG-1 gap means `SC-\d{3}` grammar is unpinned across consumer/producer surfaces — mitigated via Test #7 consumer-side grammar pin + ADR-055 Consequences disclosure), M4 (self-bootstrap claim empirically wrong under bare `SC-\d{3}` trigger — refined to closes-sentinel), M-add-2 (Test #6 bundled three assertions, violated BFRD-1 single-assertion-per-test discipline the same m4 invoked — split into Tests #6 + #7, renumbered closes-sentinel to #8, audit module bumped to 8 tests). PRESERVED unchanged: the OSDG-1 family transitively guards the new anchor inserts (no additional drift-test wiring); the slice-046 BFRD-1 section-scoped helper pattern; the slice-048 SOAD-1 multi-surface anchor-presence audit shape; the `tests/skill_drift_equality.assert_md_forward_synced` comparator; the slice-051 / v0.59.0 content-bearing entry-pin schema; INST-1 canonical inventories (no new tool/skill/agent — only `tests/methodology/test_bcr_1_backlog_round_trip.py` + an ADR + 2 entry-pin tests + the version bump).

- **Rule reference**: BCR-1 (slice-053; ADR-055 extends the BC-PROJ-10 / Inclusion-heuristic lineage; mints a new rule; supersedes nothing — naming-class peer of BFRD-1 / SOAD-1 / SRCD-1 / AVFS-1 discipline rules).
- **Defect class**: the `/diagnose → /slice → /reflect` loop reopens silently — Claude's `/slice` invocations skip `backlog.md` (the slice-053-witnessed empirical failure mode) and/or `/reflect` invocations leave closed `SC-NNN` rows stale; or the M1-locked canonical phrases reworded out of MUST-form; or the M4 closes-sentinel reverted to bare `SC-\d{3}` (false-trigger class); or the M2 grammar pin lost (cross-skill brittleness re-opened); or the slice-053 / v0.61.0 entry / the ADR-055-extends-BC-PROJ-10 lineage silently lost from the changelog.
- **Validation method**: `tests/methodology/test_bcr_1_backlog_round_trip.py` (8-test audit module — anchor-presence + position-pin pairs per surface + canonical-phrase pin + SC-grammar pin + closes-sentinel pin — authored at /design-slice + locked at /critique M1 + split at /critique-review M-add-2; 8/8 genuine FAIL→PASS contrasts proven at the mid-slice smoke gate via the M3 save-bytes-then-restore-via-hash recipe), the v0.61.0 entry-pins `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_61_0_bcr_1_backlog_round_trip_entry_present_in_repo` + `::test_v_0_61_0_bcr_1_shippability_consumer_propagation`, `architecture/shippability.md` row 53, the CLAUDE.md Self-hosting-discipline bullet naming BCR-1, and the PMI-1 4-part atomic version bump 0.60.0 → 0.61.0.

### Added

- **BCR-1 — Backlog Consume-and-Round-trip discipline**
  When `diagnose-out/backlog.md` exists, `/slice` MUST consult it as a primary candidate source (source #7 in the "Gather candidates from ALL these sources" block; M1-locked canonical phrase `MUST consult diagnose-out/backlog.md as a mandatory candidate source when it exists`); `/reflect` MUST round-trip closed `SC-\d{3}` findings (triggered by an explicit `**Closes:** SC-\d{3}` sentinel header in mission-brief.md OR reflection.md — NOT bare mentions) with an in-place additive `- **Addressed:** slice-NNN-<name> on YYYY-MM-DD` line at the END of each closed candidate block (AFTER `**Evidence:**` sub-list, BEFORE next `### SC-NNN` header).
  - **Rule reference**: BCR-1 (slice-053; ADR-055 extends BC-PROJ-10 / Inclusion-heuristic lineage; mints a new rule; supersedes nothing)
  - **Defect class**: the `/diagnose → /slice → /reflect` loop silently half-opens (Claude skips `backlog.md` at `/slice` and/or leaves closed `SC-NNN` rows stale at `/reflect`); slice-053 wires both halves
  - **Validation**: `tests/methodology/test_bcr_1_backlog_round_trip.py` (8 tests) + `test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_61_0_bcr_1_backlog_round_trip_entry_present_in_repo` / `::test_v_0_61_0_bcr_1_shippability_consumer_propagation`; `architecture/shippability.md` row 53; PMI-1 version 0.60.0 → 0.61.0

## v0.60.0 — 2026-05-20

**`/slice-candidates` gains an `--obo` ("on behalf of") interactive review mode + a bounded `/slice-candidates` Hard-rule-#2 carve-out (slice-052; ADR-054; mints NO new RULE-ID — a new skill capability + one ADR; supersedes nothing).** Previously the only path from `/diagnose` to `/slice-candidates` was the owner reading the whole `diagnosis.html` and hand-annotating every finding in a browser — high-friction for large diagnoses, so owners skipped findings or abandoned the round-trip. `--obo` walks findings one at a time, severity-ordered (critical → high → medium → low), prompting the owner via structured `AskUserQuestion` options (Approve / Validate-then-approve / Defer / Reject-with-reason / Exit) with live progress, then bakes the decisions into a faithful `diagnosis.annotated.html` copy the existing `build_backlog.py` consumes unchanged. **Behavior change** (per this file's Inclusion heuristic — "New skills … qualify"; and the slice-049/ADR-051 law that a methodology-surface behavior change with no other bump reason still takes the `## vN` + 4-part PMI-1 bump path): a `/slice-candidates --obo …` invocation was refused-yesterday (no such mode) and is accepted-today, and ADR-054 changes the `/slice-candidates` Hard rule #2 contract (a previously-forbidden analyzed-repo source read is now permitted, narrowly).

**ADR-054 scoped source peek** is the carve-out: the `--obo` "Validate then approve" branch MAY read a finding's cited `evidence[].path` files — and ONLY those — to give a real/likely/not-real verdict before the owner confirms. Enforcement is **mechanical, not honour-system**: all source reads route through a new `build_backlog.py --obo-peek` subcommand whose `Path.resolve()` containment check refuses (non-zero + logged) any path outside the resolved per-finding allow-set (incl. `../` traversal, absolute, and `./`-prefixed evasions); SKILL.md forbids Claude from `Read`-ing analyzed-repo source directly under `--obo`. Operational parity is defined as `parse_html_state` annotation equality + `backlog.md` byte-identity to the manual browser round-trip — NOT browser-output byte-equality (the browser save is a full `outerHTML` re-render, unreproducible from Python and irrelevant to the only consumer). `--obo-write` serializes `ensure_ascii=False` (Python's default emits `\uXXXX`; the browser emits raw UTF-8) and substitutes the script block by match-span string slicing, never `re.sub` (a `re.sub` replacement would corrupt `<\/` and `\uXXXX`/backslash text in finding notes); the original `diagnosis.html` SHA-256 is asserted unchanged (Hard rule #3). `slice-candidates/SKILL.md` is deliberately NOT added to the OSDG-1 guarded set this slice (slice-049/051 own-slice precedent; recorded as risk-register R-13 + a next-slice nomination).

- **Rule reference**: ADR-054 (slice-052; bounded `/slice-candidates` Hard-rule-#2 deviation for `--obo` "Validate then approve"; reversibility: cheap; supersedes nothing; mints no new RULE-ID).
- **Defect class**: annotation friction starving `/slice-candidates` of confirmed input (the largest leak between `/diagnose` and `/slice-candidates`); plus the silent annotated-copy byte-divergence + honour-system-boundary classes the dual-Critic stack surfaced (B1 `ensure_ascii`, B2 parity target, M-add-2 `re.sub` corruption, M4 mechanical scoped-peek, m1 duplicate-block).
- **Validation method**: `tests/methodology/test_slice_candidates_obo.py` (golden + boundary regression: severity order, resume predicate, duplicate-block refusal, Hard-rule-#3 SHA invariance, `collect()` absent-not-empty semantics, `ensure_ascii=False`, M-add-2 backslash/`</script>` byte-exactness, `backlog.md` == golden, `--obo-peek` allow-set incl. `../`/absolute/`./`/unknown-finding refusal, default-path no-regression) = shippability catalog row #52.

## v0.59.0 — 2026-05-19

**OSDG-1 member-addition: the in-repo↔installed content-equality drift-guard family (OSDG-1) is extended to cover the in-loop `/reflect` skill — `skills/reflect/SKILL.md` joins the guarded set alongside `triage`/`adopt` (slice-051; ADR-053 extends slice-049/ADR-051's OSDG-1 lineage; NO new RULE-ID — OSDG-1 is extended, not superseded; supersedes nothing).** slice-050 added the AVFS-1 `Step 5b-avfs` post-write block to `skills/reflect/SKILL.md`, making it load-bearing: Claude reads the *installed* copy at `/reflect` runtime, so a forgotten forward-sync would let the AVFS-1 `ai-sdlc-VERSION` gate's `/reflect` arm silently skip on a stale install (the `/build-slice` arm lives in OSDG-1-guarded `build-slice/SKILL.md`, so the gate is not fully defeated — bounded but real). slice-050's meta-Critic (M-add-1) caught this and the reflection recorded it as an N=1 latent exposure + strong next-slice candidate. **Behavior change** (per this file's Inclusion heuristic; the ADR-051 / slice-049 B2 resolution that a drift-guard family member-addition whose slice has no other bump reason IS a methodology-surface behavior change — slice-050 aggregated-lesson codified): a `reflect/SKILL.md` forward-sync miss was acceptable-yesterday (no gate caught it) and is refused-today (the new test FAILs the methodology suite + HALTs `/build-slice` Step 6 / PCA-1 auto-advance). The guard is a scope-narrow per-file equality assertion `tests/methodology/test_reflect_skill_drift.py`, a structural twin of slice-049's `test_triage_skill_drift.py` / `test_adopt_skill_drift.py` reusing `tests/skill_drift_equality.py::assert_md_forward_synced` **verbatim** (zero new comparison logic; EOL-agnostic per EOL-DRIFT-1 / ADR-033 — CRLF↔LF is not drift). Note: the guarded set now spans the pipeline openers (`triage`/`adopt`) AND an in-loop skill (`reflect`); OSDG-1's "Opener-Skill" name is henceforth a **historical label, not a scope boundary** (ADR-053; the naming-class peer of MCT-1/EOL-DRIFT-1 retaining origin-named labels post scope-growth). OSDG-1 remains a plain (non-`-D`, non-`-T`) RULE-ID; this entry mints no new rule and **extends**, does not supersede, the slice-007 CAD-1 / slice-010 mini-CAD / slice-033 EOL-DRIFT-1 / slice-049 OSDG-1 lineage (`ADR-053.supersedes: null`). The first Critic (NEEDS-FIXES, 0B/3M/1m) caught the AC2 perturbation collision with the pre-existing co-reader `test_ai_sdlc_version_forward_sync.py::test_wired_in_build_slice_step6_and_reflect_post_write` (M1), the drift-test-is-not-itself-a-content-pin tautological-green class (M2 — consciously dispositioned: OSDG-1-membership content is pinned by three surfaces — the collected+catalog-rowed test, the CLAUDE.md enumeration prose-pin, this content-bearing entry-pin — the slice-049 accepted treatment), the unspecified shippability row-construction discipline (M3), and the imprecise ADR-053 fan-out parenthetical (m1); the meta-Critic (DR-1) returned ACCEPT (no suspicious/missed/severity adjustments; M2 explicitly held Major, not downgraded). All 4 ACCEPTED-FIXED at user TRI-1 (verdict CLEAN). Defect class: a forgotten forward-sync after a `reflect/SKILL.md` edit ships every `/reflect` invocation against stale prose — and could silently skip the AVFS-1 `/reflect` arm on a stale install — with no gate catching it (the slice-035/048/049 N=2-recurrence class generalized to a non-opener in-loop skill); or OSDG-1's reflect membership / the v0.59.0 entry / the ADR-053 lineage silently lost. Validation method: `tests/methodology/test_reflect_skill_drift.py` (genuine per-member FAIL→PASS contrast proven at the mid-slice smoke gate — synced PASS, non-EOL non-AVFS-anchor perturbation FAIL with the `DRIFT` message, restore PASS), the v0.59.0 entry-pins `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_59_0_osdg_1_reflect_member_entry_present_in_repo` + `::test_v_0_59_0_osdg_1_reflect_member_shippability_consumer_propagation`, `architecture/shippability.md` row 51, the CLAUDE.md Self-hosting-discipline Mini-CAD/OSDG-1 enumeration bullet naming `reflect` at EOL-DRIFT-1 precision, and the PMI-1 4-part atomic version bump 0.58.0 → 0.59.0. Rule reference: OSDG-1 (slice-049; ADR-051; member-added at slice-051 / ADR-053 extends the slice-007 CAD-1 / slice-010 mini-CAD / slice-033 EOL-DRIFT-1 / slice-049 OSDG-1 lineage; mints no new rule; supersedes nothing).

## v0.58.0 — 2026-05-19

**AVFS-1 — ai-sdlc-VERSION Forward-Sync Gate: a new deterministic downstream gate asserts in-repo `VERSION` is content-equal modulo line endings to the installed `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` — the 4th leg of the PMI-1 atomic version bump, previously guarded only by a per-slice-manual control (slice-050; ADR-052; extends the slice-041/MCFS-1 forward-sync-via-deterministic-downstream-gate lineage; mints a new rule; supersedes nothing).** Before slice-050 the PMI-1 4-part bump had three gated legs (PMI-1 audits `VERSION`==`plugin.yaml`; MCFS-1 guards the installed `methodology-changelog.md` leg) but the installed `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` leg had **no deterministic gate** — PMI-1 never reads the installed file. It drifted silently N=2 (slice-035 DEVIATION-2; slice-048 latent → surfaced at slice-049 with installed `0.55.0` vs in-repo `0.56.0`); slice-049's reflection + `_index.md` aggregated lessons twice nominated an MCFS-1-analogue gate as a strong next-slice candidate with a known cheap fix-shape. **Behavior change** (per this file's Inclusion heuristic — a drift-guard / methodology-surface audit-gate addition with no other bump reason IS a methodology-surface behavior change, ADR-051 / slice-049 B2 resolution): an `ai-sdlc-VERSION` forward-sync miss was acceptable-yesterday (no gate caught it) and is refused-today (the new AVFS-1 gate FAILs the methodology suite + HALTs `/build-slice` Step 6). AVFS-1 is a **standalone** verbatim structural clone of MCFS-1 (`tools/ai_sdlc_version_forward_sync.py`) with two path constants swapped — NOT folded into MCFS-1's whole-file gate (ADR-052 Option 1: lowest blast radius; MCFS-1's slice-041 R-4-retirement + m-add-1 relocation-proof + closed-world allowlist stay untouched; a future consolidation slice may merge them). The comparator is the verbatim MCFS-1 `_normalized_bytes` — CRLF→LF only, NOT trailing-whitespace/newline tolerant (a `cp` of a no-trailing-newline `VERSION` is byte-identical; tolerance would only mask corruption), CSP-1 behaviour-parity-pinned to `tests/skill_drift_equality.py::_normalized_sha256`. Semantics are MCFS-1-identical: in-repo `VERSION` missing/unreadable ⇒ exit 2 (usage); installed absent ⇒ WARN exit 0 (slice-030A meta-M3 optional-install parity); installed present but content-divergent after CRLF→LF (incl. empty-present AND whitespace-only-present — empty ≠ absent, R-4-class not reopened) ⇒ HALT exit 1 with the attributed message stating this is **NOT a slice regression** and to re-run the PMI-1 4-part forward-sync. Wired at the MCFS-1 2-point shape: `/build-slice` Step 6 pre-finish (ungated, every slice) + a dedicated `/reflect` Step 5b-avfs post-write step (explicitly NOT folded into the rule-promotion-gated Step 5b — the R-7/slice-022 silent-disable class). The AVFS-1 regression suite reads the untracked, environment-mutable installed copy and is therefore **non-catalog by construction** on the slice-029/030A environment-state ground (a Machine-cmd must not depend on environment-mutable/untracked state) — NOT essential-unregistered, which keys on the changelog path only (`tools/shippability_decoupling_audit.py` `_ESSENTIAL_SHAPES`; the m-add-2 mechanism is changelog-specific and provably does not transfer to the `ai-sdlc-VERSION` path); only the two in-repo-only `test_methodology_changelog.py` pins carry catalog row #50. The first Critic (NEEDS-FIXES, 3B/4M/3m) caught the tautological entry-pin (B1), the unmapped propagation pin (B2), the unsafe trailing-whitespace comparator vs the verbatim template + CSP-1 pin (B3), the weak self-bootstrap (M1), the invented third test file (M2), and recomputed that the non-catalog rationale does not transfer (M3 — the highest-value catch); the meta-Critic (DR-1 EXTEND) added M-add-1 (the unguarded `reflect/SKILL.md` leg this slice itself opens — design must not characterize "must-not-defer + self-run" as sufficient; future-OSDG-1 nomination recorded) and M-add-2 (the guarded `build-slice/SKILL.md` forward-sync obligation must be a pinned must-not-defer/pre-finish line). All 12 ACCEPTED-FIXED at user TRI-1 (verdict CLEAN). Defect class: a forgotten forward-sync after a PMI-1 version bump ships every `/pulse` (and any installed-copy reader) against a stale `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` with no gate catching it (the slice-035/048/049 N=2-recurrence class); or AVFS-1 / the v0.58.0 entry / the ADR-052 standalone-clone lineage silently lost. Validation method: `tests/methodology/test_ai_sdlc_version_forward_sync.py` (10-test regression suite authored test-first with a genuine FAIL→PASS contrast — synced/CRLF-tolerant/CSP-1-parity/usage/installed-absent-WARN/divergent-HALT/empty-present/whitespace-only-present/2-point-wiring/non-catalog-relocation-proof), the v0.58.0 entry-pins `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_58_0_avfs_1_entry_present_in_repo` + `::test_v_0_58_0_avfs_1_shippability_consumer_propagation`, `architecture/shippability.md` row 50, and the PMI-1 4-part atomic version bump 0.57.0 → 0.58.0. Rule reference: AVFS-1 (slice-050; ADR-052 extends the slice-041 MCFS-1 forward-sync-via-deterministic-downstream-gate lineage; mints a new rule; supersedes nothing — naming-class peer of the MCFS-1/BCI-1 deterministic-downstream-gate family).

## v0.57.0 — 2026-05-19

**OSDG-1 — Opener-Skill Drift Guard: the in-repo↔installed content-equality drift-guard family is extended to the two pipeline-opener skills `/triage` and `/adopt` (slice-049; ADR-051; extends the slice-007 CAD-1 / slice-010 mini-CAD / slice-033 EOL-DRIFT-1 lineage; mints a new rule; supersedes nothing).** Before slice-049 the mini-CAD / CAD-1 / EOL-DRIFT-1 family covered `agents/critique.md` (CAD-1) and the in-loop skills `slice`/`build_slice`/`commit_slice`/`query_design`/`critique`/`diagnose`, but NOT the two pipeline-opener skills — even though both have an in-repo `skills/<name>/SKILL.md` and an installed `~/.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` Claude reads at runtime. slice-048's reflection "Discovered" recorded this as an N=1 latent exposure: a forgotten forward-sync on either opener was caught by nothing but slice-048 must-not-defer #3's human-dependent manual sync. **Behavior change** (per this file's "Inclusion heuristic": a slice acceptable yesterday refused today ⇒ a changelog entry): a triage/adopt forward-sync miss was acceptable-yesterday (no gate caught it) and is refused-today (the new OSDG-1 gate FAILs the methodology suite + HALTs PCA-1). The rev-0 design's "no changelog / no VERSION bump" pre-decision was withdrawn after the first Critic (B2) proved its cited slice-035 precedent false on recompute — those build/commit drift tests were first added by slice-021 (commit `8823c53`) riding slice-021/BRANCH-1's *existing* v0.35.0 4-part bump (`methodology-changelog.md:474`), so it is no precedent for a no-bump member-addition. OSDG-1 is enforced by two scope-narrow per-file equality assertions `tests/methodology/test_triage_skill_drift.py` + `tests/methodology/test_adopt_skill_drift.py`, each a structural twin of `test_slice_skill_drift.py` reusing `tests/skill_drift_equality.py::assert_md_forward_synced` verbatim (zero new comparison logic; EOL-agnostic per EOL-DRIFT-1 / ADR-033 — CRLF↔LF is not drift). OSDG-1 is a plain (non-`-D`, non-`-T`) RULE-ID like its siblings CAD-1 / MCT-1 / QD-1; it **extends**, does not supersede, the CAD-1/mini-CAD/EOL-DRIFT-1 lineage (`ADR-051.supersedes: null`). PRESERVED unchanged: the `assert_md_forward_synced` comparator + its EOL-DRIFT-1 semantics, the existing six guard members, the INST-1 canonical inventories + `plugin.yaml` artifact enumeration (no new tool/skill/agent — only two `tests/` modules + an ADR; only the version moves), and the `.gitattributes skills/**/SKILL.md text eol=lf` pin (already covers both new openers — no gap). The meta-Critic (DR-1 EXTEND) added B-add-1 (the catalog is one-row-per-slice — exactly ONE row #49 covering both guards, not two) and M-add-1 (the CLAUDE.md `## Self-hosting discipline` Mini-CAD generalization lands in the section `test_root_claude_md_cad1_eol_agnostic.py` prose-pins, re-run immediately after that edit — slice-039 realign-the-pin-you-touch law). ## v0.56.0 — 2026-05-19

**SOAD-1 — Structured-Options-Ask Discipline: a new pipeline-wide rule requiring every skill that pauses for user input to ask via the `AskUserQuestion` structured-options tool with a recommended choice, never a bare free-text prompt (slice-048; ADR-050 generalizes ADR-048's gate-specific structured-options-ask requirement; mints a new rule; supersedes nothing).** ADR-048 (slice-046, v0.55.0) established — for the single BFRD-1 Step 3c confirm gate — that the prompt MUST be `AskUserQuestion` structured options because a free-text question does NOT surface a user notification. That reasoning is a Claude Code *platform* property (WebSearch-verified: anthropics/claude-code#13830, open feature request — no notification on free-text/bare prompts), not gate-specific: any pipeline skill pausing on a bare free-text question blocks silently and the user is never told the pipeline is waiting. SOAD-1 promotes the ADR-048 reasoning from one gate + a personal user memory (`ask-via-structured-options.md`) to a first-class pipeline discipline. **Behavior change**: the canonical SOAD-1 sentence is written verbatim into the 4 project-root-`CLAUDE.md` template blocks the openers emit (`/triage` Step 5b Fresh+Append; `/adopt` Step 10 Fresh+Append) and into this repo's own `CLAUDE.md` (self-hosting dogfood, RSAD-1). The escape-hatch clause names the genuinely notification-less case only — ADR-048's verbal-claim-with-path bare-prose fallback used where `AskUserQuestion` cannot model the input — NOT the tool's built-in free-text option (which is itself an options prompt that DOES notify; critique M2). Per the critique-review DR-1 missed-finding M-add-1 (user-ratified fix option (a)), the pre-existing bare free-text hard-rule ASK in the same template blocks is reworded to a structured-options ask so the shipped artifact does not self-violate the discipline it codifies (slice-022 self-violation law). SOAD-1 mandates structured options as the primary/default ask form, not an absolute ban. PRESERVED unchanged: the openers' template structure + size discipline, the hard-rule semantics (check active slice; if none, ask; wait), the one-way generalization (ADR-048 remains the authority for the BFRD-1 gate specifically; SOAD-1 is the pipeline-wide superset), INST-1 canonical inventories + `plugin.yaml` artifact enumeration (no new tool/skill/agent — only the version moves). Defect class: SOAD-1 silently dropped/reworded out of any opener template (every future `/triage`|`/adopt` project ships a CLAUDE.md without the discipline) or this repo's CLAUDE.md, or the slice's own self-violation (SOAD-1 shipped beside a bare free-text ASK) silently regressing, or the changelog entry/ADR-050 lineage lost. Validation method: `tests/methodology/test_soad1_structured_options_ask_rule.py` (section-scoped per-fenced-block CANON pin × 4 + repo CLAUDE.md pin + the M-add-1 self-consistency guards × 5; authored test-first with a genuine 10-FAIL → 10-PASS contrast), the v0.56.0 entry-pin `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_56_0_soad_1_entry_present_in_repo` + `::test_v_0_56_0_soad_1_shippability_consumer_propagation`, `architecture/shippability.md` row 48, and the PMI-1 4-part atomic version bump 0.55.0 → 0.56.0. Rule reference: SOAD-1 (slice-048; ADR-050 generalizes ADR-048's gate-specific decision; mints a new rule; supersedes nothing — naming-class peer of BFRD-1/RPCD-1/SCPD-1 discipline rules).

### Added

- **SOAD-1 — Structured-Options-Ask Discipline**
  Every pipeline skill that pauses for user input MUST present it as structured options (with a recommended choice) via the `AskUserQuestion` tool — never a bare free-text prompt; a bare prose ask is legitimate only where `AskUserQuestion` genuinely cannot model the input. Codified as one canonical sentence verbatim in the `/triage` Step 5b + `/adopt` Step 10 CLAUDE.md templates (Fresh+Append) + this repo's own `CLAUDE.md`.
  - **Rule reference**: SOAD-1 (slice-048; ADR-050 generalizes ADR-048; mints a new rule; supersedes nothing)
  - **Defect class**: a skill blocking on a bare free-text question that emits no Claude Code user notification — the pipeline stalls invisibly with the user unaware it is waiting
  - **Validation**: `tests/methodology/test_soad1_structured_options_ask_rule.py` + `test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_56_0_soad_1_entry_present_in_repo` / `::test_v_0_56_0_soad_1_shippability_consumer_propagation`; `architecture/shippability.md` row 48; PMI-1 version 0.55.0 → 0.56.0

## v0.55.0 — 2026-05-19

**BFRD-1 terminal-action reclassified: the Step 3c STOP-route changes from an unconditional user hand-off to a `conditional confirm-then-auto-invoke` `/repro` edge (slice-046; ADR-048 partial-supersedes ADR-018's STOP-route decision; refines BFRD-1's terminal action, mints no new rule; supersedes nothing else).** Before slice-046, when BFRD-1 detection fired and no failing repro test existed, `/slice` Step 3c emitted an unconditional STOP instructing the user to run `/repro` themselves and then re-invoke `/slice` — the single largest residual mechanical hand-off inside the per-slice loop, directly contradicting PCA-1 (v0.41.0) auto-advance philosophy and the 2026-05-19 user directive. The sole legitimate reason the gate was unconditional (ADR-018 Failure mode 2: an auto-generated test could reproduce the *wrong* failure signature) is fully mitigated by a lightweight confirm-the-distilled-description checkpoint, not by punting the sub-skill back to the user. **Behavior change**: Step 3c now (1) distills a one-line `/repro` description from the candidate source, (2) presents it via the `AskUserQuestion` structured-options tool (free-text questions do not surface a user notification — the confirm prompt MUST be structured) with Confirm / Modify / Not-a-bug-cancel, (3) on Confirm/Modify auto-invokes `/repro` itself via the Skill tool **exactly once** then re-runs the `shippability.md grep verification` (a still-missing `tests/bugs/*` row routes into the preserved verbal-claim-with-path fallback — bounded to one `/repro` attempt, no loop; DR-1 meta-Critic missed-finding m-add-1), (4) on "Not a bug — cancel" fail-closes (no auto-`/repro`, no silent proceed; preserves the ADR-018 Failure-mode-2 protection). PRESERVED unchanged: detection sub-modes (a)/(b), the `shippability.md grep verification` mechanism, the verbal-claim-with-path fallback, the one-surface `/slice` Step 3c placement, the one-way `/slice → /repro` coupling (`/repro` is not modified), and the BFRD-1 rule-ID. The `## Pipeline position` BFRD-1 user-input-gate is reworded from an unconditional route to a confirm-gate (HALT only at the confirm/modify prompt; on confirm, auto-invoke + continue); PCA-1 chain shape (`successor: /design-slice`, `auto-advance: true`) is unchanged and `pipeline_chain_audit` (which reads only the 4 required fields + the `**user-input gates**` literal, not the gate-list contents) stays clean. STP-1 Sub-form A (slice-044) stays clean by construction: all three pre-existing `tests/methodology/test_slice_skill.py` BFRD-1 positive-pin literals (`bug-fix repro prelude discipline`, `shippability.md grep verification`, the Step 3b/3c/4 anchors) are preserved verbatim — zero stale-pin realignment; the new content guard's positive pin's literal is the deliverable and its negative `not in` pin is an `ast.NotIn` node excluded from Sub-form A (ADR-047 §"Introduced residuals" B1). Defect class: a future slice silently reverting Step 3c to the PCA-1-violating unconditional hand-off, or auto-invoking `/repro` without the confirm checkpoint (re-opening ADR-018 Failure mode 2). Validation method: the new `tests/methodology/test_slice_skill.py::test_slice_skill_md_bfrd_1_conditional_confirm_then_auto_invoke_reclassified` content pin (positive canonical-phrase-present + negative old-hand-off-literal-absent, authored test-first with a genuine FAIL→PASS), the v0.55.0 entry-pin, `architecture/shippability.md` row 46, and the PMI-1 4-part atomic version bump 0.54.0 → 0.55.0. Rule reference: BFRD-1 (slice-046; ADR-048 partial-supersedes ADR-018 STOP-route decision; refines BFRD-1 terminal action, mints no new rule).

## v0.54.0 — 2026-05-18

**STP-1: a new audit-enforced pre-finish gate detects state-transition stale pins — a slice flips a risk's `**Status**:` OR repoints a `SKILL.md` prose anchor while a pre-existing test still pins the OLD value and was not realigned in the same fix block (slice-044; ADR-047; new minted rule, refines nothing, supersedes nothing).** This class recurred N≥3 and is structurally unreachable by the dual-Critic stack — caught historically only at the pre-finish full-suite (BC-PROJ-4) run, sometimes latent several slices (R-10 slice-038→040 ~5-slice latency; slice-041 R-4 stale `test_r_4_..._stays_mitigating`; slice-042 ADR-prose recurrence N+1); slice-041 AND slice-042 each explicitly nominated it a "Candidate Builder-plan-mode checklist item", and this repo's own law (slice-038 SRSC-1: "prose binds nothing executable; the invoked single-sourced runner does") mandates an executable gate. **STP-1 — State-Transition stale-Pin** (`tools/state_transition_pin_audit.py`): two mechanically-detectable git-diff-independent standing-invariant sub-forms (the fuzzy ADR `accepted`→`superseded` sub-form is explicitly out of scope per ADR-047, a `/reflect` Discovered follow-up). **Sub-form A — SKILL.md-prose-repoint**: a `tests/**/test_*skill*.py` positive-membership prose-pin whose folded-constant (`ast.Constant.value`, post-fold — handles implicit multi-line concatenation) literal is absent from the *full* target `SKILL.md` (binding-tracer resolves module-level + function-local `read_file(...)`→`.split(...)[i]` sliced-segment chains; presence checked against the full file, not the slice); excludes own-op `ast.NotIn`, any `ast.Or`-disjunction operand (an absent alternative is intentional — Task-1 self-verify refinement), and mixed `ast.And`-with-`NotIn`/non-constant-sibling, while a positive-only `ast.And` chain IS checked per-operand. **Sub-form B — risk-status-stale**: a test `FunctionDef` name matching `(?:^|_)r[_-]?(\d+).*?_(stays|remains|is)_(open|mitigating|retired|accepted)(?:_|$)` whose claimed status differs from the *live* `architecture/risk-register.md` `**Status**:`, parsed via the object-identity-reused `risk_register_audit._parse_risks` (CSP-1, slice-038 `consumer._fn is source._fn` lesson). Per-scanned-file AST `SyntaxError` ⇒ skip-with-visible-note, NO violation, NOT exit 2 (ADR-037/PTFFD-1 discipline inherited verbatim from the cited `shippability_path_audit.py`/`_pyfn` precedent — a false-FAIL on a parse failure is the strictly-worse audit failure mode); exit-2 fail-closed reserved for hard-input failure only (register missing/unparseable, `tests/`/`skills/` dir missing, repo-root unresolvable). Wired non-opt-out at `/build-slice` Step 6 pre-finish; slice-044 self-gating bootstrap (the authoring slice's own Step-6 run MUST exit 0 — verified: no live test contradicts the register, no removed-anchor prose-pin, the lone `tests/methodology/fixtures/syntax_error.py` skip-noted). **Deviation (slice-044 /build-slice plan-mode)**: Sub-form B was originally specified git-merge-base-baseline-diff; `architecture/` is fully gitignored (`.gitignore:11`) so that mechanism was inapplicable (`git show <base>:architecture/risk-register.md` fatal) — caught only at build-plan-mode (the dual-Critic+DR-1 stack probed git semantics but missed the gitignore root cause); user-approved re-spec to the git-diff-independent standing invariant (whole audit now git-free; moots first-Critic M1, dissolves the misconfigured-clone-HALT residual). Critique loop: TRI-1 CLEAN (8 round-1 + 3 DR-1 ACCEPTED-FIXED) → plan-mode deviation → targeted re-critique BLOCKED (B4/B5 syntax_error-fixture self-HALT + ADR-037-contradiction; M4/M5 unanchored-`r`/greedy-`\w+`; m3/m4 stale git prose) → DR-1 EXTEND (+m-add-R2-1 ADR-047 L27) all ACCEPTED-FIXED → Round-2 TRI-1 CLEAN. 4-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.53.0→0.54.0 (`VERSION` + `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + this file forward-synced to `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md`); `tools/state_transition_pin_audit.py` added → `plugin.yaml` + `tools/install_audit.py` enumerate it (PMI-1/INST-1).

### Added

- **STP-1 — State-Transition stale-Pin (audit-enforced pre-finish gate)**
  `tools/state_transition_pin_audit.py` detects the recurring N≥3 class where a slice performs a state transition (risk `**Status**:` flip or `SKILL.md` prose-anchor repoint) but a pre-existing test still pins the OLD value — two git-diff-independent standing-invariant sub-forms (Sub-form A SKILL.md-prose-repoint; Sub-form B risk-status-stale vs the live register via reused `risk_register_audit._parse_risks`). Per-file AST `SyntaxError` is skip-with-note (ADR-037/PTFFD-1), exit-2 reserved for hard-input failure. Wired non-opt-out at `/build-slice` Step 6.
  - **Rule reference**: STP-1 (slice-044; ADR-047; new minted NON-`-D` `vN.N` audit-gate rule — naming-class peer of BRANCH-1/BC-1/PMI-1/UTF8-STDOUT-1/CRP-1/PCA-1/BCI-1/MCFS-1; refines nothing, supersedes nothing)
  - **Defect class**: a state-transition slice leaving a stale pre-existing test pinning the OLD value (risk status / repointed SKILL.md anchor), undetected by the dual-Critic+DR-1 stack and latent until a future slice's BC-PROJ-4 full-suite run (the R-10 ~5-slice-latency / false-PCA-1-HALT class)
  - **Validation**: `tests/methodology/test_state_transition_pin_audit.py` (22 cases — Sub-form A module-level/sliced-segment/`not in`/`Or`-disjunction/`And`-conjunction/folded-multiline/f-string-skip/SyntaxError-skip-note; Sub-form B stale/realigned/claimed==live/suffixed/embedded-`r`/regex-mechanical-contrast; fail-closed register-missing→exit2; object-identity `_parse_risks` reuse; live-repo self-application→exit0) + `tests/methodology/test_stp1_r10_skill_prose_repoint_regression.py` (R-10 reconstruction, signature-asserting + slice-043 FAIL→PASS contrast; catalogued shippability #44) + `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_54_0_stp_1_entry_present_in_repo` + `::test_v_0_54_0_stp_1_shippability_consumer_propagation` + PMI-1/INST-1/CAD-1/BCI-1/MCFS-1/PCA-1/BRANCH-1/UTF8-STDOUT-1 audits green

## v0.52.0 — 2026-05-18

**APED-1 + MEPD-1: the two user-ACCEPTED 2026-05-17 `/critic-calibrate` proposals are applied to `agents/critique.md` — APED-1 adds a Dim 9 sub-clause #12 requiring the Critic to Bash-execute a changed audit parse-rule against an adversarial battery; MEPD-1 adds a Dim 7 checklist sub-bullet for the methodology-surface RULE-ID + entry-pin obligation (slice-039; ADR-040 + ADR-041).** The 2026-05-17 (post-slice-034) calibration run generated two proposals, both ACCEPTED by the user "to apply manually"; they sat unapplied through slices 035–038 (the slice-036 reflection explicitly flagged "the proposals must actually be applied to agents/critique.md"). **APED-1 — Audit-Parse-rule Empirical-execution Discipline** (Dim 9 #12): when a slice changes an audit's own parse rule (regex / field-line matcher / token splitter / status acceptor / `;`-segment splitter / AST-shape predicate), the Critic MUST **Bash-execute a changed audit parse-rule against an adversarial battery** (own-brief field-lines / trailing-annotation / substring-collision / empty-absent / CRLF), file a **Blocker** on any silent-disable / default-off / false-FAIL / over-match, and state the battery was **executed, not reasoned** — closing the N=3-first-Critic-MISS class (slice-030A R-5 CRLF + slice-031 R-6/R-7 + slice-033 R-7 N+1) that slice-034 M1 proved Critic-reachable when *executed* rather than reasoned (the N=1 CATCH). **MEPD-1 — Methodology-surface Entry-Pin Discipline** (Dim 7 sub-bullet, run SEPARATELY from the deep-dive): a behaviour-changing in-house-methodology slice must discharge the changelog/RULE-ID obligation along EXACTLY ONE of (a) minted RULE-ID + `test_v_0_NN_0_<rule>_entry_present_in_repo_and_installed` entry-pin + atomic 4-part PMI-1 bump, OR (b) a documented why-none **verified against the actual `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py` enforcing assertion** (never a Builder-asserted prior-slice precedent — the slice-032 m1 false-precedent dual-Critic rubber-stamp); neither branch silently present is a Major (the slice-032 DEVIATION-1 + slice-034 M-add-1 class). **Both are NEW minted `-D`-suffix rule-IDs that refine nothing and supersede nothing** (ADR-040); the `-D` suffix denotes the behavioral class "/critique-time prose-heuristic discipline", NOT Dim-9 membership — **MEPD-1 is the first deliberately non-Dim-9 `-D` rule** (homed in Dim 7), a sound extension of ADR-019/ADR-023 (no prior ADR ever stated `-D ⊆ Dim 9`). Both ship under ONE version with two `Rule reference`s + two entry-pins (ADR-041 — one calibration run, one user acceptance, one atomic Critic-prompt edit cycle; splitting into two versions would imply a non-existent ordering). The structural-invariant count test `test_critique_dim_9_lists_eleven_sub_clauses` is superseded → `test_critique_dim_9_lists_twelve_sub_clauses` (PMI-1 structural-invariant supersession N=7 stable); the 5 PTFCD-1/PTFFD-1 sibling tests' `end_anchor` is tightened `### Bonus: weak graph edges` → `Audit-parse-rule empirical-execution discipline` (RPCD-1 sibling-scoping, slice-013/015/024 precedent); SCPD-1 propagation renames the 14 LIVE `::test_critique_dim_9_lists_eleven_sub_clauses` selector tokens across shippability rows 6/11/13/15/16/24/25 while the **frozen line-34 slice-025 `_lists_ten → _lists_eleven` historical-narrative occurrence is deliberately preserved** (the DR-1 meta-Critic M-add-1 catch: a blanket `not in catalog` pin would guaranteed-false-FAIL on the legitimately-preserved string; the selector-prefix discriminator scopes the rename — same frozen-history class as the changelog:266 v0.39.0 occurrence, both enumerated-and-excluded). Per the slice-022 self-violation law the codifying slice itself satisfies MEPD-1 (its own APED-1/MEPD-1 RULE-IDs + `test_v_0_52_0_*` entry-pins + this 4-part bump) and the M1/m1/m2 anti-tautology gaps were caught and content-pinned (the APED-1/MEPD-1 pins assert the behavioral verbs, not CAD-1 byte-equality — the slice-037 M-add-1 tautology law). 4-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.51.0→0.52.0 (`VERSION` + `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + this file forward-synced to `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md`); no tool/skill/agent added or removed (prose + test + changelog only) → PMI-1/INST-1 version-lockstep only; -D-suffix calibration trail N=9 → N=11 (… + PTFCD-1 + PTFFD-1 + APED-1 + MEPD-1).

## v0.51.0 — 2026-05-17

**SRSC-1: the `/validate-slice` Step-5.5 shippability-catalog runner is pinned behind an invoked `tools/shippability_runner.py` that reuses SCMD-1 `_segments()` — Step 5.5 INVOKES it; an ad-hoc hand-rolled execution loop is forbidden (slice-038; ADR-039).** SCMD-1 (slice-031 / ADR-031) defines a `Machine-cmd` cell as one or more individually backtick-wrapped ` ; `-separated segments; correct execution requires splitting on `;` **then stripping backticks + whitespace PER segment**. The Step-5.5 loop was hand-rolled per slice from `skills/validate-slice/SKILL.md` prose, and a naive runner that strips only the OUTER fence of the whole cell mangles segment 2 of the lone multi-segment row (#28) into a leading-backtick `argv[0]` → `WinError 2` — a false-FAIL of the slice's own dogfood row that recurred N=2 (slice-032 secondary discovery; slice-033 LIVE at /validate) **despite SKILL.md already saying "deterministically `;`-split + interpreter-anchored"**. R-8's root cause: prose binds nothing executable. SRSC-1 closes it by making the contract an invoked tool, not a description — **do NOT hand-roll the execution loop**; `/validate-slice` Step 5.5 item 4 runs `$PY -m tools.shippability_runner architecture/shippability.md`. The runner **reuses SCMD-1 _segments()** (imports `_segments` / `_catalog_rows` / `_machine_cmd_cell` from `tools.shippability_decoupling_audit` — the same CSP-1 cross-`tools` private-reuse pattern `shippability_decoupling_audit` itself uses to consume `shippability_path_audit`); it does NOT re-derive the split/strip, so the contract is single-sourced and a future change to the split-strip is a single-point edit consumed by both the SCMD-1 grammar audit and the SRSC-1 runner. The runner trusts (does NOT re-validate) SCMD-1's grammar: Step 5.5 item 3a runs the SCMD-1 pre-catalog gate as a hard STOP before the runner, and duplicating grammar validation in the runner would itself be the CSP-1 divergence this slice avoids. **SRSC-1 is a NEW non-`-D` audit/runner-gate-class RULE-ID with a `vN.N` version (peers BRANCH-1 / BC-1 / PMI-1 / CRP-1 / PCA-1 / BCI-1); it does NOT refine SCMD-1 and supersedes nothing — SCMD-1 owns catalog *grammar validation*, SRSC-1 owns catalog *execution*; distinct surfaces, distinct failure modes, distinct consumers (Step 5.5 item 3a gate vs item 4 invocation), shared only the `_segments()` artifact (a CSP-1 reuse relationship, not a rule-identity refinement — treating it as an in-place SCMD-1 refinement would be the wrong precedent: TFFL-1↔TF-1 was a same-surface scope extension, this is a new surface).** Per the slice-022 self-violation law, the slice pinning the runner nearly committed the defect class its own design documented: `tools/shippability_runner.py` has a `main()`, so it is auto-discovered by `tests/methodology/test_utf8_stdout_regression.py::_discovered_audit_tools()` into the `discovered_set == covered_set` parity assertion — which IS shippability row #28 segment 1, the exact row this slice fixes and dogfoods — caught at /critique B1 (the Builder's original recursion note had explicitly waved it off); discharged by a bespoke `test_shippability_runner_survives_cp1252_with_u2192` feeding covered_set via the ADR-026 mechanism-(ii) literal (sibling precedent of `shippability_path_audit` / `shippability_decoupling_audit` — a catalog-path tool is NOT in `_POSITIONAL_SLICE_TOOLS`, which passes a slice-folder arg; the B1 applied-fix's `_POSITIONAL_SLICE_TOOLS` instruction was corrected at build to the sibling precedent, B1 intent preserved). 4-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.50.0→0.51.0 (`VERSION` + `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + this file forward-synced to `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md`); `tools/shippability_runner.py` enumerated on BOTH non-identical surfaces (`tools/install_audit.py` `_CANONICAL_TOOLS` + `plugin.yaml` tools list); R-8 retired (vault bookkeeping; slice-038 + ADR-039 cite).

### Added

- **SRSC-1 — Shippability Runner Segment Contract**
  The `/validate-slice` Step-5.5 catalog runner is pinned behind an invoked `tools/shippability_runner.py` that **reuses SCMD-1 `_segments()`** (per-`;`-segment backtick+whitespace strip, single-sourced from `tools.shippability_decoupling_audit`). Step 5.5 INVOKES the runner; **do NOT hand-roll the execution loop** or re-derive the split/strip in prose or an ad-hoc loop. Closes R-8 (naive outer-fence-only strip false-FAILs the lone multi-segment row #28 → `WinError 2`, recurred N=2 despite prior prose).
  - **Rule reference**: SRSC-1 (slice-038; ADR-039; new non-`-D` `vN.N` audit/runner-gate-class RULE-ID, does NOT refine SCMD-1, supersedes nothing)
  - **Defect class**: a hand-rolled per-slice Step-5.5 runner that strips only the outer markdown fence of a whole `Machine-cmd` cell mangles segment 2 of a multi-segment row into a leading-backtick `argv[0]` → `WinError 2` false-FAIL (the false-PCA-1-HALT class adjacent to R-4/R-5; recurs whenever the runner is re-derived ad-hoc)
  - **Validation**: `tests/methodology/test_shippability_runner_segment_contract.py` (the load-bearing naive-outer-strip-vs-`_segments()` contrast on real row #28) + `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_51_0_srsc_1_entry_present_in_repo_and_installed` + `::test_v_0_51_0_srsc_1_shippability_consumer_propagation` (catalogued shippability #38) + `tests/methodology/test_utf8_stdout_regression.py::test_shippability_runner_survives_cp1252_with_u2192` + the SRSC-1 dogfood (the runner runs the full real catalog at this slice's own /validate-slice Step 5.5) + PMI-1 / INST-1 / SCMD-1 / CAD-1 audits green

## v0.50.0 — 2026-05-17

**PTFFD-1: the cited test FUNCTION (not only the FILE) must exist in an otherwise-present test file; a PASSING TF-1 plan row or a shippability `Machine-cmd` pytest `::`-selector whose file exists but whose cited function is absent is a `missing-test-function` violation (slice-037; ADR-037 + ADR-038).** FILE-level PTFCD-1 (slice-025) closed phantom test-*file* citation but both enforcing audits discarded the pytest selector (`tools/test_first_audit.py` + `tools/shippability_path_audit.py` both `split("::", 1)[0]`), so a citation of an existing file with a non-existent test *function* passed silently — the N=3-distinct-slice Critic-stack blind spot empirically demonstrated at slice-025 AC3 + slice-026 AC5 + slice-027 B1 (each missed by BOTH Critic layers, caught only by a downstream gate). PTFFD-1 extends both audits to the function level via a shared `tools/_pyfn.py` helper (tri-state `function_defined_in_file`: True / False / None). The load-bearing disciplines: (1) the function name resolves **function-column-first then `Test path` / selector `::`-tail fallback** (M3 — closes the `path::fn`-in-path-column + empty-function-column escape the `_resolve_test_path` `::`-strip would otherwise open); when both carry checkable-but-different names the function column wins (it is the authoritative TF-1 field — the conservative choice); (2) a non-identifier / prose `Test function` value (e.g. slice-034's real `(full existing module — non-regression)` row) is NOT a checkable name and **degrades to FILE-level-only** rather than false-positively flagging — `the cited test FUNCTION (not only the FILE) must exist in an otherwise-present test file` is the canonical discipline, and the strict-identifier discriminator is its zero-false-positive linchpin (verified clean against the live `architecture/shippability.md`, 36 rows / 228 tokens, and the full archived prose corpus); (3) an unparseable / non-Python / unreadable cited file is **skip-with-note** (ADR-037): NO violation, but the skip is rendered, not silent — a false-FAIL on a parse failure would halt the pipeline gate, the strictly worse audit failure mode (Option 3 silent-skip genuinely rejected: the human formatter emits `function-check skipped (file unparseable)` and `--json` carries an additive `skip_notes` key). Per the slice-022 self-violation law, the slice codifying function-level phantom detection committed exactly that defect class in its own AC4 TF-1 row (a phantom `test_critique_agent_in_repo_equals_installed`) — caught at /critique B1 and at the meta-Critic's M-add-1 (AC4 was a tautological green: the cited CAD-1 byte-equality test passes even if the Dim 9 refinement is absent; closed with content-pin tests). **PTFFD-1 is a NEW minted `-D`-suffix rule-ID that refines PTFCD-1 in place — rule-ID lineage preserved, supersedes nothing — mirroring the TFFL-1↔TF-1 (slice-034) / EOL-DRIFT-1↔CAD-1 in-place-mint precedent; NOT a `PTFCD-1 v1.1` version label (the `vN.N` label is reserved for the NON-`-D` audit-gate naming class — B3 / ADR-038).** `tools/_pyfn.py` is a leading-underscore helper with no `main()` → structurally excluded from UTF8-STDOUT-1 / INST-1 / PMI-1 `_CANONICAL_TOOLS` (mirrors `tools/_stdout.py`); no tool added/removed. 4-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.49.0→0.50.0 (`VERSION` + `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + this file forward-synced to `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md`); -D-suffix calibration trail N=8 → N=9 (… + FBCD-1 + PTFCD-1 + PTFFD-1).

### Added

- **PTFFD-1 — Phantom-Test-Function-citation Discipline**
  A PASSING TF-1 plan row, or a shippability `Machine-cmd` pytest `::`-selector, whose cited test FILE exists on disk but whose cited test FUNCTION (`def`/`async def` at any nesting depth — function-column-first, then `::`-tail fallback, `[param-id]` stripped) is absent from that file, is a `missing-test-function` violation (`tools/test_first_audit.py --strict-pre-finish` + `tools/shippability_path_audit.py`). A non-identifier / prose value degrades to FILE-level-only (zero false positives on the real corpus); an unparseable cited file is skip-with-note (ADR-037) — NO violation, rendered not silent. Refines PTFCD-1 in place; supersedes nothing.
  - **Rule reference**: PTFFD-1 (slice-037; ADR-037 + ADR-038; new minted `-D` rule, refines PTFCD-1 in place, supersedes nothing)
  - **Defect class**: a citation of an existing test FILE with a non-existent test FUNCTION — the function-level phantom-citation class missed by BOTH Critic layers at slice-025 AC3 / slice-026 AC5 / slice-027 B1, reachable only at the audit-vs-its-own-artifact interaction
  - **Validation**: `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_50_0_ptffd_1_entry_present_in_repo_and_installed` + `::test_v_0_50_0_ptffd_1_shippability_consumer_propagation` (catalogued shippability #37) + `tests/methodology/test_ptffd1_test_first_audit.py` + `tests/methodology/test_ptffd1_shippability_path_audit.py` + `tests/methodology/test_ptffd1_no_false_positive.py` + `tests/methodology/test_critique_agent.py::test_critique_dim_9_phantom_citation_function_level_layer_present` + `::test_critique_dim_9_phantom_citation_names_ptffd_1_rule_id` + CAD-1 / PMI-1 / INST-1 audits green

## v0.49.0 — 2026-05-17

**SRCD-1: a user-invocable skill name MUST NOT collide with a Claude Code built-in command name; collisions are resolved by a clean rename with no backward-compat alias, propagated atomically across the manifest, install canon, tests, vault and cross-docs (slice-035; ADR-035).** The AI SDLC pipeline shipped a skill `name: status` whose trigger `/status` shadowed the Claude Code built-in `/status` command, making the project-pulse skill ambiguously invocable for every user. The skill was renamed to `pulse` (its own pre-existing conceptual name — `skills/pulse/SKILL.md` titles itself "Project Pulse / Macro State" and `pulse` was already a declared trigger), the colliding `'/status'` + `'project status'` triggers dropped. The rename crossed PMI-1 (plugin manifest), INST-1 (install canon), CSP-1 (cross-spec parity) and the methodology test suite; the load-bearing discipline is that **a skill-rename is not a string-replace — executable filesystem-path binds (`(REPO_ROOT/"skills"/"<name>"/...).read_text()`, COST-1 path-list constants, test-module filenames + `test_<name>_*` function names) hard-fail the suite on a directory-only rename and must be enumerated separately from prose `/<name>` references**; the slice-035 design taxonomy (Bucket A executable binds / Bucket B prose / Bucket C frozen-or-unrelated) plus a mandated post-edit inventory grep (Bucket A must be empty) is the structural backstop, per the slice-022 self-violation law (a methodology slice about completing a rename inventory will itself ship an incomplete inventory unless a real-artifact grep, not Critic reasoning, is the primary catch — landed: the first Critic + meta-Critic together surfaced 14 findings, 5 of them executable-bind / installed-side misclassifications). No backward-compat `/status` alias is created — an alias would re-introduce the exact collision being resolved. Frozen-as-history exclusions (NOT rewritten — rewriting falsifies a historical record): the ADR-030 verbatim backtest corpus and `architecture/lessons-learned.md:414`. `agents/critique.md` is NOT touched — it has no `/status` *skill* reference (its only `/status` substring is inside the FBCD-1 list "NEW symbol/status/anchor"), so CAD-1 is N/A this slice. **SRCD-1 is a NEW minted rule-ID; it supersedes nothing.** 4-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.48.0→0.49.0 (`VERSION` + `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + this file forward-synced to `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md`); no tool added/removed, one skill renamed in-place → PMI-1/INST-1/CSP-1 lockstep only.

### Added

- **SRCD-1 — Skill-Rename Collision-resolution Discipline**
  A user-invocable skill name MUST NOT collide with a Claude Code built-in command name. Resolve a collision by a clean rename (no backward-compat alias — an alias re-introduces the collision), propagated atomically across `plugin.yaml`, `tools/install_audit.py` canonical list, the referencing test modules (executable path binds + prose, classified per the Bucket A/B/C taxonomy), vault docs and cross-docs, with a `methodology-changelog.md` entry + 4-part PMI-1 atomic bump and a forward-synced installed copy. A post-edit inventory grep MUST show zero surviving old-name executable binds.
  - **Rule reference**: SRCD-1 (slice-035; ADR-035; new minted rule, supersedes nothing)
  - **Defect class**: a user-invocable skill name silently shadowed by a Claude Code built-in command (ambiguous invocation); and the secondary class — a skill rename propagated as a prose string-replace that misses executable filesystem-path binds, hard-failing the methodology suite at pre-finish
  - **Validation**: `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_49_0_srcd_1_entry_present_in_repo_and_installed` (catalogued shippability #35) + `tests/methodology/test_pulse_cadence_enforcement.py` + `tests/methodology/test_skill_model_dispatch.py` + `tests/methodology/test_risk_register_audit.py::test_pulse_skill_references_rr_1` + PMI-1 / INST-1 / CSP-1 audits green + post-edit inventory grep (Bucket A empty)

## v0.48.0 — 2026-05-17

**TFFL-1: the TF-1 audit field-line value must be a standalone boolean token; a present-but-unparseable field is a loud violation, never a silent default-off (slice-034; ADR-034).** `tools/test_first_audit.py`'s `_TEST_FIRST_FIELD_RE` was `$`-anchored (`^\*\*Test[-\s]?first\*\*\s*:\s*(true|false)\s*$`). The idiomatic `/slice` mission-brief template form `**Test-first**: true  (per TF-1 — …)` failed the `\s*$` anchor → `_detect_test_first_flag` returned False → `run_audit` took the silent default-off path → `main` exited 0 reporting "not enabled" on a genuinely test-first slice, **silently bypassing the entire TF-1 gate**. Recorded firsthand in risk-register **R-7** (open; reproduced LIVE N+1 at slice-033 pre-finish, caught only by the BC-PROJ-4 real-artifact gate run — both the first-Critic and the DR-1 meta-Critic missed it at slice-033, the dual-Critic stack structurally cannot reach the audit-parsing-the-artifact interaction). Fix (ADR-034, Option 3): the **`**Test-first** field-line value must be a standalone boolean token`** — the captured `true|false` must be immediately followed by whitespace, an opening `(` (the idiomatic annotation), or end-of-line: `^\*\*Test[-\s]?first\*\*\s*:\s*(true|false)(?=[\s(]|$)`. The earlier candidate `(true|false)\b.*$` was rejected at /critique M1: `\b` matches between `false` and `-`, so `false-positive`→`false` / `true.`→`true` / `false; note`→`false` would be silently accepted — re-introducing a narrower R-7 silent-bypass through the fix's own regex (slice-022 self-violation law ~N≈11). Independently, a value-agnostic field-present matcher drives a new loud `malformed-test-first-field` violation (severity Important) when the `**Test-first**:` prefix is present but no line yields a valid standalone boolean (empty value, `maybe`, `false-positive`, `true.`); `_format_human`'s silent "not enabled" message is guarded to fire ONLY on genuine field absence. The malformed branch consults the SAME `(true|false)` matcher (capturing BOTH booleans), so a legitimately-disabled `**Test-first**: false` brief stays a clean default-off, NOT a malformed violation (/critique M2 load-bearing invariant). `_detect_test_first_flag` retains its `bool` contract; CLI surface (argparse/flags/JSON schema) unchanged — only the equivalence relation and the absent-vs-malformed distinction change. **TFFL-1 is a NEW minted rule-ID that refines TF-1 (slice-002) in place — rule-ID lineage preserved, supersedes nothing** — mirroring the EOL-DRIFT-1↔CAD-1 / PMI-1 v1.0→v1.1 in-place-refinement precedent. The catalogued failing repro (`tests/methodology/test_tf1_field_line_annotation_regression.py`, shippability #34) is the durable regression guard; R-7 escalated to `retired`. 4-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.47.0→0.48.0 (`VERSION` + `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + this file forward-synced to `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md`); no tool added/removed → INST-1 lockstep only.

### Added

- **TFFL-1 — TF-1 Field-Line robustness**
  The `**Test-first**` field-line value must be a standalone boolean token (`true`|`false` followed by whitespace, `(`, or EOL); a present-but-unparseable field emits a loud `malformed-test-first-field` violation instead of silently defaulting off.
  - **Rule reference**: TFFL-1 (refines TF-1 in place; supersedes nothing; lineage like PMI-1 v1.0→v1.1 / EOL-DRIFT-1↔CAD-1)
  - **Defect class**: silent TF-1 gate bypass on an annotated or malformed `**Test-first**` field-line (R-7)
  - **Validation**: `tests/methodology/test_tf1_field_line_annotation_regression.py` (catalogued shippability #34) + `tests/methodology/test_test_first_audit.py` AC2/AC3 cases + `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_48_0_tffl_1_entry_present_in_repo_and_installed`

## v0.47.0 — 2026-05-17

**EOL-DRIFT-1: the `.md` forward-sync drift guards (5 skill-drift tests + CAD-1) compare content modulo line endings, not raw bytes (slice-033; ADR-033).** The skill-drift / mini-CAD / CAD-1 family asserted in-repo↔installed `.md` equality by raw `read_bytes()` sha256. On Windows `core.autocrlf=true` the working tree is CRLF while the installed copy is LF — the git blob is already LF, so content is genuinely identical, but the raw-byte hash differs → spurious FAIL. Recorded firsthand N+2 in risk-register R-5 (slices 030A/031/032), each a false PCA-1 HALT on a slice that never touched the guarded file; slice-030A additionally showed the failure can be *masked* by a coincidental `cp`. The defect class these guards exist to catch is **stale runtime prose** (a forgotten forward-sync after an in-repo content edit); CRLF↔LF is semantically invisible to it. A shared `tests/skill_drift_equality.py::assert_md_forward_synced` now normalizes CRLF→LF before hashing and is the sole comparator for all five skill-drift modules; `tools/critique_agent_drift_audit.py::_sha256_of` (CAD-1) gets the same one-line normalization (exit-code contract 0/1/2 unchanged — only the equivalence relation becomes EOL-insensitive; the pre-existing `test_drift_detection_fires_on_artificial_byte_flip` + CLI exit-code-matrix tests are the bound must-not-mask-real-drift proof). A repo-root `.gitattributes` pins the guarded surface (`skills/**/SKILL.md`, `skills/diagnose/passes/*.md`, `agents/*.md`) `text eol=lf`, with a targeted `git add --renormalize` + re-checkout scoped to those files only (whole-vault renormalization explicitly out of scope; git blobs already LF → empty index diff). The relaxation is **content-equal modulo line endings**, bounded by a mandatory regression test (`test_skill_drift_normalization.py`) asserting genuine non-EOL divergence still FAILs every patched guard. The invariant is restated (NOT superseded — CAD-1/mini-CAD rule-IDs preserved, lineage like PMI-1 v1.0→v1.1 / UTF8-STDOUT-1 v1.1 which both also shipped a changelog entry + version bump); CLAUDE.md "Self-hosting discipline" L33/L36 wording corrected in-slice from "MUST be byte-equal" to the precise invariant (NOT deferred to `/reflect` — slice-022 self-violation avoidance). 4-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.46.0→0.47.0 (`VERSION` + `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + this file forward-synced to `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md`).

### Changed

- **EOL-DRIFT-1 — `.md` forward-sync drift guards are EOL-agnostic**
  The skill-drift / mini-CAD / CAD-1 `.md` byte-equality guards now compare **content-equal modulo line endings** (CRLF→LF normalized before hashing) instead of raw bytes. A CRLF working tree vs an LF installed copy with identical content is CLEAN; genuine (non-EOL) content divergence still FAILs. Shared comparator `tests/skill_drift_equality.py`; CAD-1 `_sha256_of` normalized; `.gitattributes` + targeted renormalize pins the guarded surface to LF.
  - **Rule reference**: EOL-DRIFT-1 (slice-033; ADR-033). Supersedes nothing — refines the CAD-1 (slice-007) / mini-CAD (slice-010/019/021/032) guard family in place; rule-ID lineage preserved.
  - **Defect class**: R-5 — Windows `core.autocrlf=true` checks out the guarded `.md` CRLF while the installed copy is LF; the raw-byte comparator false-FAILed on byte-identical content (N+2 false PCA-1 HALTs at slices 030A/031/032), and the failure could be masked by a coincidental `cp` (false confidence in the CAD-1/mini-CAD guard).
  - **Validation**: `pytest tests/skill_drift_equality.py tests/methodology/test_skill_drift_normalization.py tests/methodology/test_critique_agent_drift.py tests/skills/diagnose/test_diagnose_skill_drift.py tests/methodology/test_slice_skill_drift.py tests/methodology/test_build_slice_skill_drift.py tests/methodology/test_commit_slice_skill_drift.py tests/methodology/test_query_design_skill_drift.py tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py tests/methodology/test_root_claude_md_cad1_eol_agnostic.py -q` — EOL-only equality + genuine-divergence-still-FAILs + guarded-surface-no-CRLF working-tree-state + existing CAD-1 drift-detection still exit 1 + `test_v_0_47_0_eol_drift_1_entry_present_in_repo_and_installed` (this entry in both changelog surfaces with `EOL-DRIFT-1` + canonical phrase `content-equal modulo line endings` + `ADR-033`) + CLAUDE.md L33/L36 EOL-agnostic substring pin; PMI-1 atomic-bump audit green.

## v0.46.0 — 2026-05-17

**QD-1: new `/query-design` skill — read-only, delegation-only codebase Q&A (slice-032; ADR-032).** Adds an out-of-loop, user-invoked pipeline skill that holds a truthful, grounded conversation about the EXISTING codebase and changes nothing. It fills a niche no skill covered: `/discover` is greenfield + vault-writing, `/diagnose` is a heavyweight non-conversational HTML deliverable, `/status` is a one-shot pulse, `/slice-candidates` needs the diagnose round-trip. `/query-design` answers strictly from repo evidence read in-session (Read / Grep / graphify — never ungrounded recall, every substantive answer cites `path:line`/symbol/ADR/rule-ID), and on a surfaced requirement/defect OFFERS a declinable `/slice` handoff — it never authors a slice/candidate/backlog file and never auto-invokes. Per ADR-032 (reversibility=cheap) it is out-of-loop: absent from `tools/pipeline_chain_audit.py:_CANONICAL_CHAIN`, carries NO `## Pipeline position` block, never auto-advances (peer to `/status`, `/diagnose`, `/reduce`, `/drift-check`). Read-only is enforced by SKILL.md prose (no `allowed-tools` — verified zero in-repo skills use it; skills run inline in the main thread, not as restricted plugin subagents) + this QD-1 rule + a per-file drift test + the canonical-phrase pin. 4-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.45.0→0.46.0 (`VERSION` + `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` + `plugin.yaml.version` + this file forward-synced to `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md`); INST-1 `_CANONICAL_SKILLS` + `plugin.yaml` lockstep (25 skills). The canonical invariant phrase **read-only, delegation-only codebase Q&A** is pinned verbatim at two independently-tested sites (this changelog entry body via `test_v_0_46_0_qd_1_entry_present_in_repo_and_installed`, and `skills/query-design/SKILL.md` via `test_query_design_skill.py`) so a future edit cannot silently erode QD-1 to a non-read-only rule.

### Added

- **QD-1 — query-design read-only codebase Q&A skill**
  Adds `skills/query-design/SKILL.md` (+ forward-synced installed copy). The skill is **read-only, delegation-only codebase Q&A**: MUST NOT Write/Edit/NotebookEdit any source/vault/test/candidate file, MUST ground every answer in in-session repo reads with concrete citations, and MUST only ever invoke `/slice` once on explicit user acceptance (the single permitted write-path) — otherwise it ends with zero side effects. Out-of-loop per ADR-032: no `## Pipeline position` block, never auto-advances.
  - **Rule reference**: QD-1 (slice-032; ADR-032)
  - **Defect class**: ad-hoc codebase questions were either answered from ungrounded recall (no repo evidence, no citations — silent inaccuracy) or forced a full `/diagnose` HTML round-trip. No skill offered a lightweight, truthful, change-nothing conversation that could optionally hand a discovered requirement to `/slice` without itself mutating the repo.
  - **Validation**: `pytest tests/methodology/test_query_design_skill_drift.py tests/methodology/test_query_design_skill.py tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py -q` — in-repo↔installed SKILL.md byte-equality; SKILL.md prose-contract pin (grounding + delegation + 3 error-model clauses + `_QD1_PHRASE` present); `test_v_0_46_0_qd_1_entry_present_in_repo_and_installed` (this entry present in both changelog surfaces with `QD-1` + canonical phrase + `ADR-032`); PMI-1 + INST-1 lockstep audits green.

## v0.45.0 — 2026-05-17

**SCMD-1: the shippability catalog gains a machine-stable command column + a runtime incidental-decoupling invariant (slice-031, split-lineage label "030B"; ADR-030 + ADR-031).** slice-030A retired R-4's *substance* (silent BC-1 degradation) but deliberately deferred the catalog-row environment-fragility to a follow-up. /critique BLOCKED v1 + meta-Critic M-add-1 established the residual is **two categorically distinct classes**: (a) *incidental* coupling — catalog-cited tests reading the gitignored `architecture/slices/archive/**`, gitignored `architecture/build-checks.md`, or untracked `~/.claude/build-checks.md` only because they were never repointed (slice-030A's BCI-1 gate guarantees a tracked canonical fixture is byte-faithful, so decoupling is semantics-preserving); (b) *essential* coupling — the ~20 `test_v_0_NN_0_*_entry_present_in_repo_and_installed` fns whose read of `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` IS the in-repo↔installed forward-sync assertion (no BCI-1 analogue). Per the user-ratified TRI-1 **b-split**, this slice does the incidental class ONLY; the essential reframe is chartered to slice-030C via a risk-register R-4 sub-entry (R-4 stays `mitigating`; escalates to `retired` only when 030C ships). This entry adds `tools/shippability_decoupling_audit.py` (rule **SCMD-1**): (a) every catalog row carries a prose-free `Machine-cmd` 6th column — one or more `;`-separated, interpreter-anchored pytest invocations (the leading anchor rejects a narrative `Commands:` prose cell — the rows #28/#29 D-2 footgun, slice-024/029 class N+1; the `;`-separation preserves row #28's two distinct surfaces without a lossy single-`pytest` `-k` merge); (b) the cited-fn set is mechanically re-derived from EVERY row's `Machine-cmd` cell (not a hand-coded subset — the slice-030 v1/v2 + critique-v1-B1 enumeration-relocation defect) and each resolved fn is AST-classified incidental/essential/clean with closed-world completeness via module-constant + same-module-helper resolution (M1) and real-vault root-anchoring (a synthetic `tmp_path` tree is not a vault read). The 11 incidental backtests are decoupled to the slice-030A canonical fixtures + a git-tracked verbatim archived-slice mini-corpus whose membership IS the runtime derivation (ADR-030; not a hand list — incl. slice-001/002); `~/.claude/build-checks.md` `.exists()` skip-guards collapsed to hard-asserts (M4 non-vacuous). `tools/shippability_path_audit.py` (PTFCD-1) + `/validate-slice` Step 5.5 runner repointed to the `Machine-cmd` column (a missing column is a SCMD-1 violation, never a silent skip that would also disable PTFCD-1 — B3).

### Added

  New audit-enforced gate (NON-`-D` per ADR-019; naming-class peers BRANCH-1 / BC-1 / PMI-1 / UTF8-STDOUT-1 / CRP-1 / PCA-1 / BCI-1). Programmatic gate: `tools/shippability_decoupling_audit.py`. The shared artifact with `shippability_path_audit` is only the token-extraction predicate; the prose-discriminator (interpreter-anchored full-cell segment validator) and the AST classifier are net-new (v2-m2/M-add-A).
  - **Rule reference**: SCMD-1 (slice-031-complete-shippability-decoupling, split-label 030B; ADR-030 + ADR-031). Supersedes nothing. The *essential*-class entry-pin reframe + M-add-1 are chartered to slice-030C (R-4 sub-entry).

## v0.44.0 — 2026-05-16

**BCI-1: build-checks vault integrity is enforced by a deterministic, non-opt-out downstream gate against git-tracked canonical fixtures (slice-030A; ADR-028 + ADR-029).** R-4 witnessed both `architecture/build-checks.md` (gitignored) and `~/.claude/build-checks.md` (untracked) silently truncated to only the last-promoted rule, degrading BC-1 pre-finish coverage with no loud signal. The corruptor — `/reflect` Step 5b promotion — is LLM-executed prose with **no deterministic promotion function** (slice-030A B2 / ADR-029): there is no source defect to fix; the only sound control for a non-deterministic step is a deterministic gate that detects the invariant violation downstream. This entry adds `tools/build_checks_integrity.py` (rule **BCI-1**): it parses the new **git-tracked** canonical fixtures (`tests/methodology/fixtures/build_checks/canonical_{project,global}_checks.md`) via `tools.build_checks_audit._parse_rules` read-only (NO BC-1 anchor/applicability semantics change) and asserts the live build-checks files match on **full per-rule structural identity** `(rule_id, severity, applies_to, trigger_keywords, trigger_anchors, negative_anchors)` + non-empty `check` — never rule-ID-set-only (slice-030A meta-M-add-2). The BC-1 logic/tuple/schema-pin tests + a new all-5-rule literal-constant oracle (incl. the first-ever pins for the slice-028 survivor rules BC-PROJ-3/BC-GLOBAL-2 — slice-030A meta-M-add-3) assert the fixtures, decoupling them from the gitignored vault. Absent `~/.claude/build-checks.md` ⇒ WARN/exit 0 (untracked/optional-install); present-but-non-conformant **including empty** ⇒ HALT/exit 1 with an attributed *"LOCAL VAULT DRIFT — reconstruct from <fixture>; this is NOT a slice regression"* message (slice-030A meta-M3; retires the R-4 anti-pattern where a truncation read as a confusing slice regression). slice-030A is the user-approved **split** of the original slice-030 after two BLOCKED dual-Critic loops surfaced a non-convergence signal; the shippability-catalog-row decoupling + archive-backtest fidelity are deferred to **slice-030B** (BCI-1's 030A wiring is two points — `/build-slice` Step 6 non-opt-out pre-finish + `/reflect` Step 5b fail-loud post-write — which fully retires R-4's substance; the catalog-row third wiring point is 030B).

### Added

- **BCI-1 — build-checks integrity gate (deterministic, non-opt-out, full per-rule structural identity vs git-tracked canonical fixtures)**
  New audit-enforced gate (NON-`-D` per ADR-019; naming-class peers BRANCH-1 / BC-1 / PMI-1 / UTF8-STDOUT-1 / CRP-1 / PCA-1). Programmatic gate: `tools/build_checks_integrity.py`. Wired non-opt-out at `/build-slice` Step 6 pre-finish AND as a `/reflect` Step 5b fail-loud post-write instruction. The gitignored live build-checks files are byte-reconstructed FROM the tracked fixtures; the literal-constant tuples in `tests/methodology/test_build_checks_audit.py` are the tracked oracle the fixtures are asserted against (fixture = subject, literal = oracle — closes the v2-B3/meta-M-add-3 unguarded-oracle hole for all five rules).
  - **Rule reference**: BCI-1 (slice-030A-repair-build-checks-vault; ADR-028 + ADR-029). Supersedes nothing; `/reflect` Step 5b promotion semantics are untouched (one fail-loud post-write instruction added).
  - **Defect class**: R-4 — silent BC-1 coverage degradation via LLM mis-execution of the non-deterministic Step 5b promotion prose (whole-file truncation to the last rule); plus the gitignored-vault coupling that made shippability rows / tuple-pin tests environment-fragile (the row-decoupling half is 030B).
  - **Validation**: `tests/methodology/test_build_checks_audit.py` (39 passed, 0 failed — was 17 failed; repointed tuple/schema tests + extended full-structural-identity pins + new `test_bc_proj_3_and_bc_global_2_have_expected_structural_identity`); `tests/methodology/test_build_checks_integrity.py` (7 passed — conformant-PASS, 1-rule-truncation-HALT, single-corrupted-`applies_to`/`severity`-HALT proving full-identity-not-ID-set, absent-global-WARN, empty-present-global-HALT, main() exit+attribution); `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_44_0_bci_1_entry_present_in_repo_and_installed` (this entry's bidirectional in-repo + installed pin); `$PY -m tools.build_checks_integrity` self-run against this repo exits 0 (slice-030A bootstrap self-application discharge — live files reconstructed from the canonical fixtures).
