## v0.43.0 — 2026-05-16

**`/diagnose` analysis-pass dispatch is sequential by default; the prior single-message parallel batch is an explicit `--parallel` opt-in.** `/diagnose` Step 5 previously fanned out all 10 analysis passes as multiple `Agent` tool calls in a single message (parallel batch). That dispatch shape triggers the parallel-spawn permission cascade-failure ([claude-code #57037](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/57037)) tracked as `architecture/risk-register.md` R-1 — spawned `general-purpose` subagents lose Read/Grep/Bash access and produce a degraded `diagnosis.html` (user-reported). This entry changes the **default**: passes are now dispatched **one `Agent` call per message, sequentially** (spawn → await → `write_pass.py` + 3-attempt cap → next), which defeats the cascade mode on the default path. The legacy fan-out remains reachable via `/diagnose --parallel` (position-independent flag, stripped before TARGET resolution by a 3-arm `case`; unknown `--`-flags are warned + ignored, never treated as the path — a flag typo never aborts). The per-pass ADR-001 subagent contract, the single canonical contract string (CSP-1), the slice-019 LAYER-EVID-1 N=6 pin, the COST-1.1 model routing, and the entire post-subagent writer flow are unchanged; only *when* each `Agent` is spawned changes, plus Step-5/5.5 dispatch-coupled prose rewritten dispatch-mode-aware (incl. a sequential early-exit silent-gap guard). Per the unconditional inclusion heuristic (this file's "Inclusion heuristic" section): a `/diagnose` run acceptable on 2026-05-15 is non-default on 2026-05-16 — a behavior change, so a changelog entry is obligated regardless of the slice-002 prose-pin-only precedent (TRI-1 M2 option B; the slice-002 changelog omission is treated as latent under-documentation).

### Changed

- **/diagnose sequential-dispatch default (ADR-027; NO new rule-ID minted) — sequential-by-default analysis-pass dispatch with a `--parallel` opt-in**
  Default `/diagnose` no longer dispatches N `Agent` calls in one message; the parallel batch is opt-in via `/diagnose --parallel`. Recorded as a `### Changed` behavior entry, **not** an audited rule — no rule-ID is minted (per /critique M2 + /critique-review M2 re-scope, TRI-1-ratified option B: the rule-ID half follows the slice-002 prose-pin-only precedent; the changelog-entry half honors the inclusion heuristic). Cross-referenced by ADR-027 + the slice-029 prose-pins, not by an audited rule-ID.
  - **Rule reference**: ADR-027 (slice-029-make-diagnose-dispatch-sequential). Deliberately NOT a minted rule-ID — this `### Changed` entry is governed by ADR-027 + the `tests/skills/diagnose/test_skill_md_pins.py` slice-029 prose-pins; R-1 (`architecture/risk-register.md`) transitions `open` → `mitigating`.
  - **Defect class**: every default `/diagnose` run exposed to the R-1 / claude-code #57037 parallel-spawn permission cascade-failure (subagents lose Read/Grep/Bash → degraded `diagnosis.html`); plus the slice-002-precedent ambiguity that left a behavior-change changelog obligation unstated.
  - **Validation**: `tests/skills/diagnose/test_skill_md_pins.py` slice-029 pins (`test_skill_md_step5_dispatch_is_sequential_by_default`, `test_skill_md_documents_parallel_optin`, `test_skill_md_step1_flag_strip_fail_safe`, `test_skill_md_step55_dispatch_aware_with_early_exit`) + the unchanged CSP-1 byte-equality + LAYER-EVID-1 N=6 + mini-CAD drift guards; `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_43_0_diagnose_sequential_dispatch_entry_present_in_repo_and_installed` (this entry's bidirectional in-repo + installed pin); `architecture/shippability.md` slice-029 row (RPCD-1 / SCPD-1 consumer-reference propagation).

## v0.42.0 — 2026-05-16

**version-agnostic UTF-8 rollup sentinel** — the UTF8-STDOUT-1 behavioural regression module's roll-up sentinel (`tests/methodology/test_utf8_stdout_regression.py::test_every_audit_tool_survives_cp1252_stdout_with_u2192_input`) previously asserted a hard-coded absolute tool count (`assert len(actual_audits) == 20`) plus a hand-maintained `post-slice-027` ledger comment. Every slice adding a `tools/*.py` audit tool had to manually bump that literal or the suite went red — the **N=5 count-bump maintenance-tax class** flagged across slices 023/025/026/027 (the dominant new-tool-codification friction). This rule refactors the sentinel to assert **bidirectional, repo-state-derived coverage-parity**: `discovered_set == covered_set`, where `discovered_set` is every `tools/*.py` (non-`_`, non-`__init__`) whose AST has a top-level `main` (exactly the `tools_with_main` population UTF8-STDOUT-1 governs — `utf8_stdout_audit._find_main_function` semantics, NOT the looser `_candidate_tools` glob) and `covered_set` is the tool tokens OBSERVED from this module's real cp1252 call sites (the `_POSITIONAL_SLICE_TOOLS`/`_ROOT_ONLY_TOOLS` parametrize constants [execution-bound] ∪ each bespoke `_assert_no_encoding_error(proc, "<tool>")` literal [source-presence proxy]). No hard-coded count, no version anchor; failure names the offending tool(s) in both directions.

- **UTF8-STDOUT-1 v1.1 — version-agnostic UTF-8 rollup sentinel**
  Rule-ID lineage is **preserved**: this is UTF8-STDOUT-1 **v1.1**, NOT a new rule ID — exactly mirroring the PMI-1 v1.0 → v1.1 version-agnostic-gate refactor ([[ADR-013]] / slice-014). The 3-part slice-014 template is reused: (1) derive-the-real-invariant (coverage-parity, not a count proxy); (2) an AST structural meta-test pinning the version-agnostic shape (no int-count `Compare` literal in the sentinel body, no `post-slice-NNN` anchor in the sentinel or either set-construction helper) PLUS a counter-anchor meta-test confining set-construction primitives to the two scanned helpers; (3) a failure-path regression test (slice-014-faithful — `monkeypatch` of the helpers, the parity assertion stays in-body and is genuinely exercised, NOT helper-extracted).
  - **Rule reference**: UTF8-STDOUT-1 v1.1 (per slice-028 [[ADR-026]]; rule-ID lineage from UTF8-STDOUT-1 v1.0 / v0.37.0 / slice-023, mirroring PMI-1 v1.x EPGD-1 lineage precedent).
  - **Defect class**: the recurring N=5 count-bump maintenance tax; AND — caught multi-layer by the dual-Critic stack per the slice-022 self-violation law (this is an audit-adjacent codification slice) — the option-3 declaration-registry self-defect (a "version-agnostic" refactor that silently *weakens* the protected invariant: covered-set declared not observed; /critique B2) and the option-4 helper-extraction self-defect (failure-path helper-extraction would move the count-literal-risk logic out of the AST-meta-test scan scope → fail-OPEN; /critique-review B-add-1). Both resolved before /build-slice; the meta-test scans the helpers + a counter-anchor pins set-construction confinement.
  - **Validation method**: the version-agnostic-shape meta-test + the counter-anchor meta-test + the bidirectional failure-path regression test (all in `tests/methodology/test_utf8_stdout_regression.py`); the canonical phrase `version-agnostic UTF-8 rollup sentinel` pinned across **N=3 surfaces** (sentinel docstring + this changelog v0.42.0 entry [in-repo + installed, bidirectional byte-equality] + [[ADR-026]] body) per the slice-013 EPGD-1 / slice-015 SCPD-1 N-surface schema-pin precedent; plus `architecture/shippability.md` slice-028 row (RPCD-1 / SCPD-1 consumer-reference propagation) and the v0.42.0 entry-pin tests in `test_methodology_changelog.py`.

The covered-set AST walk **fails closed** (slice-028 /critique-review M-add-1): a covered param-list that is not a flat `ast.List` of `str` Constants, or an `_assert_no_encoding_error` 2nd arg that is neither a str Constant nor the parametrize loop `Name`, raises a loud named diagnostic — it is never silently skipped (silent-skip would drop a coverage token → an uncovered tool falsely GREEN). The bespoke direction is honestly an unverified source-presence proxy (the argv↔assertion-name binding is not checked here — the per-tool tests are the execution guarantee for that direction); the "cannot pass without an executed test" claim is NOT made (the same ADR-013 Consequences-honesty convention applied to /critique M2). UTF8-STDOUT-1 v1.1 is a rule-version evolution, **not** an audit-enforced gate — no new `tools/*.py`; the `## Pipeline position` / NON-`-D` audit-gate naming class (BC-1 / PMI-1 / CRP-1 / PCA-1) does not apply.

slice-028's independent-counter knowledge is preserved (the old count comment's load-bearing note, /critique M3b): this sentinel governs `tools_with_main` cp1252 coverage parity and is INDEPENDENT of the INST-1 `install_audit._CANONICAL_TOOLS` canonical-tuple inventory — a separate consumer-propagation site, out of slice-028 scope (relocated into the sentinel module block + [[ADR-026]] Consequences).

## v0.41.0 — 2026-05-16

**Pipeline-chain auto-advance codified as an audit-enforced gate**, codified as **PCA-1** — the per-slice loop (`/slice → /design-slice → /critique → /critique-review → /build-slice → /validate-slice → /reflect`) previously required the operator to manually re-invoke every step; each in-loop skill ended only with a human-readable `## Next step` prose section (advisory narrative, non-uniform, not a directive Claude acts on). PCA-1 adds a normalized, machine-actionable **`## Pipeline position`** H2 section to 8 covered skills (the 7 in-loop skills + `commit-slice`, included so the successor graph is closed and auditable). Each in-loop skill, on successful completion with no pending user-input gate, auto-invokes its declared successor via the Skill tool without waiting for the user; the chain HARD-STOPS before `/commit-slice` (`reflect` and `commit-slice` carry `auto-advance: false`), which remains user-invoked by contract. Existing manual single-skill invocation is unchanged.

The directive is **fail-closed**: an enumerated set of user-input gates HALTS auto-advance (surface to user, resume only on explicit user action) — `/critique` Step 4.5 TRI-1 user-owned triage; `/critique` verdict BLOCKED; `/build-slice` plan-mode (ExitPlanMode) approval; `/build-slice` mid-slice smoke-gate failure; `/validate-slice` any per-criterion **FAIL** OR any **PARTIAL** (per-criterion or aggregate `Result: PARTIAL` — a user-decides-remediation state, same disposition as FAIL, explicit per the must-not-defer completeness principle, not catch-all-covered). Any state not provably matching a clean-completion transition surfaces to the user; auto-advance is never the default for an ambiguous state.

Per slice-027 [[ADR-025]], PCA-1 is an **audit-enforced gate** (its programmatic gate is `tools/pipeline_chain_audit.py`), so per [[ADR-019]]'s test-pinned naming note it takes the **bare `PCA-1` form — NO `-D` suffix**. The `-D` suffix is reserved for /critique-time prose-heuristic disciplines with no programmatic audit (RSAD-1 / EPGD-1 / SCPD-1 / RPCD-1 / TPHD-1 / BFRD-1 / PTFCD-1's sub-clause); PCA-1 joins the audit-enforced-gate naming class alongside BC-1, CAD-1, PMI-1, INST-1, WIRE-1, BRANCH-1, UTF8-STDOUT-1, CRP-1. This entry **conforms to** ADR-019; it does not supersede it.

The canonical phrase `Pipeline position` is pinned across N=3 surfaces (slice-013 EPGD-1 + slice-015 SCPD-1 N-surface schema-pin precedent): (1) the `## Pipeline position` H2 section in each of the 8 covered `skills/*/SKILL.md` + (2) in-repo `methodology-changelog.md` v0.41.0 entry (this file) + (3) installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` v0.41.0 entry. The `## Pipeline position` block schema (`predecessor` / `successor` / `auto-advance` / `on-clean-completion` / `user-input gates`) is canonically defined once here and referenced — not duplicated — from skill prose and the audit (RPCD-1). `/critique`'s flat `successor:` field models only its primary `/critique-review` edge; the post-TRI-1 → `/build-slice` hop and `/critique`→`/critique` BLOCKED self-loop live in `on-clean-completion` prose and are NOT chain-flagged (per slice-027 /critique-review m-add-1).

slice-027 is **PCA-1 bootstrap-reference instance #1** (mirrors slice-021/BRANCH-1, slice-026/CRP-1): it authors the `## Pipeline position` directive, so that directive does not exist on disk during slice-027's own loop (the chain ran manually per the user's at-invocation directive — the pre-codification equivalent of PCA-1). slice-027's recursive self-application is discharged by `/critique-review` run on slice-027 (verdict EXTEND — meta-Critic added M-add-1: `/validate-slice` PARTIAL was an unenumerated clean-path gate, same defect class as the first Critic's M3, one row down; ACCEPTED-FIXED) + the `tools/pipeline_chain_audit.py` audit run against the repo at Step 6 (exit 0). Every slice after 027 inherits a self-gating PCA-1.

PMI-1 invariant maintained atomically: `VERSION` (in-repo) + `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` (installed) + `plugin.yaml.version` bumped 0.40.0 → 0.41.0 in lockstep.

### Added

- **PCA-1 — Pipeline-chain auto-advance**
  Normalized `## Pipeline position` H2 section on 8 covered skills (`slice`, `design-slice`, `critique`, `critique-review`, `build-slice`, `validate-slice`, `reflect` — in-loop, `auto-advance: true` for the first 6, `reflect` = `false` terminal-before-commit — plus `commit-slice`, `auto-advance: false` terminus). Auto-invoke-successor-on-clean-completion contract; fail-closed user-input-gate enumeration; new `tools/pipeline_chain_audit.py` at `/build-slice` Step 6 pre-finish; registered in `tools/install_audit.py` `_CANONICAL_TOOLS` + `plugin.yaml` (rule `PCA-1`). `/commit-slice` is never auto-invoked under any path.
  - **Rule reference**: PCA-1 (per slice-027 [[ADR-025]]; audit-enforced-gate naming class, NON-`-D` per [[ADR-019]]; canonical phrase `Pipeline position` pinned across N=3 surfaces). The slice-022 self-violation law fired as pre-budgeted: this PCA-1-codifying slice's own first draft cited a phantom `tests/tools/` path (the exact PTFCD-1 cross-file-reference class PCA-1's sibling disciplines catch) — caught as /critique B1 and fixed in-round; the /critique-review meta-Critic then added M-add-1 (the `/validate-slice` PARTIAL gate omission).
  - **Defect class**: A multi-step pipeline whose "what's next" knowledge lives only in human-prose `## Next step` sections forces manual re-invocation at every step and has no machine-checkable guarantee that the chain is correctly wired or that the terminal boundary (`/commit-slice` user-invoked) holds. Worse, an auto-advancing chain with no enumerated user-input gates can silently advance past a state that needs the user (TRI-1 triage, a validation FAIL/PARTIAL) — the fail-closed gate model + audit prevent both the wiring drift and the silent-advance hazard.
  - **Validation**: `tools/pipeline_chain_audit.py` exits 0 only when all 8 `## Pipeline position` blocks are well-formed, successor edges match the canonical loop, and `reflect`/`commit-slice` are `auto-advance: false`. `tests/methodology/test_pipeline_chain_audit.py` (≥2 unit tests: clean-chain-exits-zero, missing-block-exits-one). `tests/methodology/test_pipeline_position_block_drift.py` (parametrized byte-equality of the `## Pipeline position` section across all 8 in-repo↔installed skill pairs — the AC #5 traceability element). `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py` pins this v0.41.0 entry (in-repo + installed) + the shippability consumer-propagation row + ADR-025.

## v0.40.0 — 2026-05-16

**Critique-review prerequisite check codified as an audit-enforced `/build-slice` gate**, codified as **CRP-1** — `/critique-review` (DR-1) is mandatory in Standard mode for any slice with mandatory-Critic triggers (slice-010 default heuristic; CLAUDE.md "Builder ↔ Critic separation"), but DR-1 v0.17.0 recorded it as "Manual invocation only" and `tools/critique_review_audit.py` runs from exactly one site (`skills/critique-review/SKILL.md`) — it validates the *structure* of a `critique-review.md` that exists and is NOT wired into `/validate-slice`, so nothing structurally detected a *skip*. slice-025 skipped `/critique-review` and the skip was caught ONLY because AC4 coincidentally hand-authored a "DR-1 clean" assertion whose own verification failed at `/validate-slice`; absent that AC the skip ships unnoticed. CRP-1 is the **first structural skip-detector**: a `## Prerequisite check` gate (primary, refuse-to-start) + a Step 6 defense-in-depth re-run that refuses `/build-slice` when mode ∈ {STANDARD, HEAVY} AND `milestone.md` `critic-required: true` AND `critique-review.md` absent AND no canonical `critique-review-skip` escape-hatch.

Per slice-026 [[ADR-024]], CRP-1 is an **audit-enforced gate** (its programmatic gate is `tools/critique_review_prerequisite_audit.py`), so per [[ADR-019]]'s test-pinned naming note it takes the **bare `CRP-1` form — NO `-D` suffix**. The `-D` suffix is reserved for /critique-time prose-heuristic disciplines with no programmatic audit (RSAD-1 / EPGD-1 / SCPD-1 / RPCD-1 / TPHD-1 / BFRD-1 / PTFCD-1's complementary sub-clause); CRP-1 joins the audit-enforced-gate naming class alongside BC-1, CAD-1, PMI-1, INST-1, WIRE-1, BRANCH-1, UTF8-STDOUT-1. This entry **conforms to** ADR-019; it does not supersede it. The slice-022 lesson ("codification slices commit exactly the violation their codified discipline catches") fired: slice-026's first draft named the rule `CRPD-1` with a `-D` suffix — a direct ADR-019 naming-class contradiction — caught as /critique B1 and renamed in-round.

The escape-hatch is the **optional `critique-review-skip:` key in the active slice's `milestone.md` frontmatter** (value `^skip — rationale: .+`), NOT `build-log.md` Events as BRANCH-1 (the CRP-1 primary gate fires at the `## Prerequisite check` before `build-log.md` exists) NOR a free-form body line (would be clobbered by `/build-slice` Step 7b's continuous milestone.md rewrite → false-refuse at the Step 6 re-run; per slice-026 /critique B2). Detection is keyed on the frontmatter KEY, not a body-substring scan, eliminating the BRANCH-1-style narrative-prose false-positive (slice-026 /critique m2). `/build-slice` Step 7b carries an explicit instruction to preserve the key verbatim.

The canonical phrase `Critique-review prerequisite check` is pinned across N=3 surfaces (slice-013 EPGD-1 + slice-015 SCPD-1 N-surface schema-pin precedent): (1) `skills/build-slice/SKILL.md` CRP-1 prerequisite sub-block + Step 6 block + (2) in-repo `methodology-changelog.md` v0.40.0 entry (this file) + (3) installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` v0.40.0 entry.

slice-026 is **CRP-1 bootstrap-reference instance #1** (mirrors slice-021/BRANCH-1, `methodology-changelog.md` L205): it authors the CRP-1 sub-block, so that sub-block does not exist at slice-026's own prerequisite check and cannot self-gate that build. slice-026's recursive self-application is discharged by `/critique-review` run on slice-026 (verdict EXTEND — meta-Critic added M-add-1: the milestone.md template forward-sync pair is under no byte-equality gate; ACCEPTED-FIXED by enumeration-discharge) + the audit run against slice-026's own folder at Step 6 (exit 0). Every slice after 026 inherits a self-gating CRP-1.

PMI-1 invariant maintained atomically: `VERSION` (in-repo) + `~/.claude/ai-sdlc-VERSION` (installed) + `plugin.yaml.version` all bump 0.39.0 → 0.40.0.

### Added

- **CRP-1 — new `tools/critique_review_prerequisite_audit.py` + `skills/build-slice/SKILL.md` `## Prerequisite check` sub-block + Step 6 defense-in-depth re-run codify the critique-review-prerequisite check**
  Audit-enforced gate (structurally modeled on `tools/branch_workflow_audit.py`/BRANCH-1: dataclass result, `--json`, exit 0/1/2, `_stdout.reconfigure_stdout_utf8()` per UTF8-STDOUT-1). Refuse condition (exit 1, `mandatory-critique-review-absent`): mode ∈ {STANDARD, HEAVY} AND `critic-required: true` AND `critique-review.md` absent AND no canonical `critique-review-skip` key — message names which conditions held (refuse-path observability). `escape-hatch-malformed` (exit 1) on off-canonical key value; `usage-error`/`mode-unresolvable` (exit 2). Mode resolved from `architecture/triage.md` frontmatter `mode:` → fallback `CLAUDE.md` `**Mode**:`.
  - **Rule reference**: CRP-1 (per slice-026 [[ADR-024]]; audit-enforced-gate naming class, NON-`-D` per [[ADR-019]]; canonical phrase `Critique-review prerequisite check` pinned across N=3 surfaces).
  - **Defect class**: a mandatory pipeline step (DR-1 `/critique-review`) whose enforcement is prose-only/manual is detectable only via per-slice AC authorship — not structurally enforced. slice-025 demonstrated the silent-skip; there is no `/validate-slice`-side net (`critique_review_audit` runs only inside `/critique-review`). Codification at the `## Prerequisite check` (refuse-to-start) + Step 6 (defense-in-depth) is the canonical mitigation.
  - **Limitations**: CRP-1 enforces *presence* of `critique-review.md` (or a documented skip), not its *quality* (structure is `critique_review_audit`'s job; finding-ID cross-reference is a DR-1 v2 candidate). Auto-invoking `/critique-review` remains out of scope (DR-1 v2 `**Dual-review**: true`). The `milestone.md` template forward-sync pair (`templates/milestone.md` ↔ `~/.claude/templates/milestone.md`) is propagated manually in lockstep and enumerated in slice-026 design.md §"What's new" (N=5) — `install_audit.py` `_check_templates` is existence-only; a milestone-template byte-equality discipline is a separate slice's scope (enumeration-discharge per /critique-review M-add-1, not a byte-equality exemption).

## v0.39.0 — 2026-05-16

**Phantom-test-file-citation discipline codified into `agents/critique.md` Dim 9 11th sub-clause + two audit gates**, codified as **PTFCD-1** — the Critic-stack reviews TF-1 plan `Test path` cells and shippability `Command`-cell pytest targets statically for name-harmonization (TPHD-1), status, and cross-file consistency (FBCD-1), but NO static surface verifies that a cited test file actually exists on disk when the stated command runs. That question is reachable only at command execution. slice-024 cited a non-existent `tests/methodology/test_shippability_catalog.py` at 3 sibling sites; it survived 19 Critic-stack findings + BOTH Critic layers and died only at /validate-slice Step 5.5 real-command execution. slice-023 B4 cited a `tests/methodology/test_row_*.py` convention (no such file/convention exists), caught reactively at /critique. N=2 distinct slices; slice-023 lesson 5 explicitly foreshadowed the recurrence site → the project's "promote at N=2 when a lesson named the future codification target AND its recurrence site at N=1" convention is met (developer-process calibration, not a `risk-register.md` project risk).

PTFCD-1 is codified across three coordinated surfaces with two named sub-modes covering the two citation surfaces:

- **Sub-mode (a) TF-1-plan-path existence** — `/build-slice` Step 6 strict-pre-finish surface. `tools/test_first_audit.py --strict-pre-finish` emits `missing-test-path-file` for any TF-1 plan row whose `row.status == "PASSING"` and whose resolved `Test path` is absent. The PASSING gate is load-bearing: a still-PENDING test-first row (whose file the slice itself creates later) is reported exactly once by the pre-existing `non-passing-pre-finish` loop, never doubled; non-strict runs never reach the check so mid-slice PENDING rows never false-positive. Concrete miss pre-codification: slice-023 B4.
- **Sub-mode (b) shippability-Command-cell-path existence** — `/validate-slice` Step 5.5 pre-catalog surface. New `tools/shippability_path_audit.py` extracts only `tests/\S+\.py` tokens appearing after the `pytest` keyword in each `Command` cell (M2-pinned predicate: interpreter path / `-m` / `pytest` / `-q` / `--no-header` excluded), strips markdown backticks (slice-024 23-spurious-FAIL footgun), `::`-splits the selector, resolves repo-root-relative, and flags any token whose file does not exist. Wired as the first action of /validate-slice Step 5.5, gating the catalog run on exit 0 so a phantom citation surfaces as ONE clear violation instead of N confusing per-row pytest "file not found" FAILs. Concrete miss pre-codification: slice-024.

The canonical phrase `Phantom test-file citation discipline` is pinned across N=3 surfaces per the slice-013 EPGD-1 + slice-015 SCPD-1 + slice-016 RPCD-1 N-surface schema-pin precedent: (1) [[agents/critique.md]] Dim 9 11th sub-clause title + (2) in-repo `methodology-changelog.md` v0.39.0 entry (this file) + (3) installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` v0.39.0 entry. **N-surface schema-pin 3-surface shape ratchets to N=12 instances stable** post-slice-025.

Per slice-025 [[ADR-023]], the rule-ID is **PTFCD-1 "Phantom-Test-File-Citation Discipline"** with **-D suffix** at end of abbreviation, extending the RSAD-1 + EPGD-1 + SCPD-1 + RPCD-1 + TPHD-1 + LAYER-EVID-1 + FBCD-1 -D-suffix calibration-trail convention to **N=8 stable**. Unlike its prose-only -D siblings, PTFCD-1 IS additionally audit-enforced by two `tools/*_audit.py` gates (`test_first_audit.py` strict-pre-finish + the new `shippability_path_audit.py`) — the -D signals the prose-heuristic Dim 9 sub-clause that complements the gates; raw-finding-count is not a completeness signal for this class (slice-024 N=19 + 2 layers missed it), so the mechanical gates are the canonical mitigation and the sub-clause instructs the Critic to apply the check mechanically rather than rely on having "looked carefully".

PTFCD-1 is the **execution-reachable structural sibling** to Dim 9 sub-clause 9 (RPCD-1, `Runtime-prerequisite completeness on proposed fixes`): RPCD-1 catches a fix introducing a NEW symbol/status/anchor the surrounding tooling does not satisfy; PTFCD-1 catches a slice citing a test-file *path* that does not exist on disk. Cross-references in PTFCD-1's sub-clause body cite by **canonical title strings** (not ordinal sub-clause number) so `test_critique_dim_9_cross_references_resolve` verifies pointer resolution under future Dim 9 renumbering.

The new 11th sub-clause grows Dim 9's structural-invariant count from 10 to 11 sub-clauses per the PMI-1 structural-invariant supersession discipline applied at the structural-invariant level (slice-011 N=1 `_lists_five` → `_lists_six`; slice-013 N=2 `_lists_six` → `_lists_seven`; slice-015 N=3 `_lists_seven` → `_lists_eight`; slice-016 N=4 `_lists_eight` → `_lists_nine`; slice-024 N=5 `_lists_nine` → `_lists_ten`; slice-025 N=6 `_lists_ten` → `_lists_eleven`). **PMI-1 structural-invariant supersession N=5 → N=6 stable** post-slice-025. SCPD-1 proactive-application: shippability rows 14/19/21/23/24/32 pytest commands referencing `::test_critique_dim_9_lists_ten_sub_clauses` propagated `_lists_ten` → `_lists_eleven` in the same /build-slice block BEFORE the /validate-slice catalog run (historical-narrative `_lists_*` mentions in those rows' Critical-path cells preserved). The prior `_entry_present_in_repo_and_installed`-family entry-pin functions persist untouched through slice-025's atomic version bump 0.38.0 → 0.39.0 — the eleventh version bump under PMI-1 v1.1's version-agnostic-gate shape. **Empirical retirement-proof of PMI-1 v1.1 ratchets to N=11 stable** post-slice-025.

Per slice-025 /critique (first Critic) — 2 Blockers + 3 Majors + 2 Minors caught on the slice's own drafts, all ACCEPTED-FIXED in-round; B1 was the predicted FBCD-1 sub-mode (a) recursive-self-application catch (the slice codifying PTFCD-1 itself committed a `PTFC-1`-vs-`PTFCD-1` rule-ID cross-file drift across mission-brief vs design/ADR). TPHD-1 sub-mode (c) pre-flight at /build-slice additionally caught a phantom test-function citation in the slice's own TF-1 plan (`test_critique_agent_byte_equal_in_repo_vs_installed` does not exist; harmonized to the real `test_in_repo_and_installed_critique_agent_are_content_equal`) — recursive-self-application closure: the slice codifying the phantom-citation discipline committed and self-caught a phantom citation.

PTFCD-1 is applied prospectively — slice-025 onward. Past slices retain their original /critique outputs; the Critic prompt edit was applied at /build-slice Phase 1 (CAD-1 byte-equal forward-sync). No retroactive re-evaluation.

### Added

- **PTFCD-1 — `agents/critique.md` Dim 9 sub-clause 11 + `test_first_audit.py` strict-pre-finish existence check + new `tools/shippability_path_audit.py` codify the phantom-test-file-citation discipline**
  Execution-reachable test-file-existence discipline (peer cross-reference: execution-reachable structural sibling to Dim 9 sub-clause 9 RPCD-1). Two named sub-modes covering the two citation surfaces:
  - **Sub-mode (a) TF-1-plan-path existence** (slice-023 B4): `tools/test_first_audit.py --strict-pre-finish` emits `missing-test-path-file` for any PASSING TF-1 row whose resolved Test path is absent; gated on `row.status == "PASSING"` to avoid double-flagging still-PENDING test-first rows.
  - **Sub-mode (b) shippability-Command-cell-path existence** (slice-024): `tools/shippability_path_audit.py` flags non-existent `tests/<...>.py` tokens (post-`pytest`, backtick-stripped, `::`-split) in `architecture/shippability.md` `Command` cells; wired as the /validate-slice Step 5.5 pre-catalog gate.

  When reviewing a slice that authors TF-1 plan rows OR shippability catalog `Command` cells, the Critic SHOULD stat every cited path against the on-disk tree, treat a *convention/pattern* citation (`test_row_*.py`) as a phantom Blocker, and apply the check mechanically (raw-finding-count is not a completeness signal — slice-024 N=19 + 2 layers missed it).
  - **Rule reference**: PTFCD-1 (per slice-025 [[ADR-023]]; -D-suffix convention N=7 → N=8 stable; canonical title `Phantom test-file citation discipline` pinned across N=3 surfaces).

  - **Limitations**: PTFCD-1 verifies test-file existence at the *file* level; pytest *function*-name resolution inside an existing file is out of scope (N=0 witnessed; deferred). `_find_repo_root` falls back to the brief/catalog parent when no `.git`/`VERSION` sentinel is found — a wrong root yields a readable not-exists violation (the intended signal), never a crash.
  - **Validation**: `tests/methodology/test_critique_agent.py::test_critique_dim_9_lists_eleven_sub_clauses` (AC #3 — PMI-1 structural-invariant supersession 10→11; SUPERSEDES `_lists_ten_sub_clauses`); `test_critique_dim_9_phantom_test_file_citation_sub_clause_present` / `_location_pinned` / `_names_both_sub_modes` / `_paragraph_cites_slice_023_024` (AC #3 — canonical-literal + location + two-sub-mode + cross-slice anchor pins per slice-016/024 precedent); `tests/methodology/test_test_first_audit.py::test_strict_pre_finish_flags_missing_test_path_file` / `test_non_strict_does_not_flag_pending_missing_file` / `test_existence_check_covers_non_pytest_rows` / `test_pending_row_missing_file_emits_exactly_one_violation` (AC #1); `tests/methodology/test_shippability_path_existence.py::test_flags_phantom_command_cell_test_path` / `test_strips_backticks_before_path_resolution` / `test_interpreter_and_dash_m_dash_q_tokens_not_flagged` (AC #2); `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_39_0_ptfcd_1_entry_present_in_repo_and_installed` (AC #4 — 3-pin bidirectional shape) + `test_adr_023_present_and_reversibility_cheap` (AC #4 — ADR-023 surface); `tests/methodology/test_critique_agent_drift.py::test_in_repo_and_installed_critique_agent_are_content_equal` (AC #3 — CAD-1 byte-equality on agents/critique.md); `test_plugin_yaml_version_matches_version_file_invariant` (AC #4 — PMI-1 v1.1 atomic bump 0.38.0 → 0.39.0 with ZERO gate-body modification).

## v0.38.0 — 2026-05-15

**Fix-block-completeness discipline codified into `agents/critique.md` Dim 9 10th sub-clause**, codified as **FBCD-1** — when the Critic at /critique time evaluates a slice's draft, or at /critique-review time evaluates the Builder's ACCEPTED-FIXED sweeps post-disposition-triage, claims (file paths, numeric counts, ADR IDs, test function names, rule IDs, anchor literals) authored across multiple slice-authoring files (mission-brief.md + design.md + ADR-NNN + milestone.md) MUST be cross-file-consistent at every site. The Critic must grep for the claim's anchor substring across all surfaces and enumerate every site needing consistency or update. Two distinct sub-modes covering the temporal axis:

- **Sub-mode (a) Original-draft cross-file consistency** — first-Critic catch at /critique time. When a slice authors a claim across multiple files, the Critic must verify the claim is identical at every site at design time. Concrete misses pre-codification: slice-022 N=1 NEW class (sub-mode (a) inaugural instance) + slice-023 N=2 (B5 17/17/17 count drift across mission-brief AC #2 + design.md L120/121/130/131 + L225 + L344; B3 `tests/decisions/` ADR-pin path drift across mission-brief + design.md) = N=3 cumulative under sub-mode (a) per slice-023 reflection Pattern 4 enumeration.

- **Sub-mode (b) Post-ACCEPTED-FIXED sibling-sweep** — meta-Critic catch at /critique-review time. When Builder applies an ACCEPTED-FIXED fix at one site at /critique-disposition triage, sibling sites carrying the same claim may still carry the OLD value. Meta-Critic must grep for the anchor substring across all slice-authoring files post-fix to verify the fix-block propagated. Concrete misses pre-codification: slice-020 M-add-1 (N=1) + slice-021 M-add-1-rerun/2-rerun/3-rerun (N=3 within-slice; explicitly tagged "same class — fix-block-completeness recursion within the rerun's own fix-block" at slice-021 critique-review.md L39) + slice-022 M-add-2/3 (N=2 — both explicitly named "fix-block-completeness gap") + slice-023 M-add-1/2/3/4 (N=4 within-slice including M5 test-file split sibling sites at design.md L194 + mission-brief.md L54 + ADR-021 L73) = **N=10 cumulative cross-instances across N=4 distinct slices** per slice-023 reflection Pattern 3 + slice-021/022 critique-review.md M-add-* enumeration.

The canonical phrase `Fix-block-completeness discipline` is pinned across N=3 surfaces per the slice-013 EPGD-1 + slice-015 SCPD-1 + slice-016 RPCD-1 N-surface schema-pin precedent: (1) [[agents/critique.md]] Dim 9 10th sub-clause title + (2) in-repo `methodology-changelog.md` v0.38.0 entry (this file) + (3) installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` v0.38.0 entry. **N-surface schema-pin 3-surface shape ratchets to N=11 instances stable** post-slice-024 (RSAD-1 v0.26.0 + EPGD-1 v0.28.0 + PMI-1-v1.1 v0.29.0 + SCPD-1 v0.30.0 + RPCD-1 v0.31.0 + TPHD-1 v0.32.0 + LAYER-EVID-1 v0.33.0 + BFRD-1 v0.34.0 + BRANCH-1 v0.35.0 + UTF8-STDOUT-1 v0.37.0 + FBCD-1 v0.38.0).

Per slice-024 [[ADR-022]], the rule-ID is **FBCD-1 "Fix-Block-Completeness Discipline"** with **-D suffix** at end of abbreviation, extending the slice-011 RSAD-1 + slice-013 EPGD-1 + slice-015 SCPD-1 + slice-016 RPCD-1 + slice-017 TPHD-1 + slice-019 LAYER-EVID-1 -D-suffix calibration-trail convention to **N=7 stable** — the -D signals prose-heuristic / discipline semantics applied at /critique-time + /critique-review-time, distinct from the audit-enforced gate semantics of sibling rules (BC-1 / PMI-1 / CAD-1 / TF-1 / RR-1 / INST-1 / VAL-1 / WIRE-1 / NFR-1 / CSP-1 / BFRD-1 / BRANCH-1 / UTF8-STDOUT-1, each of which has a corresponding `tools/*_audit.py` enforcing the rule programmatically). FBCD-1 lives in `agents/critique.md` Dim 9 sub-clause 10 body prose only.

FBCD-1 is the **temporal extension** of Dim 9 sub-clause 2 (`Tooling-doc-vs-implementation parity` sub-clause's design.md-mechanical-tables-vs-canonical-inventory body, i.e., CCC-1 v1.1 / slice-009): sub-clause 2 catches mechanical-table-vs-inventory drift on the initial draft against an EXTERNAL canonical inventory; FBCD-1 catches drift WITHIN-slice across mission-brief.md + design.md + ADR-NNN + milestone.md (sub-mode a) AND extends to post-ACCEPTED-FIXED sibling-site propagation (sub-mode b). FBCD-1's two sub-modes match the RPCD-1 / EPGD-1 / SCPD-1 multi-sub-mode codification template established at slices 013/015/016. ### Added

- **FBCD-1 — `agents/critique.md` Dim 9 sub-clause 10 codifies fix-block-completeness discipline on cross-file claim consistency + post-ACCEPTED-FIXED sibling-sweep**
  Cross-file-consistency-at-draft-time + post-fix-propagation-completeness discipline (peer cross-reference: temporal extension of Dim 9 sub-clause 2 CCC-1 v1.1 design.md-tables-vs-canonical-inventory; structural-runtime sibling to Dim 9 sub-clause 9 RPCD-1). Two named sub-modes covering the temporal axis of the empirical evidence base:
  - **Sub-mode (a) Original-draft cross-file consistency** (slice-022 N=1 + slice-023 N=2 = N=3 cumulative sub-mode (a) per slice-023 reflection Pattern 4): at /critique time, the Critic verifies every claim authored across multiple slice-authoring files is identical at every site. Concrete miss: slice-023 B5 17/17/17 count drift across mission-brief AC #2 + design.md L120/121/130/131 + L225 + L344 — 6-site count divergence at original draft time.
  - **Sub-mode (b) Post-ACCEPTED-FIXED sibling-sweep** (slice-020 N=1 + slice-021 N=3 + slice-022 N=2 + slice-023 N=4 = N=10 cumulative sub-mode (b) per slice-021/022/023 critique-review.md M-add-* enumeration): at /critique-review time, the meta-Critic verifies that Builder's ACCEPTED-FIXED fix-block applied at /critique-disposition triage propagated to ALL sibling sites carrying the same claim. Concrete miss: slice-023 M-add-3 ADR-021 L66-67 helper-body short-circuit drift after M6 fix — Builder's ACCEPTED-FIXED at design.md L86-94 missed the sibling site at ADR-021.

  When evaluating any /critique disposition or /critique-review meta-finding, the Critic SHOULD apply both sub-modes to the slice-authoring file set ({mission-brief.md, design.md, ADR-NNN, milestone.md}) BEFORE filing the finding or ratifying the disposition — sub-mode (b) sibling-sweep is particularly important when /critique-disposition triage produces multi-site ACCEPTED-FIXED sweeps (≥3 sites).
  - **Rule reference**: FBCD-1 (per slice-024 [[ADR-022]]; -D-suffix convention N=6 → N=7 stable post-RSAD-1 v0.26.0 + EPGD-1 v0.28.0 + SCPD-1 v0.30.0 + RPCD-1 v0.31.0 + TPHD-1 v0.32.0 + LAYER-EVID-1 v0.33.0 + FBCD-1 v0.38.0; canonical title `Fix-block-completeness discipline` pinned across N=3 surfaces).
  - **Defect class**: codification slices empirically attract high vault-claim-density per slice-023 reflection observation; Builders authoring across mission-brief + design + ADR + milestone consistently produce cross-file claim drifts at draft time (sub-mode a) and miss sibling sites during multi-site ACCEPTED-FIXED sweeps at /critique-disposition triage (sub-mode b). Caught at /critique-review by DR-1 meta-Critic across slices 020/021/022/023 — four consecutive codification slices show the same FBCD-1 sub-mode (b) class at first-Critic time (N=10 cumulative cross-instances; well past N=2 proactive codification threshold per EPGD-1 + SCPD-1 precedent).
  - **Limitations**: FBCD-1 stays prose-pin-discipline-only at slice-024 (lives in `agents/critique.md` Dim 9 sub-clause 10 body prose; no audit-enforced gate). The two named sub-modes (a/b) each require context-specific application that varies by slice scope (every claim's anchor substring is unique per slice; every fix-block's scope is unique per /critique disposition); building a general-purpose audit at codification time risks under-engineering (too narrow to catch real cases) or over-engineering (broad enough to flag false positives that erode signal-to-noise). v2 candidate `tools/fbcd_1_audit.py` deferred until N≥3 FBCD-1 violations recur post-codification at slice-025+. ## v0.37.0 — 2026-05-15

**Default UTF-8 stdout in audit tools codified at `tools/_stdout.py` helper + `tools/utf8_stdout_audit.py` AST structural audit + one-line `_stdout.reconfigure_stdout_utf8()` invocation pattern as first executable statement of every `tools/*.py` `main()`** as **UTF8-STDOUT-1** — retires the Windows cp1252 console encoding class (N=6 cumulative recurrence across slices 007 / 016 / 018 / 020 / 021 / 022; aggregated lessons named `audit-tools-default-utf8-stdout` as HIGHEST-priority candidate at slice-022 ship; structurally overdue).

Per slice-023 [[ADR-021]] (reversibility: cheap; supersedes: null — shell-side `$env:PYTHONIOENCODING="utf-8"` workaround was DEVIATION pattern, not ADR-codified discipline).

Three surfaces (N-surface schema-pin shape):
- **`tools/_stdout.py`** — shared helper module exposing `reconfigure_stdout_utf8()`. Idempotent. `errors="replace"` (not "strict") so out-of-band non-UTF-8 bytes degrade gracefully. No-op when streams lack `reconfigure` (test capture / StringIO). Per M6 + M-add-3 ACCEPTED-FIXED at slice-023: unconditionally reconfigures with both `encoding="utf-8"` AND `errors="replace"` (no encoding-only short-circuit) to guarantee post-call state regardless of prior caller's errors mode.
- **`tools/utf8_stdout_audit.py`** — AST-based structural audit. For each `tools/*.py` (excluding `__init__.py` + leading-underscore helpers), parses module, finds top-level `def main`, identifies the first executable statement after any docstring, and refuses any module where that statement is not the canonical `_stdout.reconfigure_stdout_utf8()` call. Self-applies (its own `main()` conforms). Exit codes: 0 clean / 1 violation / 2 usage error. JSON output via `--json`.
- **One-line invocation pattern at every audit tool's `main()`** — first executable statement (before `argparse.ArgumentParser`) is `_stdout.reconfigure_stdout_utf8()`. Canonical import form pinned `from tools import _stdout` (per M4 ACCEPTED-FIXED at slice-023 — `from . import _stdout` not accepted). 17 audit tools conform post-slice-023.

The canonical phrase `UTF8-STDOUT-1` is pinned across N=3 surfaces: (1) `tools/_stdout.py` helper module docstring, (2) `tools/utf8_stdout_audit.py` audit module docstring, (3) in-repo `methodology-changelog.md` v0.37.0 entry (this file). **N-surface schema-pin shape ratchets to N=10 stable** with slice-023.

**PMI-1 filter narrowing (NEW class at slice-023 — B2 ACCEPTED-PENDING)**: `tools/plugin_manifest_audit.py:_list_actual_tools` extended to filter `p.name.startswith("_")` in addition to `__init__.py`. Helper modules (e.g., `tools/_stdout.py`) are NOT enumerable as invocable tools, so they MUST NOT appear in `plugin.yaml` `tools:` list; the filter prevents PMI-1 from firing an `orphan-tool` violation on legitimate helpers. PMI-1 v1.1 retirement-proof invariant preserved (gate body unchanged; only discovery filter narrows).

**Behavioural regression (AC #4)**: `tests/methodology/test_utf8_stdout_regression.py` invokes every audit tool via subprocess under `PYTHONIOENCODING=cp1252` + `PYTHONUTF8=0` (Windows-default-encoding simulation) with U+2192 fixture content. Per-tool argv strategy per M1 + M-add-2 ACCEPTED-FIXED reflects each tool's verified argparse contract (positional-slice-folder for 9 tools, `--root` for 2, `--claude-dir` for install_audit, positional-files for mock_budget_lint, `--slice` for validate_slice_layers, positional risk-register.md for risk_register_audit, `--repo-root` for critique audits). Assertion is `"UnicodeEncodeError" not in stderr and "UnicodeDecodeError" not in stderr` — NOT exit-code-0 (synthetic fixtures may legitimately return exit 1 on non-encoding violations).

UTF8-STDOUT-1 self-application N=1 canonical-reference-instance at codification time — `tools/utf8_stdout_audit.py` itself conforms to UTF8-STDOUT-1; the audit run on the post-slice-023 codebase returns `tools_scanned: 17, tools_with_main: 17, tools_clean: 17`.

### Added

- **UTF8-STDOUT-1** (no `-D` suffix — runtime structural discipline, distinct from /critique-time -D family RSAD-1 / EPGD-1 / SCPD-1 / RPCD-1 / TPHD-1 / BFRD-1 + LAYER-EVID-1 / BRANCH-1 precedent)
  3 surfaces (helper + audit + invocation pattern) + PMI-1 `_list_actual_tools` filter narrowing for leading-underscore helpers.
  - **Rule reference**: ADR-021 (reversibility: cheap; supersedes: null)
  - **Defect class**: Windows cp1252 console encoding crashes on audit-tool stdout when interpolated content contains non-cp1252 codepoints (U+2192 arrow at slice-022 D-5 / U+2014 em-dash em-dash is in cp1252 byte 0x97 but arrows + most U+2xxx geometric/box-drawing/CJK are not). N=6 cumulative recurrence across slices 007 / 016 / 018 / 020 / 021 / 022 documented in shippability rows 20/21/22 cumulative-count provenance trail.
  - **Validation**: `tests/methodology/test_stdout_helper.py::*` (5 unit tests) + `tests/methodology/test_utf8_stdout_audit.py::*` (9 audit unit tests including self-audit + JSON shape + exit codes + output-contract invariant) + `tests/methodology/test_utf8_stdout_regression.py::*` (18 subprocess-based behavioural regression tests covering every audit tool under simulated cp1252) + `tests/methodology/test_plugin_manifest_audit.py::test_plugin_yaml_lists_utf8_stdout_audit` + `::test_list_actual_tools_filters_leading_underscore_helpers` (2-assertion: real `_stdout.py` filtered + synthetic `_helper.py` filtered) + `tests/methodology/test_install_audit.py::test_install_audit_enumerates_utf8_stdout_audit` + `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_37_0_utf8_stdout_1_entry_present_in_repo_and_installed` + `::test_v_0_37_0_utf8_stdout_1_entry_names_all_three_surfaces` + `::test_v_0_37_0_utf8_stdout_1_entry_pins_canonical_invocation_pattern` + `::test_adr_021_present_and_reversibility_cheap`.
  - **Limitations**: `sys.stdout` / `sys.stderr` only — NOT `sys.stdin`. `tools/*.py` only — NOT `tests/**/*.py` (pytest capture handles those) and NOT `graphify` (upstream package). Parent-process stdout only — NOT `subprocess.run` parent-side decoding (separate audit class; deferred). `logging` module's default `StreamHandler` routes through `sys.stderr` which IS covered; `FileHandler` with default encoding is NOT covered (separate fix at handler call site). Pre-Python-3.7 `sys.stdout.reconfigure()` not available — N/A on this repo (Python 3.13 shared interpreter per CLAUDE.md global).

## v0.36.0 — 2026-05-15

**3-mode PR-aware /commit-slice taxonomy codified at `skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md` Step 5 sub-steps 5a/5b/5c/5d + new `## When to use which mode` guidance section** — replaces slice-021's single-`--merge` post-/reflect cleanup with three mutually-exclusive mode flags covering solo-dev / PR-based / post-PR-merge cleanup workflows. No-flag invocation preserves slice-021 generate-only default.

Per slice-022 [[ADR-020]], ADR-020 partially supersedes [[ADR-019]] Option 1 **sub-mode (b)** only — the `--merge` flow's BEHAVIOR is preserved verbatim from slice-021 (5-step merge + 2 pre-flight guardrails unchanged); what's superseded is the implicit claim that `--merge` is the only / default post-/reflect cleanup path. ADR-019 sub-modes (a) build-time branch-create + (c) audit-time pre-finish refusal remain UNCHANGED — slice-022 inherits and exercises both.

Three mode flags (mutually exclusive — combining 2+ → STOP with "Mode flags `--merge`, `--push`, `--sync-after-pr` are mutually exclusive; pass exactly one"):
- **`--merge`** — solo-dev / no-protected-branch path (slice-021 5-step merge flow preserved verbatim).
- **`--push`** — PR-based workflow path: WT-clean + stale-slice-branch + origin-present + slice-branch-name pre-flight; `git add` + `git commit` on slice branch; `git push -u origin slice/NNN-<name>` (NEVER `--force`); display PR-creation URL hint (`gh pr create --base <default> --head slice/NNN --web` + raw GitHub.com compare URL). Non-ff push (rebase/amend after prior push) → STOP with `git push --force-with-lease` manual-resolution diagnostic per slice-022 /critique M4 ACCEPTED-FIXED; fast-forward re-push → explicit confirmation prompt. Never merges, never deletes, never touches default branch.
- **`--sync-after-pr`** — post-PR-merge local cleanup path: SKIPS Steps 1-4 (no commit generated). WT-clean + slice-branch + origin-present + upstream-tracking pre-flight; `git fetch --prune origin <default> slice/NNN-<name>` (explicit refspec per slice-022 /critique M1 ACCEPTED-FIXED — Signal B requires fresh local `origin/<default>` view); two-signal merged-state detection (Signal A = `git ls-remote` returns non-zero for remote branch absent; Signal B = two-pass `git cherry` Pass 1 + aggregate-tree-diff Pass 2 fallback per slice-022 /critique B2 ACCEPTED-FIXED — Pass 1 covers plain merge-commit + rebase-merge + cherry-pick + single-commit-squash, Pass 2 covers GitHub squash-merge of N>1-commit slice branches via touched-file-set superset + tree-state intersection equality; Pass 2 has 3 guards per slice-022 /critique-review M-add-5 ACCEPTED-FIXED — empty-FILES STOP, perf bound N=500 commits, "covers FILES" pinned to superset); explicit confirmation prompt → `git checkout <default>` + `git pull --ff-only origin <default>` (explicit `--ff-only` per slice-022 /critique B1 ACCEPTED-FIXED — `git pull`'s default is MERGE not ff-only) + `git branch -d slice/NNN-<name>` (safe-delete only; NEVER `-D`).

The canonical phrase `3-mode PR-aware /commit-slice taxonomy` is pinned across N=3 surfaces: (1) [[ADR-020]] title + body + `skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md` `## When to use which mode` section, (2) in-repo `methodology-changelog.md` v0.36.0 entry (this file), (3) installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` v0.36.0 entry. **N-surface schema-pin shape ratchets to N=9 stable** with slice-022. [[ADR-020]] adds an additional ADR surface per ADR-pin convention N=7 → **N=8 stable**.

**Partial supersession encoding (NEW class at slice-022)**: ADR-020 frontmatter carries `supersedes: ADR-019` (one-directional slot per ADR family convention — verified across ADR-001 through ADR-020). ADR-019 stays unmodified per append-only rule. **SUP-1 does NOT apply** — that rule scopes to `/supersede-slice` for archived-slice reflection.md `**Supersedes**:` / `**Superseded by**:` field pairs, NOT to ADRs (per slice-022 /critique-review M-add-1 ACCEPTED-FIXED). Test `test_adr_020_exists_and_supersedes_adr_019` pins both directions: forward link `supersedes: ADR-019` present in ADR-020 AND reverse field `superseded-by:` ABSENT from ADR-019 (preserves append-only).

**`--push` PR-URL derivation v1 scope** (per slice-022 /critique B3 DEFERRED): GitHub.com origin remote → display both `gh pr create --web` command + raw compare URL; non-GitHub.com remotes (incl. GitHub Enterprise custom-host) → display only `gh pr create --web` + "open via your hosting UI" note. Widening to `*github*` host class deferred to follow-on slice `add-github-enterprise-url-derivation`. `gh pr create --web` works for any `gh`-configured host including Enterprise — Enterprise users get a partial (PR-creation works; raw compare URL missing) but workable experience.

PMI-1 v1.1 versioned-gate retirement-proof N=7 → **N=8 stable** post-slice-022 (eighth atomic version bump 0.35.0 → 0.36.0 with zero modification on `test_plugin_yaml_version_matches_version_file_invariant` body). EPGD-1 self-application **N=8 → N=9 stable** post-slice-022 (0 of 15 prior entry-pin functions touched at function-name level; slice-022 ADDS-only `_v_0_36_0_pr_aware_commit_slice_*` entry-pin functions + 2 ADR-pin functions `test_adr_020_exists_and_supersedes_adr_019` + `test_adr_020_documents_three_mode_taxonomy`). TPHD-1 self-application N=5 → **N=6 partial** post-slice-022 — sub-mode (c) prerequisite-check pre-flight at /build-slice CAUGHT a TF-1 plan staleness drift (function-name + test-path conventions across 4 surfaces) introduced during /critique + /critique-review fix-blocks; harmonization-log line added inline.

BRANCH-1 self-application N=1 bootstrap → **N=2 stable** with slice-022 as the **first non-bootstrap canonical-reference instance** (per slice-022 /critique m2 + /critique-review M-add-2 ACCEPTED-FIXED off-by-one correction "second" → "first"; slice-021 was bootstrap-reference instance #1).

### Added

- **3-mode PR-aware /commit-slice taxonomy** (no new rule ID — slice-022 introduces an additive taxonomy + partial ADR supersession; no new -D-suffix rule codified)
  3 mutually-exclusive mode flags + preserved no-flag default at `skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md` Step 5 sub-steps 5a/5b/5c/5d. `--merge` preserved verbatim from slice-021; `--push` NEW (push to origin + PR URL hint); `--sync-after-pr` NEW (two-signal merged-state detection with two-pass Signal B handling GitHub squash-merge + 3 Pass-2 guards; `git pull --ff-only` explicit; safe-delete only).
  - **Rule reference**: ADR-020 (partially supersedes ADR-019 sub-mode (b))
  - **Defect class**: slice-021 `--merge` was structurally wrong for non-solo / protected-branch / PR-required workflows (all 4 Critic-stack passes at slice-021 missed this — slice-021 DEVIATION-5). Without `--push`, protected-branch users must drop out of the skill to manual `git push`. Without `--sync-after-pr`, post-PR-merge local cleanup is undefined (stale-slice-branch guardrail fires on legitimate post-merge stragglers with no resolution path).
  - **Validation**: `tests/methodology/test_commit_slice_skill_push_flag.py::*` (3 prose-pin) + `tests/methodology/test_commit_slice_skill_sync_after_pr_flag.py::*` (5 prose-pin incl. two-signal + ff-only) + `tests/methodology/test_commit_slice_skill_merge_flag.py::test_commit_slice_skill_md_documents_when_to_use_which_mode_section` + `::test_commit_slice_skill_md_merge_flow_5_steps_unchanged` + `::test_commit_slice_skill_md_documents_no_flag_default_mode` + `::test_commit_slice_skill_md_documents_mutual_exclusion_of_three_mode_flags` + `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_36_0_pr_aware_commit_slice_entry_present_in_repo_and_installed` + `::test_v_0_36_0_entry_names_three_modes_in_repo_and_installed` + `::test_adr_020_exists_and_supersedes_adr_019` + `::test_adr_020_documents_three_mode_taxonomy` + `tests/methodology/test_commit_slice_skill_drift.py::test_commit_slice_skill_md_in_repo_byte_equal_installed` (mini-CAD-1 PASSING post-Phase-3 forward-sync).
  - **Limitations**: GitHub.com URL derivation only in v1 (Enterprise custom-host deferred to follow-on slice `add-github-enterprise-url-derivation`). Auto-PR-creation via `gh pr create` execution is out-of-scope (URL hint only). `--push` requires `origin` remote present (no multi-remote support in v1). `--sync-after-pr` requires upstream tracking (i.e., `--push` was run for this slice branch previously — using `--merge` then trying `--sync-after-pr` STOPs with "Slice branch has no upstream" diagnostic). Pass 2 perf bounded to N=500 commits on `BASE..origin/<default>`; long-lived slice branches on busy default branches STOP with manual-resolution diagnostic. Empty-FILES (net-zero slice tree-diff) STOPs Pass 2 to close data-loss false-YES path.

## v0.35.0 — 2026-05-14

**Branch-per-slice workflow codified at `/build-slice` `## Prerequisite check ### Branch state` sub-section + `/commit-slice --merge` + `tools/branch_workflow_audit.py`** as **BRANCH-1** — every `/build-slice` invocation runs on a `slice/NNN-<slice-name>` branch created from the resolved default branch HEAD; `/commit-slice --merge` no-ff merges the slice branch back into the resolved default branch and safe-deletes the slice branch. The audit at `/build-slice` Step 6 pre-finish refuses any slice that ran on the default branch or on a `slice/<wrong-number>-<wrong-name>` branch, unless `build-log.md` Events documents a canonical `BRANCH=skip — rationale: <text>` escape-hatch.

Per slice-021 [[ADR-019]], BRANCH-1 is audit-enforced (NOT `-D`-suffix runtime-discipline) — branch state is mechanically checkable. Three distinct sub-modes:
- **Sub-mode (a) build-time branch-create**: at `/build-slice` `## Prerequisite check ### Branch state` sub-section — resolve default branch via `git symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` (fallback `git config init.defaultBranch`; STOP if neither resolves per /critique M1 ACCEPTED-PENDING — no hard-coded `master`/`main` for cross-project portability); `git checkout -b slice/NNN-<slice-name>` from default HEAD; switch if branch exists; STOP otherwise; STOP on dirty WT.
- **Sub-mode (b) commit-time `--merge` flow**: at `/commit-slice` Step 5 with `--merge` flag — 2 pre-flight guardrails (refuse if stale `slice/*` branches present per /critique B5 ACCEPTED-PENDING; refuse if `git status --porcelain` non-empty per /critique M5 ACCEPTED-PENDING closing silent-WT-discard path) + `git add` + `git commit` on slice branch + `git checkout <default>` + `git merge --no-ff slice/NNN-<name>` + explicit `Confirm merge + delete? (yes/no)` user-confirmation (per /critique M5 ACCEPTED-PENDING closing unrecoverable-without-push path) + `git branch -d slice/NNN-<name>` (safe-delete only; NEVER `-D`).
- **Sub-mode (c) audit-time pre-finish refusal**: at `/build-slice` Step 6 — `$PY -m tools.branch_workflow_audit <slice-folder>` exits 1 with `on-default-branch` / `slice-branch-mismatch` / `escape-hatch-malformed` / 2 with `default-branch-unresolvable` / warning-class `stale-slice-branch`.

The canonical `BRANCH=skip` line shape pinned at `skills/build-slice/SKILL.md` Step 7c (per slice-021 AC #2 + /critique B1 ACCEPTED-PENDING — narrows the empirically-permissive parent DEVIATION convention for audit-quality): `<YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM> DEVIATION: BRANCH=skip — rationale: <text>` (HH:MM required + `rationale:` token required). Audit's escape-hatch regex: `^- \d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2} \d{2}:\d{2} DEVIATION: BRANCH=skip\b.+rationale: .+`.

Default-branch resolution canonical phrase pinned across N=3 surfaces per Fowler rule-of-three + slice-019 LAYER-EVID-1 N-surface schema-pin precedent: `tools/branch_workflow_audit.py` (Python helper `_resolve_default_branch()`) + `skills/build-slice/SKILL.md` `### Branch state` (prose-heuristic) + `skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md` Step 5 `--merge` flow (prose-heuristic). All 3 name `git symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` AND `git config init.defaultBranch`.

The canonical phrase `branch-per-slice workflow` is pinned across N=3 surfaces: (1) [[ADR-019]] title + body + `skills/build-slice/SKILL.md` + `skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md`, (2) in-repo `methodology-changelog.md` v0.35.0 entry (this file), (3) installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` v0.35.0 entry. **N-surface schema-pin shape ratchets to N=8 stable** with slice-021. [[ADR-019]] adds an additional ADR surface per ADR-pin convention N=6 → **N=7 stable**.

**v1 carveout** (per slice-021 /critique M-add-4 ESCALATED → option (a) chosen): the 3 sub-modes fire only at `/build-slice` + `/commit-slice` + audit. The 4 upstream skills (`/slice`, `/design-slice`, `/critique`, `/critique-review`) have NO branch guard in v1 — pipeline-wide enforcement queued as follow-on slice `add-pipeline-wide-branch-discipline-to-upstream-slice-skills`. Cross-slice-branch leakage is surfaced AT `/build-slice` STOP-and-route, not prevented upstream.

PMI-1 v1.1 versioned-gate retirement-proof N=6 → **N=7 stable** post-slice-021 (seventh atomic version bump 0.34.0 → 0.35.0 with zero modification on `test_plugin_yaml_version_matches_version_file_invariant` body). EPGD-1 self-application **N=7 → N=8 stable** post-slice-021 (0 of 15 prior entry-pin functions touched at function-name level; slice-021 ADDS-only `_v_0_35_0_branch_1_*` entry-pin functions + 1 ADR-pin `test_adr_019_branch_per_slice_workflow_exists_and_links_to_branch_1`).

BRANCH-1 is applied prospectively from the next non-/repro slice onward. Slice-021 IS the canonical **bootstrap-reference instance #1**: the slice that authors the `## Prerequisite check ### Branch state` sub-section cannot itself use the sub-section at /build-slice time (the sub-section is being added in /build-slice's own task plan). Manual branch-create at /build-slice Step 1 plan-mode entry + canonical `BRANCH=skip-bootstrap` DEVIATION line in build-log.md Events documents the bootstrap. RSAD-1 self-application at slice-021 is a **bootstrap reference instance**, distinct class from slice-015/017/019/020 prior design-time / fix-prose-time canonical-reference-instance precedents.

### Added

- **BRANCH-1 — Branch-per-slice workflow at /build-slice + /commit-slice + audit**
  3 sub-modes: (a) build-time branch-create at `/build-slice` `## Prerequisite check ### Branch state` sub-section; (b) commit-time `--merge` flow at `/commit-slice` Step 5 with 2 pre-flight guardrails + 5-step merge flow; (c) audit-time pre-finish refusal via `tools/branch_workflow_audit.py` at `/build-slice` Step 6. Canonical phrase `branch-per-slice workflow` pinned across N=3 surfaces. Runtime default-branch resolution via `git symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD` + `git config init.defaultBranch` fallback. Canonical `BRANCH=skip — rationale: <text>` escape-hatch line shape pinned at `skills/build-slice/SKILL.md` Step 7c. v1 carveout: `/build-slice` + `/commit-slice` only; upstream-pipeline skills have no branch guard in v1.
  - **Rule reference**: BRANCH-1
  - **Defect class**: slice work shipped directly to default branch → no rollback boundary per slice + no discrete merge-commit attribution + no per-slice diff surface for review
  - **Validation**: `tests/methodology/test_branch_workflow_audit.py::*` (8 unit tests) + `tests/methodology/test_build_slice_skill_branch_create.py::*` (4 prose-pin) + `tests/methodology/test_build_slice_skill_step_7c_branch_skip_canonical_shape.py::*` (1) + `tests/methodology/test_commit_slice_skill_merge_flag.py::*` (4) + `tests/methodology/test_root_claude_md_branch_per_slice_rule.py::*` (1) + `tests/methodology/test_build_slice_skill_drift.py::*` + `tests/methodology/test_commit_slice_skill_drift.py::*` (mini-CAD-1 N=11 → N=13 stable with 2 NEW byte-equality tests) + `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_35_0_branch_1_entry_*` (3 entry-pin) + `::test_adr_019_branch_per_slice_workflow_exists_and_links_to_branch_1` (ADR-pin) + `tests/methodology/test_install_audit.py::test_install_audit_enumerates_branch_workflow_audit` + `tests/methodology/test_plugin_manifest_audit.py::test_plugin_yaml_lists_branch_workflow_audit` + `tests/methodology/test_critique_agent_drift.py::test_critique_agent_drift_audit_clean_at_slice_021_ship`.
  - **Limitations**: local-only (no `git push`, no remote-delete). Single-merge-strategy (`--no-ff` only; squash + rebase deferred). Branch-prefix uniformity (`slice/NNN-<name>` for all). No auto-stash. Safe-delete only. No history rewrite. Detection = branch-name only. v1 carveout: upstream skills have no branch guard. Merge-conflict-recovery deferred.

## v0.34.0 — 2026-05-14

**Bug-fix repro prelude discipline codified at `/slice` Step 3c** as **BFRD-1** — when the chosen slice candidate is a bug fix (mode (a) name-shape fast-path covering `fix-*` prefix + `*-fix` suffix + `bugfix-*` + `hotfix-*` + `defect-*` + `repair-*` + `patch-*` + `harden-*-bug` regex variants, OR mode (b) PRIMARY candidate-source signal from risk-register bug-class / prior-reflection bug-observed note / user description identifying defect / `/repro` invocation context), `/slice` MUST verify a failing repro test exists in `architecture/shippability.md` BEFORE writing the mission brief. If no such test exists → STOP and instruct the user to run `/repro <issue>` first; on re-invocation post-`/repro`, cite the failing-test path under `Dependencies` and assert one AC: "the failing repro test PASSES at slice end". The rule operationalizes CLAUDE.md's existing "Tests-first for bug fixes" cultural rule at `/slice` skill-runtime, closing the exercise-on-the-honor-system gap.

Empirical evidence base (false-negative anchor at slice-001): the project's own `architecture/slices/archive/slice-001-diagnose-orchestration-fix/` is named with `-fix` suffix (NOT prefix), with mission-brief.md L7 stating "regression discovered in the 2026-05-09 session" — confirming it IS a bug-fix slice per slice-020 /critique B1 ACCEPTED-FIXED empirical catch. The widened mode (a) regex set covers this in-project counter-example PLUS canonical bug-fix naming variants from adjacent projects.

Per slice-020 [[ADR-018]], the rule-ID is **BFRD-1 "Bug-Fix-Repro-Discipline"** with **-D suffix** at end of abbreviation, extending the slice-011 RSAD-1 + slice-013 EPGD-1 + slice-015 SCPD-1 + slice-016 RPCD-1 + slice-017 TPHD-1 -D-suffix calibration-trail convention to **N=6 stable** — the -D signals prose-heuristic / discipline semantics applied at /slice-time, distinct from audit-enforced gate semantics of sibling rules (BC-1 / PMI-1 / CAD-1 / TF-1 / RR-1 / INST-1 / VAL-1 / WIRE-1 / NFR-1 / CSP-1, each with a corresponding `tools/*_audit.py` enforcing the rule programmatically). BFRD-1 lives in `skills/slice/SKILL.md` Step 3c body prose only. v2 candidate `tools/bfrd_1_audit.py` (programmatic mission-brief scan for bug-fix name shape + Dependencies failing-test reference) deferred until N≥3 BFRD-1 violations recur post-codification at slice-021+.

The two detection modes covering the empirical evidence base:
- **Sub-mode (a) name-shape fast-path**: candidate name matches one of `fix-*` (prefix), `*-fix` (suffix — witnessed in-project at slice-001-diagnose-orchestration-fix per slice-020 /critique B1 ACCEPTED-FIXED), `bugfix-*`, `hotfix-*`, `defect-*`, `repair-*`, `patch-*`, `harden-*-bug` (regex set covering canonical bug-fix naming variants). **Necessary-but-not-sufficient** — fast-path fallback only; primary signal is mode (b). Concrete miss class without widened set: slice-001's `-fix` suffix shape would escape naïve `fix-*`-prefix-only detection.
- **Sub-mode (b) candidate-source signal (PRIMARY)**: candidate sourced from risk-register bug-class entry, prior reflection's "bug observed" note, user description explicitly identifying a defect, or `/repro` invocation context. When mode (a) misses (e.g., a `harden-X` security-hardening slice that fixes a latent defect), mode (b) catches.

Per slice-020 [[ADR-018]] Option 1, the verification mechanism (`shippability.md grep verification` canonical phrase pinned per /critique B2 ACCEPTED-FIXED + /critique-review M-add-2 ACCEPTED-FIXED Option (a)): when either detection mode fires, Claude greps `architecture/shippability.md` for a row whose `Command` cell targets `tests/bugs/*` (the documented `/repro` skill convention at `skills/repro/SKILL.md` L87/L94/L114). If no such row exists → ask the user to paste the failing-test path and confirm it was just-added by `/repro` (verbal-claim-with-path is the documented fallback covering `/repro`'s "or project's convention for bug-fix tests" caveat at L87). If the user can't produce a path → STOP-and-route to `/repro`. The prior `bug:` provenance branch was DROPPED at /critique-review M-add-2 ACCEPTED-FIXED Option (a) — shippability rows 1-19 contain zero `bug:` provenance comments AND no /repro skill update in slice-020 mandates `bug:` going forward; the branch was aspirational and would have made the verification mechanism depend on non-existent state (RPCD-1 sub-mode (b) class self-application N=2 stable).

The canonical phrase `bug-fix repro prelude discipline` is pinned across N=3 surfaces per slice-013 EPGD-1 + slice-014 PMI-1 v1.1 + slice-015 SCPD-1 + slice-016 RPCD-1 + slice-017 TPHD-1 + slice-019 LAYER-EVID-1 N-surface schema-pin precedent: (1) [[ADR-018]] title + body + `skills/slice/SKILL.md` Step 3c section opener, (2) in-repo `methodology-changelog.md` v0.34.0 entry (this file), (3) installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` v0.34.0 entry. **N-surface schema-pin 3-surface shape ratchets to N=7 instances stable** post-slice-020 (RSAD-1 v0.26.0 + EPGD-1 v0.28.0 + PMI-1-v1.1 v0.29.0 + SCPD-1 v0.30.0 + RPCD-1 v0.31.0 + TPHD-1 v0.32.0 + LAYER-EVID-1 v0.33.0 + BFRD-1 v0.34.0 = 8 instances; minus LAYER-EVID-1 which is not -D-suffix runtime-discipline = 7 stable -D-suffix instances). [[ADR-018]] adds an additional ADR surface per slice-014 ADR-013 + slice-017 ADR-016 + slice-019 ADR-017 ADR-pin convention N=5 → **N=6 stable** with slice-020.

Codification at **N=1 cumulative evidence** (proactive ratchet ahead of N=2 promotion threshold, aligned with slice-017 TPHD-1 + slice-019 LAYER-EVID-1 pattern of codifying high-impact discipline-completeness classes at N=1 per user-invoked /slice argument). The rule's value proposition: bug-fix slices shipped without a failing repro test ship either (a) a passing-test-on-buggy-code mirage or (b) no regression guard at all, allowing silent recurrence. CLAUDE.md's "Tests-first for bug fixes" cultural rule has existed since brownfield adoption but lived only at the project-root readme layer, never structurally enforced at `/slice` skill runtime. BFRD-1 operationalizes the existing implicit discipline at the skill-runtime surface where it's actually enforceable.

When the discipline applies, Claude main thread at `/slice` Step 3c performs the bug-detection check (mode (a) name-shape regex + mode (b) source-signal) AND the verification mechanism (`shippability.md grep verification` for `tests/bugs/*` Command-cell match + verbal-claim-with-path fallback). BFRD-1 is a `/slice`-runtime discipline applied at the single canonical entry point for slice definition, NOT at `/critique` or `/build-slice` (timing/sequencing discipline belongs in the skill that owns the inflection point per slice-017 ADR-016 reasoning).

The new v0.34.0 entry does NOT supersede any prior PMI-1 versioned gate (PMI-1 v1.1 version-agnostic gate from slice-014 retirement-proof atomic-bump count N=5 → **N=6 stable** post-slice-020 — sixth atomic version bump 0.33.0 → 0.34.0 with zero modification on `test_plugin_yaml_version_matches_version_file_invariant` body). The 15 prior entry-pin functions (v0.22.0..v0.33.0 with v0.29.0 doubled per slice-014 (a)↔(b) duality + v0.31.0 doubled per slice-016 RPCD-1 (a)↔(b) duality = 13 singles + 2 doublets = 15 functions) persist untouched through slice-020's NEW SECTION header insertion + 4 new entry-pin/ADR-pin functions. EPGD-1 self-application **N=6 → N=7 stable** post-slice-020 (0 of 15 prior entry-pin functions touched at function-name level — body-only refactor at L12-74 generalizing `_extract_v031_body` + `_extract_v033_body` to thin wrappers around new `_extract_version_body(content, version)` helper per slice-020 /critique M5 ACCEPTED-FIXED rule-of-three promotion N=2 → N=3 stable).

BFRD-1 is applied prospectively — slice-021 onward (the FIRST slice candidate to exercise the rule). Slice-020 is the codification reference but does NOT self-apply at codification time per /critique M1 ACCEPTED-FIXED + design.md Audit 7: contingent N/A (slice-020 happens to be a NEW-feature methodology-codification slice that does not fix a runtime bug; STOP-route vacuously satisfied; hypothetical future codification-AND-bug-fix slice would self-apply BFRD-1 trivially via mode (a) or mode (b) detection — contingent inapplicability, not architectural impossibility per slice-019 DR-1 catch-class *Self-application-qualifier coherence on canonical-reference-instance naming* N=1 watch-list ratchet to N=2 stable at slice-020).

## v0.33.0 — 2026-05-13

**Textual import-evidence requirement codified at `/diagnose` 03f-layering pass-template level** as **LAYER-EVID-1** — the 03f-layering subagent MUST grep-verify a textual import statement in the cited evidence file before emitting any HIGH-severity layering-violation finding. The rule operationalizes `/diagnose` Hard Rule #5 ("Never invent findings") for the boundary-violation category and closes a witnessed false-positive class.

Witnessed failure mode (slice-019, finding ID F-LAYER-bca9c001): graphify symbol-resolution collapsed cross-file same-name symbols (parallel type files defining identically-named enums — backend `src/workflow/adapters/types.ts` + frontend `frontend/lib/workflow/types.ts`, both defining `enum NodeType / TaskType / AssigneeType`) into phantom edges, causing the 03f-layering pass to flag a HIGH "45 frontend files bypass HTTP boundary" finding when zero frontend files textually import from `src/` (the frontend `tsconfig.json`'s `@/*` alias maps to `./*` rooted at `frontend/`, physically preventing alias-based access to `src/`).

Per slice-019 [[ADR-017]], the rule-ID is **LAYER-EVID-1 "Layering-Evidence-1"** without the -D suffix (calibration-trail convention) — LAYER-EVID-1 is a `/diagnose`-runtime discipline applied INSIDE the subagent's reasoning loop, NOT a /critique-time / /critique-review-time / /build-slice-time discipline like the -D-suffix family (RSAD-1 / EPGD-1 / SCPD-1 / RPCD-1 / TPHD-1). N-surface schema-pin shape: N=3 surfaces (`skills/diagnose/passes/03f-layering.md` Method step 4 + Severity rubric + Anti-patterns; `skills/diagnose/SKILL.md` Step 5 cross-reference; `methodology-changelog.md` v0.33.0 entry — this file) with bidirectional in-repo ↔ installed sha256 byte-equal (N=6 total surfaces) per slice-016 RPCD-1 + slice-017 TPHD-1 3-surface precedent (N=6 stable -> N=7 with slice-019). [[ADR-017]] adds an additional N=7 surface (the ADR file itself per slice-014 ADR-013 + slice-017 ADR-016 ADR-pin convention N=4 stable -> N=5 with slice-019).

Codification at **N=1 cumulative evidence** (proactive ratchet ahead of N=2 promotion threshold, aligned with slice-017 TPHD-1 pattern of codifying high-impact false-positive classes at N=1 per user-invoked /slice argument). The rule's value proposition: HIGH-severity false-positives in `/diagnose` output erode user trust in the entire forensic pass and pollute `/slice-candidates` backlog generation; closing the class at the witnessed pass is cheap, reversible, and witness-driven (Principle 2 risk-first ordering).

The rule lives at the pass-template prose layer (NOT the graphify code layer) per [[ADR-017]] Option B vs Option A analysis. Trade-off: narrow blast radius + cheap reversibility + empirical-evidence-basis at the cost of NOT fixing other graphify consumers. [[R-3]] explicit escalation criteria — if `/critic-calibrate` flags additional symbol-conflation false-positives in other `/diagnose` passes (`02-architecture`, `03d-half-wired`, `03e-contradictions`) OR in other graphify consumers (`/architect`, `/validate`, `/sprint-runner`, etc.) at N≥2 distinct slices, promote a follow-on slice to either (a) extend LAYER-EVID-1 to those passes or (b) fix graphify symbol-resolution upstream.

Step 5 dispatch enumeration (per AC #1 + must-not-defer #1): of the 11 `/diagnose` passes, only **03f-layering** emits `category: layering-violation` per `skills/diagnose/schema/finding.yaml:21-32` enum (ground-truth verified 2026-05-13: `grep -h '^- \`category\`:' skills/diagnose/passes/*.md` returns exactly one match at `passes/03f-layering.md:47`). The other 10 passes are OUT of LAYER-EVID-1 scope with rationale grounded in finding-category emission shape (02-architecture emits `findings: []` per Block contents; 03b-duplicates flags shape-equivalence not import-bypass; 03d-half-wired flags UI↔backend endpoint-existence not import-edges; 03e-contradictions flags cross-module assumption-divergence not import-violation; etc.) — see slice-019 `architecture/slices/slice-019-harden-diagnose-layering-evidence/design.md` "Step 5 dispatch enumeration" subsection for per-pass per-rationale table.

### Added

  Pass-template prose at `skills/diagnose/passes/03f-layering.md` Method step 4 + Severity rubric downgrade rule + Anti-patterns negative-pin (literal regex strings byte-equal to test-local helper `_grep_textual_import` per slice-019 /critique M1 visual byte-equality mitigation); cross-referenced at `skills/diagnose/SKILL.md` Step 5 with one-line paragraph.   - **Validation**: `tests/skills/diagnose/test_skill_md_pins.py::test_skill_md_step5_documents_textual_evidence_rule` + `::test_layering_pass_template_emits_textual_evidence_rule` + `::test_textual_evidence_rule_byte_equal_across_n_3_surfaces`; `tests/skills/diagnose/test_diagnose_skill_drift.py::test_in_repo_and_installed_diagnose_skill_md_are_content_equal` + `::test_in_repo_and_installed_diagnose_03f_layering_md_are_content_equal` (mini-CAD bidirectional byte-equality, 2 single-file tests per slice-007 CAD-1 convention); `tests/skills/diagnose/test_layering_pass_textual_evidence.py::test_synthetic_parallel_types_no_import_yields_zero_high_layering_findings` + `::test_synthetic_real_cross_tier_import_still_fires_high_layering_finding` (synthetic fixture integration covering 5 TS variants + 3 re-export forms + multi-line via re.DOTALL + alias-aware tsconfig resolution); `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py::test_v_0_33_0_layer_evid_1_*` (entry pins + sibling-scoping regression via `_extract_v033_body` helper per slice-018 sibling-scoping discipline N=2 cumulative); `tests/methodology/test_risk_register_audit_real_file.py::test_r_3_added_post_slice_019_with_graphify_symbol_conflation_class` (R-3 RR-1 conformance).

TPHD-1's value proposition is **prophylactic against an audit gap** (function-name-staleness mode) + **defense-in-depth for status-staleness mode**. ## v0.31.0 — 2026-05-13

**Runtime-prerequisite-completeness discipline codified into `agents/critique.md` Dim 9 9th sub-clause**, codified as **RPCD-1** — when the Critic proposes a fix (whether at /critique or /critique-review), the fix may introduce a runtime prerequisite that surrounding tooling/context doesn't satisfy. The first Critic correctly identifies the design-semantic defect AND a structurally sound fix; the runtime-prerequisite gap is what the DR-1 meta-Critic has historically caught. To close that loop at first-Critic time, when proposing any fix involving (a) a NEW symbol used in a Python test body — verify the module imports that symbol; (b) a NEW status string / enum value / token introduced by the fix — verify the relevant audit/parser/allowlist accepts it (e.g., `tools/test_first_audit.py` `_ALLOWED_STATUSES`); (c) a NEW end_anchor / start_anchor / body-bound for a test — grep the test file for sibling tests sharing the same anchor pair and propose tightening them in lockstep. Codification at N=3 distinct-slice promotion threshold per /critic-calibrate 2026-05-13 (post-slice-015) ACCEPTED proposal — three consecutive first-Critic-MISS / meta-Critic-CATCH events: **slice-013 M-add-1** (sibling-test grep absent at first-Critic; meta-Critic caught `_cites_at_least_two_cross_slice_anchors` sharing same anchors → needed lockstep tighten) + **slice-014 M-add-1** (missing `import pytest` + `import ast` at canonical regression-test body; would have NameError'd all 3 rows at smoke gate) + **slice-015 M-add-1** (`WRITTEN-AS-EDIT` status introduced by first Critic's M1 disposition not in `tools/test_first_audit.py`'s `_ALLOWED_STATUSES`; would have caused /build-slice Phase 6 strict-pre-finish FAILURE with 11 violations). The canonical phrase `Runtime-prerequisite completeness on proposed fixes` is pinned across N=3 surfaces per the slice-013 EPGD-1 + slice-014 PMI-1 v1.1 + slice-015 SCPD-1 N-surface schema-pin precedent: (1) [[agents/critique.md]] Dim 9 9th sub-clause title + (2) in-repo `methodology-changelog.md` v0.31.0 entry + (3) installed `~/.claude/methodology-changelog.md` v0.31.0 entry. **N-surface schema-pin 3-surface shape ratchets to N=5 instances stable** post-slice-016 (RSAD-1 v0.26.0 + EPGD-1 v0.28.0 + PMI-1-v1.1 v0.29.0 + SCPD-1 v0.30.0 + RPCD-1 v0.31.0).

Per slice-016 [[ADR-015]], the rule-ID is **RPCD-1 "Runtime-Prerequisite Completeness Discipline"** with **-D suffix** at end of abbreviation, extending the slice-011 RSAD-1 + slice-013 EPGD-1 + slice-015 SCPD-1 -D-suffix calibration-trail convention to **N=4 stable** — the -D signals prose-heuristic / discipline semantics applied at /critique-time + /critique-review-time, distinct from the audit-enforced gate semantics of sibling rules (BC-1 / PMI-1 / CAD-1 / TF-1 / RR-1 / INST-1 / VAL-1 / WIRE-1 / NFR-1 / CSP-1, each of which has a corresponding `tools/*_audit.py` enforcing the rule programmatically).
